/**\n
 * The ticket workspace (/it/tickets/{ticket}): the conversation thread,\n
 * activity timeline and properties rail where tickets actually get worked.\n
 * Requesters reach their OWN tickets only, with internal notes stripped\n
 * server-side — the payload is the privacy boundary, never the UI.\n
 */\n
class ItTicketController extends Controller\n
{\n
    use BuildsItOptions, ServesPrivateAttachments;\n

        private readonly ItSlaReadService $slaRead,\n
    ) {}\n
\n
    public function show(Request $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        // Preserve the drawer's actor/record-bound JSON contract. Browser links\n
        // follow the authorized survivor; historical commands never redirect.\n
        if ($ticket->isMerged() && ($request->header('X-Inertia') || ! $request->wantsJson())) {\n
            $destination = $this->mergeService->destinationForViewer($ticket, $request->user());\n
            if ($destination) {\n
                $tab = $request->query('tab');\n
\n
                return redirect()->route('it.tickets.show', [\n
                    'ticket' => $destination->id,\n
                    'merged_from' => $ticket->id,\n
                    ...(in_array($tab, ['messages', 'files', 'tasks', 'approvals', 'properties', 'links', 'sla', 'history'], true)\n
                        ? ['tab' => $tab] : []),\n
                ])->header('Cache-Control', 'no-store, private');\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        return $this->renderWorkspace($request, $ticket);\n
    }\n
\n
    public function original(Request $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        abort_unless($this->workAccess->canView($request->user(), $ticket), 404);\n
        if (! $ticket->isMerged()) {\n
            return redirect()->route('it.tickets.show', $ticket)->header('Cache-Control', 'no-store, private');\n
        }\n
\n
        return $this->renderWorkspace($request, $ticket);\n
    }\n
\n
    private function renderWorkspace(Request $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $payload = $this->showPayload($request, $ticket);\n
\n
        // One route, two callers: the detail page (Inertia) and the\n
        // quick-peek drawer (axios). Policy + internal-note stripping run\n
        // identically for both — the payload IS the privacy boundary.\n
        if (! $request->header('X-Inertia') && $request->wantsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json($payload)->header('Cache-Control', 'no-store, private');\n
        }\n
\n
        return Inertia::render('it/tickets/show', $payload)->toResponse($request)\n
            ->header('Cache-Control', 'no-store, private');\n
    }\n
\n
    public function linkDevice(LinkTicketDeviceRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        try {\n
            $changed = $this->deviceContext->add(\n
                $ticket,\n
                (int) $request->validated('device_id'),\n
                $request->user(),\n
            );\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with(\n
            'success',\n
            $changed ? 'Device linked to ticket.' : 'Device is already linked to this ticket.',\n
        );\n
    }\n
\n
    public function unlinkDevice(Request $request, ItTicket $ticket, Device $device)\n
    {\n
        abort_unless($this->workAccess->canWork($request->user(), $ticket), 404);\n
        $this->authorize('update', $ticket);\n
\n
        try {\n
            $changed = $this->deviceContext->remove($ticket, $device, $request->user());\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with(\n
            'success',\n
            $changed ? 'Device link removed.' : 'Device was already unlinked.',\n
        );\n
    }\n

                    ? [\n
                        'score' => (int) $ticket->csat_score,\n
                        'comment' => $ticket->csat_comment,\n
                        'submitted_at' => $ticket->csat_submitted_at->toIso8601String(),\n
                    ]\n
                    : null,\n
                'created_at' => $ticket->created_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                'created_human' => $ticket->created_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
                'updated_at' => $ticket->updated_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                'resolved_at' => $ticket->resolved_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                'monitoring_recovered_at' => $ticket->monitoring_recovered_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                'closed_at' => $ticket->closed_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                // §P-S2: the survivor this ticket was folded into, for the banner.\n
                'is_merged' => $ticket->isMerged(),\n
                'merge_origin' => $mergeOrigin,\n
                'merged_originals' => $this->mergeService->originalRecords($ticket, $user,\n
                    max(1, (int) $request->query('originals_page', 1))),\n
                'merged_into' => $mergeDestination\n
                    ? [\n
                        'id' => $mergeDestination->id,\n
                        'reference' => $mergeDestination->reference,\n
                        'title' => $mergeDestination->title,\n
                    ]\n
                    : null,\n
                // §P-S3 approval — flag + the latest request, for the rail.\n
                'requires_approval' => (bool) $ticket->requires_approval,\n
                'approval' => $latestApproval ? [\n
                    'id' => $latestApproval->id,\n
                    'status' => $latestApproval->effectiveStatus(),\n
                    'requested_by_name' => $latestApproval->requester?->name,\n
                    'approver_name' => $latestApproval->approver?->name,\n
                    'reason' => $canManage ? $latestApproval->reason : null,\n
                    'requested_at' => $latestApproval->created_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                    'decided_at' => $latestApproval->decided_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                ] : null,\n
            ],\n
            'comments' => $comments,\n
            'draftRecovery' => $this->draftRecoveryOptions(),\n
            'events' => $this->activityPresenter->present($ticket, $user),\n
            'linked_context' => $this->contextPresenter->present($ticket, $user),\n
            'assignees' => $canManage ? $this->staffUserOptions($user, $ticket) : [],\n
            'approvals' => $canManage ? $ticket->approvals()->orderByDesc('id')->get(['id', 'status'])\n
                ->map(fn ($approval): array => ['id' => (int) $approval->id, 'status' => $approval->status])->all() : [],\n
            'task_work' => $canManage ? ['storage_ready' => $taskStorageReady,\n
                'can_create' => $canChangeTasks, 'can_reorder' => $canChangeTasks] : null,\n
            'approval_work' => app(ItTicketApprovalPresenter::class)->work($ticket, $user),\n
            // Rail picker over the canonical (fleet-)assets register — never\n
            // a parallel IT register. Agents only.\n
            'assetOptions' => $canManage ? $this->assetOptions($user, $ticket) : [],\n
            'deviceOptions' => $canLinkDevices\n
                ? $this->linkedContextOptions->devices($user, $ticket)\n
                : [],\n
            'siteOptions' => $canManage\n
                ? collect($this->linkedContextOptions->sites($user))\n
                    ->when(\n
                        $ticket->site,\n
                        fn ($options) => $options->contains('id', $ticket->site->id)\n
                            ? $options\n
                            : $options->push(['id' => $ticket->site->id, 'name' => $ticket->site->name]),\n
                    )\n
                    ->sortBy('name')\n
                    ->values()\n
                    ->all()\n
                : [],\n
            'serviceOptions' => $canManage ? $this->linkedContextOptions->services() : [],\n
            'teamOptions' => $canManage ? $this->linkedContextOptions->teams() : [],\n
            'queueOptions' => $canManage\n
                ? app(ItTicketRoutingService::class)->queueOptions($ticket)\n
                : [],\n
            // §I composer deflection: published articles an agent can reference\n
            // as they type a reply. Only technicians who can work this ticket\n
            // receive the internal composer context.\n
            'kbSuggestions' => $isAgent ? $this->kbSuggestions($user) : [],\n
            // Suggestions remain scoped and explainable; they never authorize a merge.\n
            'mergeTargets' => $canManage\n
                ? $this->mergeService->candidates($ticket, $user)\n
                : [],\n
            'watcherOptions' => $canManage && ! $ticket->isMerged()\n
                ? ItStaffDirectory::watchersForTicket($ticket)->map(fn (User $watcher): array => [\n
                    'id' => (int) $watcher->id, 'name' => $watcher->name,\n
                ])->all()\n
                : [],\n
            'can' => [\n
                'manage' => $canManage && ! $ticket->isMerged(),\n
                'manageWatchers' => $canManage && ! $ticket->isMerged(),\n
                'linkDevices' => $canLinkDevices,\n
                'assignApplicationWide' => $canManage\n
                    && $this->workAccess->canAssignScope($user, null, true),\n
                'view' => $isAgent,\n
                'internal' => $canManage,\n
                'comment' => $canComment,\n
                'reopen' => $user->can('reopen', $ticket),\n
                'confirmResolution' => $user->can('confirmResolution', $ticket),\n
                'watching' => $ticket->watchers->contains('id', $user->id),\n
                // The requester may rate their own resolved ticket (§K).\n
                'rate' => $isRequester && ! $ticket->isMerged() && $ticket->status === 'resolved',\n
                // Fold a duplicate into another live ticket (§P-S2). Agents only.\n
                'merge' => $canManage && ! $ticket->isMerged() && $ticket->status !== 'closed',\n
                // Approval affordances (§P-S3).\n
                'requestApproval' => (bool) $user->can('requestApproval', $ticket),\n
                'decideApproval' => ! $ticket->isMerged() && $pendingApproval !== null && (bool) $user->can('decide', $pendingApproval),\n
            ],\n
            'replyUnavailableReason' => $replyUnavailableReason,\n
        ];\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * Resolve only current staff profiles that the viewer can actually open.\n
     * The ticket remains the source of names; this map grants no extra HR data.\n
     *\n
     * @param  array[39m[31m<int, mixed>[39m[31m  $userIds\n
     * @return array[39m[31m<int, string>[39m[31m\n
     */\n
    private function staffProfileHrefs(User $viewer, array $userIds): array\n
    {\n
        if (! $viewer->canDo('hr.employees.viewAny')) {\n
            return [];\n
        }\n
\n
        $userIds = collect($userIds)\n
            ->filter(fn (mixed $id): bool => is_numeric($id) && (int) $id > 0)\n
            ->map(fn (mixed $id): int => (int) $id)\n
            ->unique()\n
            ->values()\n
            ->all();\n
        if ($userIds === []) {\n
            return [];\n
        }\n
\n
        $visibleUsers = User::query()->whereKey($userIds)->select('users.id');\n
        app(UserSiteAccessService::class)->applyStaffScope($visibleUsers, $viewer);\n
\n
        return HrEmployeeProfile::query()\n
            ->whereIn('user_id', $visibleUsers)\n
            ->pluck('id', 'user_id')\n
            ->mapWithKeys(fn (mixed $profileId, mixed $userId): array => [\n
                (int) $userId => "/hr/people/{$profileId}",\n
            ])\n
            ->all();\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @return array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m|null */\n
    private function waitingPayload(ItTicket $ticket, bool $canManage): ?array\n
    {\n
        if ($ticket->status !== 'waiting') {\n
            return null;\n
        }\n
\n
        $payload = [\n
            'party' => $canManage\n
                ? ($ticket->waiting_party ?: 'other')\n
                : ($ticket->waiting_party === 'requester' ? 'requester' : 'other'),\n
            'since' => $ticket->waiting_since?->toIso8601String(),\n
            'since_human' => $ticket->waiting_since?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
        ];\n
\n
        if ($canManage) {\n
            $payload['reason'] = $ticket->waiting_reason;\n
            $payload['next_action'] = $ticket->next_action;\n
        }\n
\n
        return $payload;\n
    }\n
\n
    public function storeComment(StoreTicketCommentRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($this->workAccess->canView($user, $ticket), 404);\n
        abort_unless($user->can('comment', $ticket), 403);\n
        if ($request->boolean('is_internal')) {\n
            abort_unless($this->workAccess->canWork($user, $ticket), 403);\n
        }\n
        $isInternal = $request->boolean('is_internal');\n
        if ($request->has('request_uuid')) {\n
            try {\n
                $result = $this->interactionService->addCommentCommand($ticket, $user, $request->validated(), $request->file('attachments', []));\n
            } catch (ItTicketCommandConflict $exception) {\n
                return response()->json(['code' => 'idempotency_conflict', 'message' => $exception->getMessage()], 409);\n
            } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
                return response()->json(['code' => 'comment_rejected', 'message' => $exception->getMessage(), 'errors' => ['body' => [$exception->getMessage()]]], 422);\n
            }\n
            if ($result instanceof ItTicketCommentCancellationResult) {\n
                return response()->json(['status' => 'cancelled', 'data' => $result->toArray((int) $user->id)], 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
            }\n
            try {\n
                DispatchItTicketNotifications::dispatchAfterResponse((int) $result->ticket->id);\n
            } catch (\Throwable) {\n
                // The committed outbox remains available to the scheduled\n
                // drain. Scheduling failure cannot undo the saved reply ACK.\n
            }\n
\n
            return response()->json(['status' => 'committed', 'data' => $result->toArray((int) $user->id)], $result->replayed ? 200 : 201, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
        }\n
        try {\n
            $result = $this->interactionService->addComment(\n
                $ticket,\n
                $user,\n
                (string) $request->validated('body'),\n
                $isInternal,\n
                array_values(array_filter((array) $request->file('attachments'))),\n
            );\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            return redirect()->back()->withErrors(['body' => $exception->getMessage()]);\n
        }\n
        $ticket = $result['ticket'];\n
        $comment = $result['comment'];\n
        $isRequester = $result['is_requester'];\n
\n
        // Public replies notify the other side of the conversation; internal\n
        // notes notify nobody (they do not exist for requesters).\n
        if (! $isInternal) {\n
            if ($isRequester) {\n
                $recipients = $ticket->watchers\n
                    ->when($ticket->assignee, fn ($c) => $c->push($ticket->assignee))\n
                    ->unique('id')\n
                    ->reject(fn ($u) => $u->id === $user->id);\n
                $this->emailDeliveries->send(\n
                    $recipients,\n
                    new TicketRepliedNotification($ticket, 'agent_side', $comment->id),\n
                );\n
            } elseif ($ticket->requester && $ticket->requester_user_id !== $user->id) {\n
                $this->emailDeliveries->send(\n
                    $ticket->requester,\n
                    new TicketRepliedNotification($ticket, 'requester', $comment->id),\n
                );\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', $isInternal ? 'Internal note added.' : 'Reply added.');\n
    }\n
\n
    public function recoverCommentCommand(RecoverItTicketCommentCommandRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket, string $requestUuid)\n
    {\n
        $result = $this->interactionService->recoverCommentCommand($ticket, $request->user(), $requestUuid);\n
\n
        return response()->json([\n
            'status' => $result instanceof ItTicketCommentCancellationResult ? 'cancelled' : 'committed',\n
            'data' => $result->toArray((int) $request->user()->id),\n
        ], 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
    }\n
\n
    public function cancelCommentCommand(CancelItTicketCommentCommandRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket, string $requestUuid)\n
    {\n
        try {\n
            $result = $this->interactionService->cancelCommentCommand($ticket, $request->user(), $requestUuid, $request->boolean('is_internal'));\n
        } catch (ItTicketCommandConflict $exception) {\n
            return response()->json(['code' => 'idempotency_conflict', 'message' => $exception->getMessage()], 409);\n
        }\n
\n
        return response()->json([\n
            'status' => $result instanceof ItTicketCommentCancellationResult ? 'cancelled' : 'committed',\n
            'data' => $result->toArray((int) $request->user()->id),\n
        ], 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * Authorised download for thread evidence. The parent write's audience\n
     * is the read audience: ticket files follow the ticket policy; comment\n
     * files additionally hide with their internal note.\n
     */\n
    public function downloadAttachment(Request $request, ItAttachment $attachment)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        $parent = $attachment->attachable;\n
\n
        if ($parent instanceof ItTicketDraft) {\n
            try {\n
                app(ItTicketDraftAttachmentService::class)->authorizeDownload($user, $attachment);\n
            } catch (ItTicketDraftException) {\n
                abort(404);\n
            }\n
        } elseif ($parent instanceof ItTicketComment) {\n
            abort_unless($this->workAccess->canView($user, $parent->ticket), 404);\n
            $this->authorize('view', $parent->ticket);\n
            if ($parent->is_internal) {\n
                abort_unless($this->workAccess->canWork($user, $parent->ticket), 404);\n
            }\n
        } elseif ($parent instanceof ItTicket) {\n
            abort_unless($this->workAccess->canView($user, $parent), 404);\n
            $this->authorize('view', $parent);\n
        } else {\n
            // KB attachments arrive with the Knowledge tab; orphans 404.\n
            abort(404);\n
        }\n
\n
        return $this->streamPrivateAttachment(\n
            null,\n
            $attachment->path,\n
            $attachment->original_name,\n
            $attachment->mime,\n
        );\n
    }\n
\n
    public function transition(TransitionItWorkRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($this->workAccess->canWork($user, $ticket), 404);\n
        abort_unless($user->can('update', $ticket), 403);\n
        $validated = $request->validated();\n
\n
        try {\n
            $ticket = $this->transitionService->transition(\n
                $ticket,\n
                new ItTransitionInput(\n
                    actor: $user,\n
                    to: ItWorkflowState::from((string) $validated['workflow_state']),\n
                    reason: $validated['reason'] ?? null,\n
                    waitingParty: $validated['waiting_party'] ?? null,\n
                    nextAction: $validated['next_action'] ?? null,\n
                    resolutionCode: $validated['resolution_code'] ?? null,\n
                    resolutionSummary: $validated['resolution_summary'] ?? null,\n
                    resolutionVerification: $validated['resolution_verification'] ?? null,\n
                    source: 'workspace',\n
                    expectedVersion: (int) $validated['expected_version'],\n
                ),\n
            );\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json(['data' => [\n
                'id' => (int) $ticket->id,\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $ticket->lock_version,\n
            ]]);\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', "Updated {$ticket->reference}.");\n
    }\n
\n
    /** The requester confirms the reviewed resolution without private work access. */\n
    public function confirmResolution(ConfirmTicketResolutionRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        try {\n
            $saved = $this->transitionService->transition($ticket, new ItTransitionInput(\n
                actor: $request->user(), to: ItWorkflowState::Closed,\n
                reason: 'Requester confirmed the fix', source: 'requester_confirmation',\n
                expectedVersion: (int) $request->validated('expected_version'),\n
            ));\n
        } catch (ItSettlementBlocked) {\n
            // The requester must not receive private task titles or direct IDs.\n
            return response()->json(['code' => 'confirmation_blocked',\n
                'message' => 'IT still needs to complete required work before this ticket can close. Your confirmation was not recorded.'],\n
                422, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
        } catch (DomainException) {\n
            return response()->json(['code' => 'confirmation_unavailable',\n
                'message' => 'This ticket can no longer be confirmed in its current state. Review the current ticket.'],\n
                422, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
        }\n
\n
        return response()->json(['status' => 'committed', 'data' => [\n
            'id' => (int) $saved->id, 'viewer_user_id' => (int) $request->user()->id,\n
            'lock_version' => (int) $saved->lock_version, 'operation' => 'resolution.confirm', 'status' => $saved->status,\n
        ]], 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
    }\n
\n
    /** Close a settled (or abandoned) ticket — terminal until reopened. */\n
    public function close(CloseTicketRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
\n
        try {\n
            $saved = $this->triageService->closeWithReason(\n
                $ticket,\n
                $user,\n
                (string) $request->validated('reason'),\n
                expectedVersion: (int) $request->validated('expected_version'),\n
            );\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
                return response()->json(['code' => 'close_unavailable', 'message' => $exception->getMessage()],\n
                    422, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
            }\n
\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json(['status' => 'committed', 'data' => [\n
                'id' => (int) $saved->id,\n
                'viewer_user_id' => (int) $user->id,\n
                'operation' => 'ticket.close',\n
                'status' => $saved->status,\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $saved->lock_version,\n
                'reason' => (string) $request->validated('reason'),\n
            ]], 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', "Closed {$ticket->reference}.");\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * Bring a settled ticket back: agents anytime, the requester within\n
     * 7 days of resolution (ItTicketPolicy::reopen).\n
     */\n
    public function reopen(ReopenTicketRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
\n
        try {\n
            $result = $this->interactionService->reopenWithReason(\n
                $ticket,\n
                $user,\n
                (string) $request->validated('reason'),\n
                (int) $request->validated('expected_version'),\n
            );\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
                return response()->json(['code' => 'reopen_unavailable', 'message' => $exception->getMessage()],\n
                    422, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
            }\n
\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
        $ticket = $result['ticket'];\n
\n
        $recipients = $ticket->watchers\n
            ->when($ticket->assignee, fn ($collection) => $collection->push($ticket->assignee))\n
            ->unique('id')\n
            ->reject(fn ($recipient) => (int) $recipient->id === (int) $user->id);\n
        $this->emailDeliveries->send($recipients, new TicketReopenedNotification($ticket));\n
\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json(['status' => 'committed', 'data' => [\n
                'id' => (int) $ticket->id,\n
                'viewer_user_id' => (int) $user->id,\n
                'operation' => 'ticket.reopen',\n
                'status' => $ticket->status,\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $ticket->lock_version,\n
                'reason' => $result['comment']->body,\n
                'comment_id' => (int) $result['comment']->id,\n
                'visibility' => $result['is_requester'] ? 'public' : 'internal',\n
            ]], 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', "Reopened {$ticket->reference}.");\n
    }\n
\n
    public function mergePreview(PreviewTicketMergeRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $target = ItTicket::query()->findOrFail($request->integer('target_ticket_id'));\n
        $preview = $this->mergeService->preview($ticket, $target, $request->user(),\n
            $request->integer('source_version'), $request->integer('target_version'));\n
\n
        return response()->json(['status' => 'reviewed', 'data' => [\n
            'viewer_user_id' => (int) $request->user()->id,\n
            'review_nonce' => $request->validated('review_nonce'),\n
            ...$preview,\n
        ]], 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * Fold a duplicate SOURCE ticket into a TARGET survivor: the conversation\n
     * and watchers move across, the source closes as merged, and both\n
     * timelines get a `merged` marker. The locked lifecycle keeps the same\n
     * requester audience private and preserves each ticket's own history.\n
     */\n
    public function merge(MergeTicketRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        $target = $request->targetTicket();\n
        abort_unless($target instanceof ItTicket, 404);\n
        abort_unless(\n
            $this->workAccess->canWork($user, $ticket)\n
                && $this->workAccess->canWork($user, $target),\n
            404,\n
        );\n
        try {\n
            $result = $this->mergeService->execute(\n
                $ticket,\n
                $target,\n
                $user,\n
                $request->validated(),\n
            );\n
        } catch (ItTicketCommandConflict $exception) {\n
            return response()->json(['code' => 'command_conflict', 'message' => $exception->getMessage()],\n
                409, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
                return response()->json(['code' => 'merge_blocked', 'message' => $exception->getMessage(),\n
                    'errors' => ['form' => [$exception->getMessage()]]], 422, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
            }\n
\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json($result, 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
        }\n
        if ($result['status'] !== 'committed') {\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', 'This merge command was cancelled. Review the tickets before starting another merge.');\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()\n
            ->route('it.tickets.show', $target)\n
            ->with('success', "Merged {$ticket->reference} into {$target->reference}.");\n
    }\n
\n
    public function mergeCommand(ReadTicketMergeCommandRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket, string $requestUuid)\n
    {\n
        $target = ItTicket::query()->findOrFail($request->integer('target_ticket_id'));\n
        try {\n
            $result = $this->mergeService->recover($ticket, $target, $request->user(), $requestUuid, $request->isMethod('post'));\n
        } catch (ModelNotFoundException $exception) {\n
            if ($exception->getModel() !== ItTicketCommandReceipt::class) {\n
                throw $exception;\n
            }\n
\n
            // Both parents and the actor were reauthorized inside recover(). A\n
            // missing receipt is distinct from lost access and proves no outcome.\n
            return response()->json(['code' => 'merge_receipt_unconfirmed', 'viewer_user_id' => (int) $request->user()->id,\n
                'source_id' => (int) $ticket->id, 'target_id' => (int) $target->id, 'request_uuid' => $requestUuid],\n
                404, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
        }\n
\n
        return response()->json($result, 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
    }\n
\n
    public function validateMergeCandidate(ValidateItMergeCandidateRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        return response()->json($this->mergeService->validateCandidate($ticket, $request->user(), $request->validated()),\n
            200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * CSAT (§K): the requester rates the resolution 1–5 (+ optional comment).\n
     * The first submission receives the immutable stamp; later edits retain it\n
     * and receive their own explicit change trail.\n
     */\n
    public function csat(SubmitCsatRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($this->workAccess->canView($user, $ticket), 404);\n
        abort_unless($user->can('csat', $ticket), 403);\n
        $saved = $this->interactionService->submitCsat(\n
            $ticket,\n
            $user,\n
            (int) $request->validated('score'),\n
            $request->validated('comment') ?: null,\n
            (int) $request->validated('expected_version'),\n
        );\n
\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json(['status' => 'committed', 'data' => [\n
                'id' => (int) $saved->id,\n
                'viewer_user_id' => (int) $user->id,\n
                'operation' => 'csat.save',\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $saved->lock_version,\n
                'score' => (int) $saved->csat_score,\n
                'comment' => $saved->csat_comment,\n
                'submitted_at' => $saved->csat_submitted_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
            ]], 200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Thanks — your feedback helps IT improve.');\n
    }\n
\n
    public function watch(Request $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($user, 403);\n
        $this->interactionService->watch($ticket, $user);\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', "Watching {$ticket->reference}.");\n
    }\n
\n
    public function unwatch(Request $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($user, 403);\n
        $this->interactionService->unwatch($ticket, $user);\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', "Stopped watching {$ticket->reference}.");\n
    }\n
\n
    public function updateWatcher(UpdateTicketWatcherRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket, int $watcherUserId)\n
    {\n
        $result = $this->interactionService->setWatcher($ticket, $request->user(), $watcherUserId,\n
            $request->boolean('watching'), (int) $request->validated('expected_version'));\n
\n
        return response()->json(['status' => 'committed', 'data' => $result->toArray((int) $request->user()->id)],\n
            200, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
    }\n
\n
    /* ================================================================== */\n
    /*  Bulk actions (§F2) */\n
    /* ================================================================== */\n
\n
    /**\n
     * One action over many tickets: assign, set priority (restamps the SLA\n
     * clock), set a working status (waiting transitions bank the pause), or\n
     * close. Inaccessible IDs silently drop out of the canonical work-access\n
     * query; settled tickets are skipped rather than mutated — the flash\n
     * reports both as "unchanged". One event row per actual change, same\n
     * payload shape as the single-ticket routes.\n
     */\n
    public function bulk(BulkTicketActionRequest $request)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        $validated = $request->validated();\n
        $action = (string) $validated['action'];\n
\n
        $assignee = null;\n
        if ($action === 'assign' && ! empty($validated['assigned_to_user_id'])) {\n
            $assignee = User::query()->find((int) $validated['assigned_to_user_id']);\n
            if (! $assignee || ! ItStaffDirectory::agents()->contains('id', $assignee->id)) {\n
                return redirect()->back()->with('error', 'Choose a current IT technician.');\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        $tickets = $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)\n
            ->whereIn('id', $validated['ids'])\n
            ->get()\n
            ->filter(fn (ItTicket $ticket) => $this->workAccess->canWork($user, $ticket))\n
            ->keyBy('id');\n
\n
        $items = [];\n
\n
        foreach ($validated['ids'] as $selectedId) {\n
            $ticket = $tickets->get((int) $selectedId);\n
            $outcome = $ticket === null ? ItBulkActionResult::outcome('unavailable') : match ($action) {\n
                'assign' => $this->triageService->bulkOutcome($ticket, $user, [\n
                    'assigned_to_user_id' => $assignee?->id,\n
                    'routing_reason' => $validated['routing_reason'],\n
                    'expected_version' => (int) $validated['expected_versions'][$ticket->id],\n
                ], 'bulk'),\n
                'priority' => $this->triageService->bulkOutcome($ticket, $user, [\n
                    'priority' => (string) $validated['priority'],\n
                    'priority_reason' => $validated['priority_reason'],\n
                    'expected_version' => (int) $validated['expected_versions'][$ticket->id],\n
                ], 'bulk'),\n
                'status' => $this->triageService->bulkOutcome($ticket, $user, [\n
                    'status' => (string) $validated['status'],\n
                    'expected_version' => (int) $validated['expected_versions'][$ticket->id],\n
                    ...Arr::only($validated, ['waiting_party', 'waiting_reason', 'next_action']),\n
                ], 'bulk'),\n
                'close' => ItBulkActionResult::capture(fn (): bool => $this->triageService->close(\n
                    $ticket,\n
                    $user,\n
                    (string) $validated['reason'],\n
                    source: 'bulk_close',\n
                    expectedVersion: (int) $validated['expected_versions'][$ticket->id],\n
                )),\n
            };\n
            $items[] = ['id' => (int) $selectedId, ...$outcome];\n
        }\n
\n
        $result = ItBulkActionResult::payload('tickets', $action, $items);\n
        $label = ['assign' => 'assigned', 'priority' => 'reprioritised', 'status' => 'updated', 'close' => 'closed'][$action];\n
        $unchanged = $result['selected'] - $result['updated'];\n
        $message = "{$result['updated']} ticket(s) {$label}".($unchanged > 0 ? " · {$unchanged} unchanged" : '').'.';\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json(['status' => 'completed', 'viewer_user_id' => (int) $user->id, 'message' => $message, 'result' => $result]);\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()\n
            ->with('it_bulk_result', ['actor_user_id' => (int) $user->id, ...$result])\n
            ->with($result['rejected'] > 0 ? 'warning' : ($result['updated'] > 0 ? 'success' : 'info'), $message);\n
    }\n
}\n

    public function retryPending(int $limit = 100): array\n
    {\n
        if ($limit < 1 || $limit > 1000 || DB::transactionLevel() !== 0 || DB::connection()->getPdo()->inTransaction()) {\n
            throw new LogicException('Cleanup requires a bounded run outside an existing transaction.');\n
        }\n
        $direct = app(ItAttachmentStorageIntentService::class);\n
        if (! Schema::hasColumn('it_attachments', 'inbound_storage_state')) {\n
            return $direct->retryPending($limit);\n
        }\n
        $staging = app(ItInboundAttachmentStaging::class);\n
        // One shared budget. Never-attempted work comes first, then the oldest retry;\n
        // one failed file cannot consume the whole run or starve the other source.\n
        $candidates = ItAttachmentStorageIntent::query()->useWritePdo()->where('state', 'cleanup_pending')\n
            ->orderBy('last_cleanup_attempt_at')->orderBy('cleanup_attempts')->orderBy('id')->limit($limit)\n
            ->get(['id', 'last_cleanup_attempt_at', 'cleanup_attempts'])\n
            ->map(fn ($row) => ['kind' => 'direct', 'id' => (int) $row->id,\n
                'at' => $row->last_cleanup_attempt_at?->getTimestamp(), 'attempts' => $row->cleanup_attempts])->all();\n
        foreach ($staging->pendingCleanupQuery()\n
            ->orderByRaw('CASE WHEN inbound_cleanup_attempts = 0 THEN 0 ELSE 1 END')\n
            ->orderByRaw('CASE WHEN inbound_cleanup_attempts = 0 THEN NULL ELSE updated_at END')\n
            ->orderBy('inbound_cleanup_attempts')->orderBy('id')->limit($limit)\n
            ->get(['id', 'updated_at', 'inbound_cleanup_attempts']) as $row) {\n
            $candidates[] = ['kind' => 'inbound', 'id' => (int) $row->id,\n
                'at' => $row->inbound_cleanup_attempts === 0 ? null : $row->updated_at->getTimestamp(),\n
                'attempts' => $row->inbound_cleanup_attempts];\n
        }\n
        if (Schema::hasColumn('it_attachment_storage_intents', 'inbound_email_id')) {\n
            foreach ($direct->abandonedMailboxQuery()->orderBy('last_cleanup_attempt_at')->orderBy('cleanup_attempts')->orderBy('id')->limit($limit)\n
                ->get(['id', 'last_cleanup_attempt_at', 'cleanup_attempts']) as $row) {\n
                $candidates[] = ['kind' => 'mailbox', 'id' => (int) $row->id,\n
                    'at' => $row->last_cleanup_attempt_at?->getTimestamp(), 'attempts' => $row->cleanup_attempts];\n
            }\n
        }\n
        usort($candidates, fn ($a, $b) => [$a['at'] !== null, $a['at'] ?? 0, $a['attempts'], $a['id'], $a['kind']]\n
            <=> [$b['at'] !== null, $b['at'] ?? 0, $b['attempts'], $b['id'], $b['kind']]);\n
        $selected = ['direct' => [], 'inbound' => [], 'mailbox' => []];\n
        foreach (array_slice($candidates, 0, $limit) as $candidate) {\n
            $selected[$candidate['kind']][] = $candidate['id'];\n
        }\n
        $result = ['requested' => 0, 'deleted' => 0, 'failed' => 0, 'deferred' => 0, 'reconciliation_required' => 0];\n
        if ($selected['direct'] !== []) {\n
            $result = $direct->retryPending(count($selected['direct']), $selected['direct']);\n
        }\n
        if ($selected['inbound'] !== []) {\n
            $inbound = $staging->cleanupPending(count($selected['inbound']), onlyIds: $selected['inbound']);\n
            $result['requested'] += $inbound['deleted'] + $inbound['failed'];\n
            $result['deleted'] += $inbound['deleted'];\n
            $result['failed'] += $inbound['failed'];\n
        }\n
        if ($selected['mailbox'] !== []) {\n
            $mailbox = $direct->retryAbandonedMailboxReservations($selected['mailbox']);\n
            foreach ($result as $key => $count) {\n
                $result[$key] = $count + $mailbox[$key];\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        return $result;\n
    }\n
\n
    /** Write only a previously committed, opaque draft-owned private path. */\n
    public function storeReserved(ItAttachment $attachment, UploadedFile $file): void\n
    {\n
        if ($attachment->attachable_type !== (new ItTicketDraft)->getMorphClass()\n
            || $attachment->draft_generation_uuid === null\n
            || ! in_array($attachment->draft_storage_state, ['reserved', 'failed'], true)\n
            || preg_match('/^it_attachments\/[a-f0-9-]{36}$/', $attachment->path) !== 1) {\n

        }\n
        $path = Storage::disk(ItAttachment::DISK)->putFileAs('it_attachments', $file, basename($attachment->path));\n
        if ($path !== $attachment->path) {\n
            throw new DomainException('The private attachment could not be stored. Retry the same upload.');\n
        }\n
    }\n

/** Flash error carried by an Inertia redirect (validation / logic-guard). Read\n
 *  from the page passed to onSuccess — `back()->with('error')` fires onSuccess,\n
 *  not onError (see reference_inertia_flash_error). */\n
function pageFlashError(page: {\n
    props: Record[39m[31m<string, unknown>[39m[31m;\n
}): string | null {\n

/** Sentinel — Radix [39m[31m<SelectItem value="">[39m[31m crashes at runtime. */\n
const UNASSIGNED = 'unassigned';\n
\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
/*  Dispatcher                                                        */\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
\n
export function ItWizard({\n
    modal,\n
    assignees,\n
    employeeOptions = [],\n
    assetOptions = [],\n
    siteOptions = [],\n
    deviceOptions = [],\n
    serviceOptions = [],\n
    intakePolicy,\n
    slaPolicies,\n
    slaCalendar,\n
    kbSuggestions = [],\n
    kbOptions = { owners: [], sites: [], services: [] },\n
    onOpenArticle,\n
    onDraftKb,\n
    onClose,\n
}: {\n
    modal: ItModal | null;\n
    assignees: AssigneeOption[];\n
    employeeOptions?: EmployeeOption[];\n
    assetOptions?: AssetOption[];\n
    siteOptions?: SiteOption[];\n
    deviceOptions?: DeviceOption[];\n
    serviceOptions?: ServiceOption[];\n
    intakePolicy?: TicketIntakePolicy;\n
    slaPolicies?: SlaPolicyGrid | null;\n
    slaCalendar?: SlaCalendar | null;\n
    kbSuggestions?: KbSuggestion[];\n
    kbOptions?: KbOptions;\n
    onOpenArticle?: (id: number) => void;\n
    onDraftKb?: (draft: KbDraft) => void;\n
    onClose: () => void;\n
}) {\n
    const page = usePage<\n
        SharedData & { draftRecovery?: { enabled: boolean } }\n
    >();\n
    const actorId = page.props.auth.user.id;\n
    const draftRecoveryEnabled = page.props.draftRecovery?.enabled === true;\n
    if (!modal) return null;\n
    switch (modal.type) {\n
        case 'ticket':\n
            return (\n
                [39m[31m<CreateTicketWizard\n
                    key={actorId}\n
                    actorId={actorId}\n
                    draftRecoveryEnabled={draftRecoveryEnabled}\n
                    assignees={assignees}\n
                    employeeOptions={employeeOptions}\n
                    assetOptions={assetOptions}\n
                    siteOptions={siteOptions}\n
                    deviceOptions={deviceOptions}\n
                    serviceOptions={serviceOptions}\n
                    intakePolicy={intakePolicy}\n
                    slaPolicies={slaPolicies}\n
                    slaCalendar={slaCalendar}\n
                    provisioning={modal.provisioning}\n
                    onClose={onClose}\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            );\n
        case 'new-request':\n
            return (\n
                [39m[31m<NewProvisioningRequestDialog\n
                    employeeOptions={employeeOptions}\n
                    assignees={assignees}\n
                    onClose={onClose}\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            );\n
        case 'kb':\n
            return (\n
                [39m[31m<KbArticleDialog\n
                    article={modal.article}\n
                    draft={modal.draft}\n
                    options={kbOptions}\n
                    onClose={onClose}\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            );\n
        case 'raise':\n
            return (\n
                [39m[31m<RaiseTicketDialog\n
                    key={actorId}\n
                    actorId={actorId}\n
                    draftRecoveryEnabled={draftRecoveryEnabled}\n
                    siteOptions={siteOptions}\n
                    kbSuggestions={kbSuggestions}\n
                    onOpenArticle={onOpenArticle}\n
                    onClose={onClose}\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            );\n
        case 'sla':\n
            return slaPolicies ? (\n
                [39m[31m<SlaPolicyDialog\n
                    policies={slaPolicies}\n
                    calendar={slaCalendar}\n
                    onClose={onClose}\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            ) : null;\n
        case 'resolve':\n
            return (\n
                [39m[31m<ResolveTicketDialog\n
                    ticket={modal.ticket}\n
                    onDraftKb={onDraftKb}\n
                    onClose={onClose}\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            );\n
        case 'fulfil':\n
            return (\n
                [39m[31m<FulfilRequestDialog\n
                    request={modal.request}\n
                    onClose={onClose}\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            );\n
        case 'fail-request':\n
            return (\n
                [39m[31m<FailRequestDialog request={modal.request} onClose={onClose} />[39m[31m\n
            );\n
        case 'assign-request':\n
            return (\n
                [39m[31m<AssignDialog\n
                    heading="Assign request"\n
                    subject={modal.request.item}\n
                    currentId={modal.request.assignee?.id ?? null}\n
                    endpoint={{\n
                        method: 'post',\n
                        url: `/it/provisioning/${modal.request.id}/assign`,\n
                        field: 'assigned_to_user_id',\n
                    }}\n
                    assignees={assignees}\n
                    onClose={onClose}\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            );\n
        case 'assign-ticket':\n
            return (\n
                [39m[31m<AssignDialog\n
                    heading="Assign ticket"\n
                    subject={modal.ticket.title}\n
                    currentId={modal.ticket.assignee?.id ?? null}\n
                    endpoint={{\n
                        method: 'patch',\n
                        url: `/it/tickets/${modal.ticket.id}`,\n
                        field: 'assigned_to_user_id',\n
                        ticketId: modal.ticket.id,\n
                        expectedVersion: modal.ticket.lock_version,\n
                    }}\n
                    assignees={assignees}\n
                    onClose={onClose}\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            );\n
    }\n
}\n
\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
/*  Log ticket (3 steps)                                              */\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
\n
const TICKET_STEPS: readonly WizardStep[] = [\n
    { key: 'details', label: 'Details', blurb: 'What & where', icon: FileText },\n
    { key: 'triage', label: 'Triage', blurb: 'Priority & owner', icon: Flag },\n
    {\n
        key: 'review',\n
        label: 'Review',\n
        blurb: 'Confirm & log',\n
        icon: CheckCircle2,\n
    },\n
];\n
\n
const CATEGORY_OPTIONS = [\n
    {\n
        key: 'hardware',\n
        label: 'Hardware',\n
        description: 'Laptops, phones, printers & devices',\n
        icon: Laptop,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'account',\n
        label: 'Account',\n
        description: 'Logins, email & software access',\n
        icon: Mail,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'network',\n
        label: 'Network',\n
        description: 'Wi-Fi, VPN & connectivity',\n
        icon: Wifi,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'other',\n
        label: 'Other',\n
        description: 'Anything else IT should look at',\n
        icon: Server,\n
    },\n
] as const;\n
\n
const PRIORITY_OPTIONS = [\n
    { key: 'low', label: 'Low', description: 'When time allows' },\n
    { key: 'normal', label: 'Normal', description: 'Business as usual' },\n
    { key: 'high', label: 'High', description: 'Blocking someone’s work' },\n
    {\n
        key: 'urgent',\n
        label: 'Urgent',\n
        description: 'Site-wide / safety impact',\n
    },\n
] as const;\n
\n
const INTAKE_WORK_TYPE_OPTIONS = [\n
    {\n
        key: 'incident',\n
        label: 'Incident',\n
        description: 'Something is broken or degraded',\n
        icon: Ticket,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'service_request',\n
        label: 'Service request',\n
        description: 'Access, equipment or standard help',\n
        icon: ClipboardCheck,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'security_request',\n
        label: 'Security request',\n
        description: 'A security concern or sensitive access',\n
        icon: KeyRound,\n
    },\n
] as const;\n
\n
function CreateTicketWizard({\n
    actorId,\n
    draftRecoveryEnabled,\n
    assignees,\n
    employeeOptions,\n
    assetOptions,\n
    siteOptions,\n
    deviceOptions,\n
    serviceOptions,\n
    intakePolicy,\n
    slaPolicies,\n
    slaCalendar,\n
    provisioning,\n
    onClose,\n
}: {\n
    actorId: number;\n
    draftRecoveryEnabled: boolean;\n
    assignees: AssigneeOption[];\n
    employeeOptions: EmployeeOption[];\n
    assetOptions: AssetOption[];\n
    siteOptions: SiteOption[];\n
    deviceOptions: DeviceOption[];\n
    serviceOptions: ServiceOption[];\n
    intakePolicy?: TicketIntakePolicy;\n
    slaPolicies?: SlaPolicyGrid | null;\n
    slaCalendar?: SlaCalendar | null;\n
    provisioning?: { id: number; item: string };\n
    onClose: () => void;\n
}) {\n
    const wizard = useWizard(TICKET_STEPS.length);\n
    const attachmentId = useId();\n
    const command = useItTicketCommand({\n
        actorId,\n
        draftRequestId:\n
            itIntakeDraftMemoryRequestIds(actorId, 'technician_intake')[0] ??\n
            preferredIntakeDraft(\n
                draftRecoveryEnabled,\n
                actorId,\n
                'technician_intake',\n
            ),\n
    });\n
    const created = command.result;\n
    const done = created !== null;\n
    const finishClose = () => {\n
        onClose();\n
        if (created) router.reload();\n
    };\n
\n
    const form = useForm<{\n
        title: string;\n
        description: string;\n
        category: string;\n
        subcategory: string;\n
        priority: TicketPriority | 'automatic';\n
        impact: TicketImpact;\n
        urgency: TicketUrgency;\n
        priority_reason: string;\n
        routing_reason: string;\n
        work_type: string;\n
        it_service_id: string;\n
        requester_user_id: string;\n
        assigned_to_user_id: string;\n
        asset_id: string;\n
        site_id: string;\n
        device_id: string;\n
        watchers: number[];\n
        provisioning_request_id: number | null;\n
        attachments: File[];\n
    }>({\n
        title: provisioning ? `Issue with ${provisioning.item}` : '',\n
        description: '',\n
        category: 'hardware',\n
        subcategory: '',\n
        priority: 'automatic',\n
        impact: 'individual',\n
        urgency: 'normal',\n
        priority_reason: '',\n
        routing_reason: '',\n
        work_type: 'incident',\n
        it_service_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
        requester_user_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
        assigned_to_user_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
        asset_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
        site_id:\n
            siteOptions.length === 1 ? String(siteOptions[0].id) : UNASSIGNED,\n
        device_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
        watchers: [],\n
        provisioning_request_id: provisioning?.id ?? null,\n
        attachments: [],\n
    });\n
\n
    const initialValues = useRef(form.data);\n
    const savedSnapshot = intakeDraftSnapshot(\n
        'technician_intake',\n
        form.data,\n
        wizard.index,\n
    );\n
    const intake = useItIntakeDraft({\n
        enabled: draftRecoveryEnabled,\n
        actorId,\n
        purpose: 'technician_intake',\n
        command,\n
        snapshot: savedSnapshot,\n
        dirty: form.isDirty,\n
        files: form.data.attachments,\n
    });\n
    const resumeBrowserWork = (restored: ItDraftBrowserRestored) => {\n
        form.setData({\n
            ...restoreIntakeDraft(initialValues.current, restored.snapshot),\n
            attachments: restored.files,\n
        });\n
        form.clearErrors();\n
        wizard.goTo(restored.snapshot.step_index);\n
    };\n
    const resumeDraft = (saved: ItDraftResumed) => {\n
        form.setData({\n
            ...restoreIntakeDraft(initialValues.current, saved.payload),\n
            attachments: [],\n
        });\n
        form.clearErrors();\n
        wizard.goTo(saved.payload.step_index);\n
    };\n
    const resetDraftFields = () => {\n
        form.reset();\n
        form.clearErrors('attachments');\n
        wizard.goTo(0);\n
    };\n
    const { setData, setDefaults, clearErrors } = form;\n
    useEffect(() => {\n
        if (\n
            command.state !== 'access_denied' &&\n
            intake.draft.state !== 'access_denied'\n
        )\n
            return;\n
        const cleared = {\n
            title: '',\n
            description: '',\n
            category: 'hardware',\n
            subcategory: '',\n
            priority: 'automatic' as const,\n
            impact: 'individual' as const,\n
            urgency: 'normal' as const,\n
            priority_reason: '',\n
            routing_reason: '',\n
            work_type: 'incident',\n
            it_service_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
            requester_user_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
            assigned_to_user_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
            asset_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
            site_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
            device_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
            watchers: [],\n
            provisioning_request_id: null,\n
            attachments: [],\n
        };\n
        setData(cleared);\n
        setDefaults(cleared);\n
        clearErrors();\n
    }, [command.state, intake.draft.state, setData, setDefaults, clearErrors]);\n
    const eligibleAssignees = assignees.filter((candidate) =>\n
        candidate.site_ids?.includes(Number(form.data.site_id)),\n
    );\n
    const eligibleRequesters = employeeOptions.filter((candidate) =>\n
        candidate.requester?.site_ids.includes(Number(form.data.site_id)),\n
    );\n
    const requester = eligibleRequesters.find(\n
        (candidate) =>\n
            String(candidate.requester?.user_id) ===\n
            form.data.requester_user_id,\n
    );\n
    const requesterValid =\n
        form.data.requester_user_id === UNASSIGNED || requester !== undefined;\n
    const assignee =\n
        eligibleAssignees.find(\n
            (a) => String(a.id) === form.data.assigned_to_user_id,\n
        ) ?? null;\n
    const asset =\n
        assetOptions.find((a) => String(a.id) === form.data.asset_id) ?? null;\n
    const site =\n
        siteOptions.find((s) => String(s.id) === form.data.site_id) ?? null;\n
    const availableDevices = deviceOptions.filter(\n
        (candidate) =>\n
            form.data.site_id !== UNASSIGNED &&\n
            candidate.site_id === Number(form.data.site_id),\n
    );\n
    const device =\n
        availableDevices.find(\n
            (candidate) => String(candidate.id) === form.data.device_id,\n
        ) ?? null;\n
    const service =\n
        serviceOptions.find((s) => String(s.id) === form.data.it_service_id) ??\n
        null;\n
    const requesterName =\n
        form.data.requester_user_id === UNASSIGNED\n
            ? 'Me (myself)'\n
            : requester?.name;\n
    const detailsValid =\n
        form.data.title.trim().length > 0 &&\n
        requesterValid &&\n
        siteOptions.some((site) => String(site.id) === form.data.site_id);\n
    const attachmentCount = draftRecoveryEnabled\n
        ? intake.draft.attachments.length\n
        : form.data.attachments.length;\n
    const hasPriorityOverride = form.data.priority !== 'automatic';\n
    const triageValid =\n
        (!hasPriorityOverride || form.data.priority_reason.trim().length > 0) &&\n
        (form.data.assigned_to_user_id === UNASSIGNED ||\n
            (assignee !== null && form.data.routing_reason.trim().length > 0));\n
    const assessedPriority =\n
        intakePolicy?.priority_matrix[form.data.impact]?.[form.data.urgency];\n
    const effectivePriority =\n
        form.data.priority === 'automatic'\n
            ? assessedPriority\n
            : form.data.priority;\n
\n
    // Targets describe the configured policy; the server records the calendar\n
    // and calculates actual deadlines when the ticket is saved.\n
    const slaTarget = effectivePriority\n
        ? (slaPolicies?.[effectivePriority] ?? null)\n
        : null;\n
    const targetLabel = (mins: number) =>\n
        `${mins.toLocaleString('en-NZ')} ${slaCalendar?.enabled ? 'working minutes' : 'minutes'}`;\n
\n
    const submit = () => {\n
        const data = form.data;\n
        void intake.submit({\n
            ...data,\n
            priority: data.priority === 'automatic' ? null : data.priority,\n
            priority_reason: hasPriorityOverride ? data.priority_reason : null,\n
            routing_reason:\n
                data.assigned_to_user_id === UNASSIGNED\n
                    ? null\n
                    : data.routing_reason,\n
            requester_user_id:\n
                data.requester_user_id === UNASSIGNED\n
                    ? null\n
                    : Number(data.requester_user_id),\n
            assigned_to_user_id:\n
                data.assigned_to_user_id === UNASSIGNED\n
                    ? null\n
                    : Number(data.assigned_to_user_id),\n
            asset_id:\n
                data.asset_id === UNASSIGNED ? null : Number(data.asset_id),\n
            site_id: Number(data.site_id),\n
            device_id:\n
                data.device_id === UNASSIGNED ? null : Number(data.device_id),\n
            it_service_id:\n
                data.it_service_id === UNASSIGNED\n
                    ? null\n
                    : Number(data.it_service_id),\n
            subcategory:\n
                data.subcategory.trim() === '' ? null : data.subcategory,\n
        });\n
    };\n
\n
    const logAnother = () => {\n
        form.reset();\n
        form.clearErrors('attachments');\n
        command.reset('new');\n
        wizard.goTo(0);\n
    };\n
\n
    const addWatcher = (v: string) => {\n
        if (v === UNASSIGNED) return;\n
        const id = Number(v);\n
        if (!form.data.watchers.includes(id)) {\n
            form.setData('watchers', [...form.data.watchers, id]);\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    return (\n
        [39m[31m<TicketCommandWizard\n
            command={command}\n
            dirty={intake.dirty}\n
            onDiscardBrowserWork={() =>[39m[31m {\n
                intake.draft.clearOwnedBrowserWork();\n
                resetDraftFields();\n
            }}\n
            draftExit={\n
                draftRecoveryEnabled\n
                    ? {\n
                          busy: intake.draft.busy,\n
                          canSave:\n
                              intake.draft.state === 'ready' &&\n
                              intake.draft.draft?.capabilities.save === true,\n
                          canDiscard:\n
                              ['ready', 'available'].includes(\n
                                  intake.draft.state,\n
                              ) &&\n
                              intake.draft.draft?.capabilities.discard === true,\n
                          save: intake.saveForClose,\n
                          discard: intake.discardForClose,\n
                          keepReference: intake.draft.draft\n
                              ? intake.keepReference\n
                              : undefined,\n
                      }\n
                    : undefined\n
            }\n
            open\n
            onClose={finishClose}\n
            title="Log & triage ticket"\n
            description="Log a helpdesk ticket on behalf of a colleague and triage it in one pass."\n
            railIcon={Ticket}\n
            railTitle="Log & triage"\n
            railSub="IT helpdesk"\n
            steps={TICKET_STEPS}\n
            stepIndex={wizard.index}\n
            onStepClick={wizard.goTo}\n
            pct={Math.round(\n
                ([\n
                    form.data.title.trim().length > 0,\n
                    form.data.site_id !== UNASSIGNED,\n
                    !!form.data.category,\n
                    !!form.data.impact && !!form.data.urgency,\n
                    triageValid,\n
                ].filter(Boolean).length /\n
                    5) *\n
                    100,\n
            )}\n
            success={\n
                done ? (\n
                    <WizardSuccessPane\n
                        title={\n
                            created?.reference\n
                                ? `Logged — ${created.reference}`\n
                                : 'Ticket logged'\n
                        }\n
                        blurb={\n
                            command.restoredFromReference ? (\n
                                <>\n
                                    Your saved request was found. Open it to\n
                                    review its details and current owner.\n
                                [39m[39;1m\n
                            ) : (\n
                                <>\n
                                    “{form.data.title}” is now in the helpdesk\n
                                    queue\n
                                    {assignee ? (\n
                                        <> with {assignee.name}[39;22m\n
                                    ) : null}\n
                                    .\n
                                \n
                            )\n
                        }\n
                        actions={\n
                            <>\n
                                {created ? (\n
                                    <Button\n
                                        onClick={() =>\n
                                            router.visit(\n
                                                `/it/tickets/${created.id}`,\n
                                            )\n
                                        }\n
                                    >\n
                                        Open {created.reference ?? 'ticket'}\n
                                    </Button>\n
                                ) : null}\n
                                <Button variant="outline" onClick={logAnother}>\n
                                    Log another\n
                                </Button>\n
                                <Button variant="ghost" onClick={finishClose}>\n
                                    Done\n
                                </Button>\n
                            \n
                        }\n
                    />\n
                ) : undefined\n
            }\n
            footerStart={\n
                wizard.isFirst ? null : (\n
                    <Button variant="outline" onClick={wizard.back}>\n
                        Back\n
                    </Button>\n
                )\n
            }\n
            footerEnd={\n
                <>\n
                    {wizard.isLast ? (\n
                        <Button\n
                            onClick={submit}\n
                            disabled={\n
                                !intake.canSubmit ||\n
                                !detailsValid ||\n
                                !triageValid\n
                            }\n
                        >\n
                            {command.isBusy || intake.preparing\n
                                ? 'Logging…'\n
                                : 'Log ticket'}\n
                        </Button>\n
                    ) : (\n
                        <Button\n
                            onClick={wizard.next}\n
                            disabled={\n
                                !command.canEdit ||\n
                                (wizard.index === 0 && !detailsValid) ||\n
                                (wizard.index === 1 && !triageValid)\n
                            }\n
                        >\n
                            Continue\n
                        </Button>\n
                    )}\n
                \n
            }\n
        >\n
            <TicketIntakeDraftPanel\n
                intake={intake}\n
                command={command}\n
                snapshot={savedSnapshot}\n
                dirty={form.isDirty}\n
                onResume={resumeDraft}\n
                onResumeMemory={resumeBrowserWork}\n
                onDiscarded={resetDraftFields}\n
                onStartNew={resetDraftFields}\n
                optionLabels={{\n
                    site_id: Object.fromEntries(\n
                        siteOptions.map((row) => [row.id, row.name]),\n
                    ),\n
                    assigned_to_user_id: Object.fromEntries(\n
                        assignees.map((row) => [row.id, row.name]),\n
                    ),\n
                    requester_user_id: Object.fromEntries(\n
                        eligibleRequesters.map((row) => [\n
                            row.requester!.user_id,\n
                            row.name,\n
                        ]),\n
                    ),\n
                    it_service_id: Object.fromEntries(\n
                        serviceOptions.map((row) => [row.id, row.name]),\n
                    ),\n
                    asset_id: Object.fromEntries(\n
                        assetOptions.map((row) => [row.id, row.name]),\n
                    ),\n
                }}\n
            />\n
            {wizard.index === 0 && (\n
                <WizardStepPane>\n
                    <StepHead\n
                        icon={FileText}\n
                        title="What’s the issue?"\n
                        blurb="Log it for the person who hit it, with any detail IT needs to act."\n
                    />\n
                    {form.data.provisioning_request_id !== null &&\n
                    command.state !== 'access_denied' ? (\n
                        <InfoCard icon={Server}>\n
                            {provisioning?.id ===\n
                            form.data.provisioning_request_id\n
                                ? `Linked to provisioning request — “${provisioning.item}”.`\n
                                : `Linked to provisioning request #${form.data.provisioning_request_id}.`}{' '}\n
                            The ticket will show on that request too.\n
                        </InfoCard>\n
                    ) : null}\n
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3.5">\n
                        {employeeOptions.length > 0 ||\n
                        form.data.requester_user_id !== UNASSIGNED ? (\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="Requester"\n
                                hint="who hit the problem"\n
                                error={\n
                                    command.fieldErrors.requester_user_id ||\n
                                    (!requesterValid\n
                                        ? 'The selected requester is unavailable for this Site. Choose an eligible employee or log for yourself.'\n
                                        : undefined)\n
                                }\n
                            >\n
                                <SelectInput\n
                                    value={form.data.requester_user_id}\n
                                    onChange={(v) =>\n
                                        form.setData('requester_user_id', v)\n
                                    }\n
                                    placeholder="Me (myself)"\n
                                    ariaLabel="Requester"\n
                                    options={[\n
                                        {\n
                                            value: UNASSIGNED,\n
                                            label: 'Me — logging for myself',\n
                                        },\n
                                        ...(!requesterValid\n
                                            ? [\n
                                                  {\n
                                                      value: form.data\n
                                                          .requester_user_id,\n
                                                      label: 'Selected requester unavailable for this Site',\n
                                                  },\n
                                              ]\n
                                            : []),\n
                                        ...eligibleRequesters.map((a) => ({\n
                                            value: String(a.requester!.user_id),\n
                                            label: a.name,\n
                                        })),\n
                                    ]}\n
                                />\n
                            </Field>\n
                        ) : null}\n
                        <TicketSiteField\n
                            sites={siteOptions}\n
                            error={command.fieldErrors.site_id}\n
                            value={form.data.site_id}\n
                            onChange={(v) => {\n
                                const selectedDevice = deviceOptions.find(\n
                                    (candidate) =>\n
                                        String(candidate.id) ===\n
                                        form.data.device_id,\n
                                );\n
                                const keepsAssignee = assignees\n
                                    .find(\n
                                        (candidate) =>\n
                                            String(candidate.id) ===\n
                                            form.data.assigned_to_user_id,\n
                                    )\n
                                    ?.site_ids?.includes(Number(v));\n
                                form.setData({\n
                                    ...form.data,\n
                                    site_id: v,\n
                                    assigned_to_user_id: keepsAssignee\n
                                        ? form.data.assigned_to_user_id\n
                                        : UNASSIGNED,\n
                                    routing_reason: keepsAssignee\n
                                        ? form.data.routing_reason\n
                                        : '',\n
                                    device_id:\n
                                        selectedDevice?.site_id === Number(v)\n
                                            ? form.data.device_id\n
                                            : UNASSIGNED,\n
                                });\n
                            }}\n
                        />\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Title"\n
                            required\n
                            error={command.fieldErrors.title}\n
                        >\n
                            <Input\n
                                value={form.data.title}\n
                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                    form.setData('title', e.target.value)\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="e.g. Printer offline — Sunnyside Lodge"\n
                                maxLength={255}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Detail"\n
                            hint="optional"\n
                            error={command.fieldErrors.description}\n
                        >\n
                            <Textarea\n
                                value={form.data.description}\n
                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                    form.setData('description', e.target.value)\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="Anything that helps IT reproduce or locate the issue…"\n
                                rows={4}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Category"\n
                            error={command.fieldErrors.category}\n
                        >\n
                            <TilePicker\n
                                value={form.data.category}\n
                                onChange={(v) => form.setData('category', v)}\n
                                options={[...CATEGORY_OPTIONS]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Subcategory"\n
                            hint="optional"\n
                            error={command.fieldErrors.subcategory}\n
                        >\n
                            <Input\n
                                value={form.data.subcategory}\n
                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                    form.setData('subcategory', e.target.value)\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="e.g. Label printer, VPN, mailbox…"\n
                                maxLength={255}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Photos or files"\n
                            hint="optional"\n
                            htmlFor={\n
                                !draftRecoveryEnabled\n
                                    ? `${attachmentId}-input`\n
                                    : undefined\n
                            }\n
                            labelId={\n
                                !draftRecoveryEnabled\n
                                    ? `${attachmentId}-label`\n
                                    : undefined\n
                            }\n
                            errorId={\n
                                !draftRecoveryEnabled\n
                                    ? `${attachmentId}-error`\n
                                    : undefined\n
                            }\n
                            error={\n
                                form.errors.attachments ??\n
                                command.fieldErrors.attachments\n
                            }\n
                        >\n
                            {draftRecoveryEnabled ? (\n
                                <TicketDraftFiles\n
                                    draft={intake.draft}\n
                                    disabled={!command.canEdit}\n
                                />\n
                            ) : (\n
                                <>\n
                                    <FileDropzone\n
                                        id={attachmentId}\n
                                        aria-labelledby={`${attachmentId}-label ${attachmentId}-title`}\n
                                        aria-describedby={\n
                                            (form.errors.attachments ??\n
                                            command.fieldErrors.attachments)\n
                                                ? `${attachmentId}-error`\n
                                                : undefined\n
                                        }\n
                                        aria-invalid={Boolean(\n
                                            form.errors.attachments ??\n
                                            command.fieldErrors.attachments,\n
                                        )}\n
                                        disabled={!command.canEdit}\n
                                        onFiles={(files) => {\n
                                            if (!command.canEdit) return;\n
                                            const error =\n
                                                itAttachmentSelectionError(\n
                                                    files,\n
                                                    form.data.attachments\n
                                                        .length,\n
                                                );\n
                                            if (error) {\n
                                                form.setError(\n
                                                    'attachments',\n
                                                    error,\n
                                                );\n
                                                return;\n
                                            }\n
                                            form.clearErrors('attachments');\n
                                            form.setData('attachments', [\n
                                                ...form.data.attachments,\n
                                                ...files,\n
                                            ]);\n
                                        }}\n
                                        accept={IT_ATTACHMENT_ACCEPT}\n
                                        title="Drop a photo or file"\n
                                        hint="Images, PDF or documents — up to 5 files"\n
                                    />\n
                                    {form.data.attachments.map((file, i) => (\n
                                        <StagedFileCard\n
                                            key={`${file.name}-${i}`}\n
                                            file={file}\n
                                            onRemove={() => {\n
                                                form.clearErrors('attachments');\n
                                                form.setData(\n
                                                    'attachments',\n
                                                    form.data.attachments.filter(\n
                                                        (_, j) => j !== i,\n
                                                    ),\n
                                                );\n
                                            }}\n
                                        />\n
                                    ))}\n
