                                \n
                            )}\n
                        </Field>\n
                    </div>\n
                </WizardStepPane>\n
            )}\n
\n
            {wizard.index === 1 && (\n
                <WizardStepPane>\n
                    <StepHead\n
                        icon={Flag}\n
                        title="Priority & owner"\n
                        blurb="How urgent is it, who picks it up, and what’s it about?"\n
                    />\n
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3.5">\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Work type"\n
                            hint="required — controls the support workflow"\n
                            error={command.fieldErrors.work_type}\n
                        >\n
                            <TilePicker\n
                                value={form.data.work_type}\n
                                onChange={(v) => form.setData('work_type', v)}\n
                                options={[...INTAKE_WORK_TYPE_OPTIONS]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Affected service"\n
                            hint="optional — helps route the ticket to the right queue"\n
                            error={command.fieldErrors.it_service_id}\n
                        >\n
                            <SelectInput\n
                                value={form.data.it_service_id}\n
                                onChange={(v) =>\n
                                    form.setData('it_service_id', v)\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="No service selected"\n
                                options={[\n
                                    {\n
                                        value: UNASSIGNED,\n
                                        label: 'No service selected',\n
                                    },\n
                                    ...serviceOptions.map((serviceOption) => ({\n
                                        value: String(serviceOption.id),\n
                                        label: serviceOption.name,\n
                                    })),\n
                                ]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <TicketImpactUrgencyFields\n
                            impact={form.data.impact}\n
                            urgency={form.data.urgency}\n
                            onImpactChange={(value) =>\n
                                form.setData('impact', value)\n
                            }\n
                            onUrgencyChange={(value) =>\n
                                form.setData('urgency', value)\n
                            }\n
                            errors={command.fieldErrors}\n
                        />\n
                        <InfoCard icon={Flag}>\n
                            {assessedPriority ? (\n
                                <>\n
                                    Assessed priority:{' '}\n
                                    <strong>\n
                                        {\n
                                            PRIORITY_OPTIONS.find(\n
                                                (option) =>\n
                                                    option.key ===\n
                                                    assessedPriority,\n
                                            )?.label\n
                                        }\n
                                    </strong>\n
                                    . This follows the current impact and\n
                                    urgency policy.\n
                                \n
                            ) : (\n
                                'The server will assess priority from impact and urgency when this ticket is saved. A preview is unavailable.'\n
                            )}\n
                        </InfoCard>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Priority decision"\n
                            error={command.fieldErrors.priority}\n
                        >\n
                            <SelectInput\n
                                value={form.data.priority}\n
                                ariaLabel="Priority decision"\n
                                onChange={(value) =>\n
                                    form.setData({\n
                                        ...form.data,\n
                                        priority: value as\n
                                            | TicketPriority\n
                                            | 'automatic',\n
                                        priority_reason:\n
                                            value === 'automatic'\n
                                                ? ''\n
                                                : form.data.priority_reason,\n
                                    })\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="Use assessed priority"\n
                                options={[\n
                                    {\n
                                        value: 'automatic',\n
                                        label: 'Use assessed priority',\n
                                    },\n
                                    ...PRIORITY_OPTIONS.map((option) => ({\n
                                        value: option.key,\n
                                        label: `Set ${option.label.toLowerCase()} priority`,\n
                                    })),\n
                                ]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        {hasPriorityOverride && (\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="Why change the assessed priority?"\n
                                required\n
                                error={command.fieldErrors.priority_reason}\n
                            >\n
                                <Textarea\n
                                    value={form.data.priority_reason}\n
                                    onChange={(event) =>\n
                                        form.setData(\n
                                            'priority_reason',\n
                                            event.target.value,\n
                                        )\n
                                    }\n
                                    maxLength={1000}\n
                                    rows={3}\n
                                    placeholder="Explain the operational reason for this priority."\n
                                />\n
                            </Field>\n
                        )}\n
                        {slaTarget ? (\n
                            <InfoCard icon={Timer}>\n
                                First response target:{' '}\n
                                <strong>\n
                                    {targetLabel(\n
                                        slaTarget.first_response_minutes,\n
                                    )}\n
                                </strong>\n
                                . Resolution target:{' '}\n
                                <strong>\n
                                    {targetLabel(slaTarget.resolution_minutes)}\n
                                </strong>{' '}\n
                                at this priority. Deadlines are calculated when\n
                                saved.\n
                            </InfoCard>\n
                        ) : null}\n
                        {/* Triage is agent work — self-service requesters get no\n
                            assignee list from the server, so these fields hide. */}\n
                        {assignees.length > 0 ? (\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="Assign to"\n
                                hint="optional — automatic routing applies when saved"\n
                                error={\n
                                    command.fieldErrors.assigned_to_user_id ??\n
                                    (form.data.assigned_to_user_id !==\n
                                        UNASSIGNED && !assignee\n
                                        ? 'Choose a currently eligible technician for this Site.'\n
                                        : undefined)\n
                                }\n
                            >\n
                                <SelectInput\n
                                    value={form.data.assigned_to_user_id}\n
                                    onChange={(v) =>\n
                                        form.setData({\n
                                            ...form.data,\n
                                            assigned_to_user_id: v,\n
                                            routing_reason:\n
                                                v === UNASSIGNED\n
                                                    ? ''\n
                                                    : form.data.routing_reason,\n
                                        })\n
                                    }\n
                                    placeholder="Unassigned"\n
                                    options={[\n
                                        {\n
                                            value: UNASSIGNED,\n
                                            label: 'Unassigned',\n
                                        },\n
                                        ...eligibleAssignees.map((a) => ({\n
                                            value: String(a.id),\n
                                            label: a.name,\n
                                        })),\n
                                    ]}\n
                                />\n
                            </Field>\n
                        ) : null}\n
                        {form.data.assigned_to_user_id !== UNASSIGNED && (\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="Why this technician?"\n
                                required\n
                                error={command.fieldErrors.routing_reason}\n
                            >\n
                                <Textarea\n
                                    value={form.data.routing_reason}\n
                                    onChange={(event) =>\n
                                        form.setData(\n
                                            'routing_reason',\n
                                            event.target.value,\n
                                        )\n
                                    }\n
                                    maxLength={1000}\n
                                    rows={3}\n
                                    placeholder="Explain why this technician should take the work."\n
                                />\n
                            </Field>\n
                        )}\n
                        <InfoCard icon={Users}>\n
                            {form.data.assigned_to_user_id !== UNASSIGNED\n
                                ? 'This manual assignment and its reason are recorded. Later classification changes preserve it until an authorized technician releases the override; the selected technician must remain eligible.'\n
                                : 'Routing is applied when the ticket is saved, using its Site, service and classification. The saved ticket shows the actual queue, team and accountable owner, or any setup gap.'}\n
                        </InfoCard>\n
                        {assetOptions.length > 0 ? (\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="Linked asset"\n
                                hint="optional — from the assets register"\n
                                error={command.fieldErrors.asset_id}\n
                            >\n
                                <SelectInput\n
                                    value={form.data.asset_id}\n
                                    onChange={(v) =>\n
                                        form.setData('asset_id', v)\n
                                    }\n
                                    placeholder="No asset"\n
                                    options={[\n
                                        {\n
                                            value: UNASSIGNED,\n
                                            label: 'No asset',\n
                                        },\n
                                        ...assetOptions.map((a) => ({\n
                                            value: String(a.id),\n
                                            label: a.tag\n
                                                ? `${a.name} · ${a.tag}`\n
                                                : a.name,\n
                                        })),\n
                                    ]}\n
                                />\n
                            </Field>\n
                        ) : null}\n
                        {availableDevices.length > 0 ? (\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="Affected Device"\n
                                hint="optional — canonical Security & Devices record"\n
                                error={command.fieldErrors.device_id}\n
                            >\n
                                <SelectInput\n
                                    value={form.data.device_id}\n
                                    onChange={(v) =>\n
                                        form.setData('device_id', v)\n
                                    }\n
                                    placeholder="No Device"\n
                                    options={[\n
                                        {\n
                                            value: UNASSIGNED,\n
                                            label: 'No Device',\n
                                        },\n
                                        ...availableDevices.map((device) => ({\n
                                            value: String(device.id),\n
                                            label: `${device.name} · ${device.uid}`,\n
                                        })),\n
                                    ]}\n
                                />\n
                            </Field>\n
                        ) : null}\n
                        {assignees.length > 0 ? (\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="Watchers"\n
                                hint="optional — kept in the loop on updates"\n
                            >\n
                                <SelectInput\n
                                    value={UNASSIGNED}\n
                                    onChange={addWatcher}\n
                                    placeholder="Add a watcher"\n
                                    options={[\n
                                        {\n
                                            value: UNASSIGNED,\n
                                            label: 'Add a watcher…',\n
                                        },\n
                                        ...assignees\n
                                            .filter(\n
                                                (a) =>\n
                                                    !form.data.watchers.includes(\n
                                                        a.id,\n
                                                    ),\n
                                            )\n
                                            .map((a) => ({\n
                                                value: String(a.id),\n
                                                label: a.name,\n
                                            })),\n
                                    ]}\n
                                />\n
                                {form.data.watchers.length > 0 ? (\n
                                    <div className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">\n
                                        {form.data.watchers.map((id) => {\n
                                            const w = assignees.find(\n
                                                (a) => a.id === id,\n
                                            );\n
                                            return (\n
                                                <span\n
                                                    key={id}\n
                                                    className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full bg-accent px-2 py-0.5 text-[12px] font-medium text-primary"\n
                                                >\n
                                                    {w?.name ?? `#${id}`}\n
                                                    {/* eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax -- inline chip remove, not button chrome */}\n
                                                    <button\n
                                                        type="button"\n
                                                        onClick={() =>\n
                                                            form.setData(\n
                                                                'watchers',\n
                                                                form.data.watchers.filter(\n
                                                                    (x) =>\n
                                                                        x !==\n
                                                                        id,\n
                                                                ),\n
                                                            )\n
                                                        }\n
                                                        aria-label={`Remove ${w?.name ?? 'watcher'}`}\n
                                                        className="text-primary/70 hover:text-primary"\n
                                                    >\n
                                                        <X className="h-3 w-3" />\n
                                                    </button>\n
                                                </span>\n
                                            );\n
                                        })}\n
                                    </div>\n
                                ) : null}\n
                            </Field>\n
                        ) : null}\n
                    </div>\n
                </WizardStepPane>\n
            )}\n
\n
            {wizard.index === 2 && (\n
                <WizardStepPane>\n
                    <StepHead\n
                        icon={ClipboardCheck}\n
                        title="Review & log"\n
                        blurb="Check the ticket before it lands in the queue."\n
                    />\n
                    <TicketDuplicateSuggestions\n
                        actorId={actorId}\n
                        title={form.data.title}\n
                        siteId={\n
                            form.data.site_id === UNASSIGNED\n
                                ? null\n
                                : Number(form.data.site_id)\n
                        }\n
                        workType={form.data.work_type}\n
                        serviceId={\n
                            form.data.it_service_id === UNASSIGNED\n
                                ? null\n
                                : Number(form.data.it_service_id)\n
                        }\n
                    />\n
                    <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">\n
                        <ReviewCard\n
                            icon={FileText}\n
                            title="Details"\n
                            onEdit={() => wizard.goTo(0)}\n
                        >\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Requester"\n
                                value={requesterName}\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow label="Site" value={site?.name} />\n
                            <ReviewRow label="Title" value={form.data.title} />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Category"\n
                                value={\n
                                    CATEGORY_OPTIONS.find(\n
                                        (c) => c.key === form.data.category,\n
                                    )?.label\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Subcategory"\n
                                value={form.data.subcategory || undefined}\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Detail"\n
                                value={form.data.description || undefined}\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Files"\n
                                value={\n
                                    attachmentCount > 0\n
                                        ? `${attachmentCount} attached`\n
                                        : undefined\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                        </ReviewCard>\n
                        <ReviewCard\n
                            icon={Flag}\n
                            title="Triage"\n
                            onEdit={() => wizard.goTo(1)}\n
                        >\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Work type"\n
                                value={\n
                                    INTAKE_WORK_TYPE_OPTIONS.find(\n
                                        (option) =>\n
                                            option.key === form.data.work_type,\n
                                    )?.label\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Service"\n
                                value={service?.name ?? 'Not selected'}\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Impact"\n
                                value={\n
                                    TICKET_IMPACT_OPTIONS.find(\n
                                        (option) =>\n
                                            option.value === form.data.impact,\n
                                    )?.label\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Urgency"\n
                                value={\n
                                    TICKET_URGENCY_OPTIONS.find(\n
                                        (option) =>\n
                                            option.value === form.data.urgency,\n
                                    )?.label\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Priority"\n
                                value={\n
                                    PRIORITY_OPTIONS.find(\n
                                        (p) => p.key === effectivePriority,\n
                                    )?.label ?? 'Assessed when saved'\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            {hasPriorityOverride && (\n
                                <ReviewRow\n
                                    label="Priority reason"\n
                                    value={form.data.priority_reason}\n
                                />\n
                            )}\n
                            {slaTarget ? (\n
                                <ReviewRow\n
                                    label="Resolution target"\n
                                    value={`${targetLabel(slaTarget.resolution_minutes)} · Deadline calculated when saved`}\n
                                />\n
                            ) : null}\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Assign to"\n
                                value={\n
                                    assignee?.name ??\n
                                    'Automatic routing when saved'\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            {assignee && (\n
                                <ReviewRow\n
                                    label="Assignment reason"\n
                                    value={form.data.routing_reason}\n
                                />\n
                            )}\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Asset"\n
                                value={\n
                                    asset\n
                                        ? asset.tag\n
                                            ? `${asset.name} · ${asset.tag}`\n
                                            : asset.name\n
                                        : undefined\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Device"\n
                                value={\n
                                    device\n
                                        ? `${device.name} · ${device.uid}`\n
                                        : undefined\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            <ReviewRow\n
                                label="Watchers"\n
                                value={\n
                                    form.data.watchers.length > 0\n
                                        ? `${form.data.watchers.length}`\n
                                        : undefined\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                        </ReviewCard>\n
                    </div>\n
                </WizardStepPane>\n
            )}\n
        </TicketCommandWizard>\n
    );\n
}\n
\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
/*  New provisioning request (agent, 2 steps)                         */\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
\n
const PROVISIONING_STEPS: readonly WizardStep[] = [\n
    { key: 'what', label: 'Request', blurb: 'Who & what', icon: FileText },\n
    { key: 'assign', label: 'Assign', blurb: 'Owner & due', icon: Flag },\n
];\n
\n
const REQUEST_TYPE_OPTIONS = [\n
    {\n
        key: 'account',\n
        label: 'Account',\n
        description: 'Email, logins & software',\n
        icon: Mail,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'access',\n
        label: 'Access',\n
        description: 'Systems & permissions',\n
        icon: KeyRound,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'equipment',\n
        label: 'Equipment',\n
        description: 'Laptop, phone & devices',\n
        icon: Laptop,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'other',\n
        label: 'Other',\n
        description: 'Anything else to provision',\n
        icon: Server,\n
    },\n
] as const;\n
\n
function NewProvisioningRequestDialog({\n
    employeeOptions,\n
    assignees,\n
    onClose,\n
}: {\n
    employeeOptions: EmployeeOption[];\n
    assignees: AssigneeOption[];\n
    onClose: () => void;\n
}) {\n
    const wizard = useWizard(PROVISIONING_STEPS.length);\n
    const [done, setDone] = useState(false);\n
\n
    const form = useForm({\n
        employee_profile_id: '',\n
        type: 'account',\n
        item: '',\n
        assigned_to_user_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
        priority: 'normal',\n
        due_date: '',\n
        notes: '',\n
    });\n
\n
    const employee =\n
        employeeOptions.find(\n
            (e) => String(e.id) === form.data.employee_profile_id,\n
        ) ?? null;\n
    const detailsValid =\n
        form.data.employee_profile_id !== '' &&\n
        form.data.item.trim().length > 0;\n
\n
    const submit = () => {\n
        form.transform((data) => ({\n
            ...data,\n
            employee_profile_id: Number(data.employee_profile_id),\n
            assigned_to_user_id:\n
                data.assigned_to_user_id === UNASSIGNED\n
                    ? null\n
                    : Number(data.assigned_to_user_id),\n
            due_date: data.due_date === '' ? null : data.due_date,\n
            notes: data.notes.trim() === '' ? null : data.notes,\n
        }));\n
        form.post('/it/provisioning', {\n
            preserveScroll: true,\n
            onSuccess: (page) => {\n
                const err = pageFlashError(page);\n
                if (err) {\n
                    toast.error(err);\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
                setDone(true);\n
            },\n
        });\n
    };\n
\n
    return (\n
        <WizardShell\n
            open\n
            onClose={onClose}\n
            title="New provisioning request"\n
            description="Raise an ad-hoc account, access or equipment request."\n
            railIcon={Server}\n
            railTitle="New request"\n
            railSub="Provisioning"\n
            steps={PROVISIONING_STEPS}\n
            stepIndex={wizard.index}\n
            onStepClick={wizard.goTo}\n
            pct={wizard.progress}\n
            success={\n
                done ? (\n
                    <WizardSuccessPane\n
                        title="Request raised"\n
                        blurb={\n
                            <>\n
                                “{form.data.item}” is on the provisioning queue\n
                                {employee ? <> for {employee.name} : null}.\n
                            \n
                        }\n
                        actions={<Button onClick={onClose}>Done</Button>}\n
                    />\n
                ) : undefined\n
            }\n
            footerStart={\n
                wizard.isFirst ? null : (\n
                    <Button variant="outline" onClick={wizard.back}>\n
                        Back\n
                    </Button>\n
                )\n
            }\n
            footerEnd={\n
                <>\n
                    <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>\n
                        Cancel\n
                    </Button>\n
                    {wizard.isLast ? (\n
                        <Button\n
                            onClick={submit}\n
                            disabled={form.processing || !detailsValid}\n
                        >\n
                            {form.processing ? 'Raising…' : 'Raise request'}\n
                        </Button>\n
                    ) : (\n
                        <Button onClick={wizard.next} disabled={!detailsValid}>\n
                            Continue\n
                        </Button>\n
                    )}\n
                \n
            }\n
        >\n
            {wizard.index === 0 && (\n
                <WizardStepPane>\n
                    <StepHead\n
                        icon={FileText}\n
                        title="Who & what"\n
                        blurb="Who’s this for, and what needs provisioning?"\n
                    />\n
                    <div className="grid gap-3.5">\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Employee"\n
                            required\n
                            error={form.errors.employee_profile_id}\n
                        >\n
                            <SelectInput\n
                                value={form.data.employee_profile_id}\n
                                onChange={(v) =>\n
                                    form.setData('employee_profile_id', v)\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="Choose an employee"\n
                                options={employeeOptions.map((e) => ({\n
                                    value: String(e.id),\n
                                    label: e.name,\n
                                }))}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field label="Type" error={form.errors.type}>\n
                            <TilePicker\n
                                value={form.data.type}\n
                                onChange={(v) => form.setData('type', v)}\n
                                options={[...REQUEST_TYPE_OPTIONS]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field label="Item" required error={form.errors.item}>\n
                            <Input\n
                                value={form.data.item}\n
                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                    form.setData('item', e.target.value)\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="e.g. Replacement laptop"\n
                                maxLength={255}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                    </div>\n
                </WizardStepPane>\n
            )}\n
            {wizard.index === 1 && (\n
                <WizardStepPane>\n
                    <StepHead\n
                        icon={Flag}\n
                        title="Assign & schedule"\n
                        blurb="Owner, priority and a due date if there is one."\n
                    />\n
                    <div className="grid gap-3.5">\n
                        <Field label="Priority" error={form.errors.priority}>\n
                            <TilePicker\n
                                value={form.data.priority}\n
                                onChange={(v) => form.setData('priority', v)}\n
                                options={[...PRIORITY_OPTIONS]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        {assignees.length > 0 ? (\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="Assign to"\n
                                hint="optional"\n
                                error={form.errors.assigned_to_user_id}\n
                            >\n
                                <SelectInput\n
                                    value={form.data.assigned_to_user_id}\n
                                    onChange={(v) =>\n
                                        form.setData('assigned_to_user_id', v)\n
                                    }\n
                                    placeholder="Unassigned"\n
                                    options={[\n
                                        {\n
                                            value: UNASSIGNED,\n
                                            label: 'Unassigned',\n
                                        },\n
                                        ...assignees.map((a) => ({\n
                                            value: String(a.id),\n
                                            label: a.name,\n
                                        })),\n
                                    ]}\n
                                />\n
                            </Field>\n
                        ) : null}\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Due date"\n
                            hint="optional"\n
                            error={form.errors.due_date}\n
                        >\n
                            <Input\n
                                type="date"\n
                                value={form.data.due_date}\n
                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                    form.setData('due_date', e.target.value)\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Notes"\n
                            hint="optional"\n
                            error={form.errors.notes}\n
                        >\n
                            <Textarea\n
                                value={form.data.notes}\n
                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                    form.setData('notes', e.target.value)\n
                                }\n
                                rows={3}\n
                                placeholder="Anything the fulfiller should know…"\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                    </div>\n
                </WizardStepPane>\n
            )}\n
        </WizardShell>\n
    );\n
}\n
\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
/*  Raise a ticket (self-service, single step — speed IS the spec)    */\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
\n
const RAISE_STEPS: readonly WizardStep[] = [\n
    {\n
        key: 'raise',\n
        label: 'Raise a ticket',\n
        blurb: 'Tell IT what you need',\n
        icon: Ticket,\n
    },\n
];\n
\n
/** Plain-language categories for people mid-shift — no IT jargon. */\n
const RAISE_CATEGORY_OPTIONS = [\n
    {\n
        key: 'hardware',\n
        label: 'Device or hardware',\n
        description: 'Phone, laptop, printer, charger…',\n
        icon: Laptop,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'account',\n
        label: 'Account or sign-in',\n
        description: 'Locked out, passwords, email',\n
        icon: User,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'network',\n
        label: 'Wi-Fi or network',\n
        description: 'No internet, VPN trouble',\n
        icon: Wifi,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'other',\n
        label: 'Something else',\n
        description: 'Anything IT should look at',\n
        icon: Server,\n
    },\n
] as const;\n
\n
function RaiseTicketDialog({\n
    actorId,\n
    draftRecoveryEnabled,\n
    siteOptions,\n
    kbSuggestions = [],\n
    onOpenArticle,\n
    onClose,\n
}: {\n
    actorId: number;\n
    draftRecoveryEnabled: boolean;\n
    siteOptions: SiteOption[];\n
    kbSuggestions?: KbSuggestion[];\n
    onOpenArticle?: (id: number) => void;\n
    onClose: () => void;\n
}) {\n
    const wizard = useWizard(RAISE_STEPS.length);\n
    const attachmentId = useId();\n
    const command = useItTicketCommand({\n
        actorId,\n
        draftRequestId:\n
            itIntakeDraftMemoryRequestIds(actorId, 'requester_intake')[0] ??\n
            preferredIntakeDraft(\n
                draftRecoveryEnabled,\n
                actorId,\n
                'requester_intake',\n
            ),\n
    });\n
    const done = command.result !== null;\n
    const [moreDetails, setMoreDetails] = useState(false);\n
    const reference = command.result?.reference;\n
    const finishClose = () => {\n
        onClose();\n
        if (command.result) router.reload();\n
    };\n
\n
    const form = useForm<{\n
        title: string;\n
        description: string;\n
        category: string;\n
        impact: TicketImpact;\n
        urgency: TicketUrgency;\n
        site_id: string;\n
        attachments: File[];\n
    }>({\n
        title: '',\n
        description: '',\n
        category: 'hardware',\n
        impact: 'individual',\n
        urgency: 'normal',\n
        site_id:\n
            siteOptions.length === 1 ? String(siteOptions[0].id) : UNASSIGNED,\n
        attachments: [],\n
    });\n
\n
    const initialValues = useRef(form.data);\n
    const savedSnapshot = intakeDraftSnapshot(\n
        'requester_intake',\n
        form.data,\n
        wizard.index,\n
    );\n
    const intake = useItIntakeDraft({\n
        enabled: draftRecoveryEnabled,\n
        actorId,\n
        purpose: 'requester_intake',\n
        command,\n
        snapshot: savedSnapshot,\n
        dirty: form.isDirty,\n
        files: form.data.attachments,\n
    });\n
    const resumeBrowserWork = (restored: ItDraftBrowserRestored) => {\n
        form.setData({\n
            ...restoreIntakeDraft(initialValues.current, restored.snapshot),\n
            attachments: restored.files,\n
        });\n
        form.clearErrors();\n
        wizard.goTo(restored.snapshot.step_index);\n
        setMoreDetails(\n
            !!restored.snapshot.fields.description || restored.files.length > 0,\n
        );\n
    };\n
    const resumeDraft = (saved: ItDraftResumed) => {\n
        form.setData({\n
            ...restoreIntakeDraft(initialValues.current, saved.payload),\n
            attachments: [],\n
        });\n
        form.clearErrors();\n
        wizard.goTo(saved.payload.step_index);\n
        setMoreDetails(\n
            !!saved.payload.fields.description || saved.attachments.length > 0,\n
        );\n
    };\n
    const resetDraftFields = () => {\n
        form.reset();\n
        form.clearErrors('attachments');\n
        wizard.goTo(0);\n
    };\n
    const { setData, setDefaults, clearErrors } = form;\n
    useEffect(() => {\n
        if (\n
            command.state !== 'access_denied' &&\n
            intake.draft.state !== 'access_denied'\n
        )\n
            return;\n
        const cleared = {\n
            title: '',\n
            description: '',\n
            category: 'hardware',\n
            impact: 'individual' as const,\n
            urgency: 'normal' as const,\n
            site_id: UNASSIGNED,\n
            attachments: [],\n
        };\n
        setData(cleared);\n
        setDefaults(cleared);\n
        clearErrors();\n
    }, [command.state, intake.draft.state, setData, setDefaults, clearErrors]);\n
    const valid =\n
        form.data.title.trim().length > 0 &&\n
        siteOptions.some((site) => String(site.id) === form.data.site_id);\n
\n
    // Deflection (§I): live-match published article titles as the requester types.\n
    const query = form.data.title.trim().toLowerCase();\n
    const kbMatches =\n
        onOpenArticle && query.length >= 3\n
            ? kbSuggestions\n
                  .filter((s) => s.title.toLowerCase().includes(query))\n
                  .slice(0, 3)\n
            : [];\n
\n
    const submit = () => {\n
        void intake.submit({\n
            ...form.data,\n
            site_id: Number(form.data.site_id),\n
        });\n
    };\n
\n
    return (\n
        <TicketCommandWizard\n
            command={command}\n
            dirty={intake.dirty}\n
            draftExit={\n
                draftRecoveryEnabled\n
                    ? {\n
                          busy: intake.draft.busy,\n
                          canSave:\n
                              intake.draft.state === 'ready' &&\n
                              intake.draft.draft?.capabilities.save === true,\n
                          canDiscard:\n
                              ['ready', 'available'].includes(\n
                                  intake.draft.state,\n
                              ) &&\n
                              intake.draft.draft?.capabilities.discard === true,\n
                          save: intake.saveForClose,\n
                          discard: intake.discardForClose,\n
                          keepReference: intake.draft.draft\n
                              ? intake.keepReference\n
                              : undefined,\n
                      }\n
                    : undefined\n
            }\n
            open\n
            onClose={finishClose}\n
            title="Raise a ticket"\n
            onDiscardBrowserWork={() => {\n
                intake.draft.clearOwnedBrowserWork();\n
                resetDraftFields();\n
            }}\n
            description="Tell IT what you need help with."\n
            railIcon={Ticket}\n
            railTitle="Raise a ticket"\n
            railSub="IT helpdesk"\n
            steps={RAISE_STEPS}\n
            stepIndex={wizard.index}\n
            onStepClick={wizard.goTo}\n
            pct={Math.round(\n
                ([\n
                    form.data.title.trim().length > 0,\n
                    siteOptions.some(\n
                        (site) => String(site.id) === form.data.site_id,\n
                    ),\n
                    !!form.data.category,\n
                    !!form.data.impact,\n
                    !!form.data.urgency,\n
                ].filter(Boolean).length /\n
                    5) *\n
                    100,\n
            )}\n
            success={\n
                done ? (\n
                    <WizardSuccessPane\n
                        title={\n
                            reference\n
                                ? `Raised — ${reference}`\n
                                : 'Ticket raised'\n
                        }\n
                        blurb={\n
                            <>\n
                                Your request is saved. Open it now or return to{' '}\n
                                <strong>My tickets</strong> to follow its\n
                                progress.\n
                            \n
                        }\n
                        actions={\n
                            <>\n
                                <Button\n
                                    onClick={() =>\n
                                        command.result &&\n
                                        router.visit(command.result.url)\n
                                    }\n
                                >\n
                                    Open {reference}\n
                                </Button>\n
                                <Button variant="ghost" onClick={finishClose}>\n
                                    Done\n
                                </Button>\n
                            \n
                        }\n
                    />\n
                ) : undefined\n
            }\n
            footerStart={null}\n
            footerEnd={\n
                <>\n
                    <Button\n
                        onClick={submit}\n
                        disabled={!intake.canSubmit || !valid}\n
                    >\n
                        {command.isBusy || intake.preparing\n
                            ? 'Raising…'\n
                            : 'Raise ticket'}\n
                    </Button>\n
                \n
            }\n
        >\n
            <TicketIntakeDraftPanel\n
                intake={intake}\n
                command={command}\n
                snapshot={savedSnapshot}\n
                dirty={form.isDirty}\n
                onResume={resumeDraft}\n
                onDiscarded={resetDraftFields}\n
                onStartNew={resetDraftFields}\n
                optionLabels={{\n
                    site_id: Object.fromEntries(\n
                        siteOptions.map((row) => [row.id, row.name]),\n
                    ),\n
                }}\n
                onResumeMemory={resumeBrowserWork}\n
            />\n
            <WizardStepPane>\n
                <StepHead\n
                    icon={Ticket}\n
                    title="What’s the problem?"\n
                    blurb="Choose the affected Site and tell us what happened. You can add more detail if it helps."\n
                />\n
                <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3.5">\n
                    <TicketSiteField\n
                        sites={siteOptions}\n
                        value={form.data.site_id}\n
                        onChange={(value) => form.setData('site_id', value)}\n
                        error={command.fieldErrors.site_id}\n
                    />\n
                    <Field\n
                        label="What's broken?"\n
                        required\n
                        error={command.fieldErrors.title}\n
                    >\n
                        <Input\n
                            value={form.data.title}\n
                            onChange={(e) =>\n
                                form.setData('title', e.target.value)\n
                            }\n
                            placeholder="e.g. My work phone won’t charge"\n
                            maxLength={255}\n
                            autoFocus\n
                        />\n
                    </Field>\n
                    <TicketDuplicateSuggestions\n
                        actorId={actorId}\n
                        title={form.data.title}\n
                        siteId={\n
                            form.data.site_id === UNASSIGNED\n
                                ? null\n
                                : Number(form.data.site_id)\n
                        }\n
                    />\n
                    <Field\n
                        label="What kind of thing is it?"\n
                        error={command.fieldErrors.category}\n
                    >\n
                        <TilePicker\n
                            value={form.data.category}\n
                            onChange={(v) => form.setData('category', v)}\n
                            options={[...RAISE_CATEGORY_OPTIONS]}\n
                        />\n
                    </Field>\n
                    <TicketImpactUrgencyFields\n
                        impact={form.data.impact}\n
                        urgency={form.data.urgency}\n
                        onImpactChange={(value) =>\n
                            form.setData('impact', value)\n
                        }\n
                        onUrgencyChange={(value) =>\n
                            form.setData('urgency', value)\n
                        }\n
                        errors={command.fieldErrors}\n
                    />\n
                    {kbMatches.length > 0 ? (\n
                        <div className="min-w-0 rounded-xl border border-primary/30 bg-primary/5 p-3">\n
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[12px] font-semibold text-primary">\n
                                <BookOpen className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> These might\n
                                fix it now\n
                            </div>\n
                            <div className="mt-1.5 flex flex-col gap-1">\n
                                {kbMatches.map((s) => (\n
                                    // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax -- KB suggestion link, not button chrome\n
                                    <button\n
                                        key={s.id}\n
                                        type="button"\n
                                        onClick={() => onOpenArticle?.(s.id)}\n
                                        className="flex items-center justify-between gap-2 rounded-lg px-2 py-1.5 text-left text-[13px] transition-colors hover:bg-primary/10"\n
                                    >\n
                                        <span className="truncate font-medium">\n
                                            {s.title}\n
                                        </span>\n
                                        <span className="flex-none text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">\n
                                            Read →\n
                                        </span>\n
                                    </button>\n
                                ))}\n
                            </div>\n
                        </div>\n
                    ) : null}\n
                    {moreDetails ? (\n
                        <>\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="More details"\n
                                hint="optional"\n
                                error={command.fieldErrors.description}\n
                            >\n
                                <Textarea\n
                                    value={form.data.description}\n
                                    onChange={(e) =>\n
                                        form.setData(\n
                                            'description',\n
                                            e.target.value,\n
                                        )\n
                                    }\n
                                    placeholder="Anything that helps IT find or fix it — where you are, what you tried…"\n
                                    rows={4}\n
                                />\n
                            </Field>\n
                            <Field\n
                                label="Photos or files"\n
                                hint="optional — a photo says a lot"\n
                                htmlFor={\n
                                    !draftRecoveryEnabled\n
                                        ? `${attachmentId}-input`\n
                                        : undefined\n
                                }\n
                                labelId={\n
                                    !draftRecoveryEnabled\n
                                        ? `${attachmentId}-label`\n
                                        : undefined\n
                                }\n
                                errorId={\n
                                    !draftRecoveryEnabled\n
                                        ? `${attachmentId}-error`\n
                                        : undefined\n
                                }\n
                                error={\n
                                    form.errors.attachments ??\n
                                    command.fieldErrors.attachments\n
                                }\n
                            >\n
                                {draftRecoveryEnabled ? (\n
                                    <TicketDraftFiles\n
                                        draft={intake.draft}\n
                                        disabled={!command.canEdit}\n
                                    />\n
                                ) : (\n
                                    <>\n
                                        <FileDropzone\n
                                            id={attachmentId}\n
                                            aria-labelledby={`${attachmentId}-label ${attachmentId}-title`}\n
                                            aria-describedby={\n
                                                (form.errors.attachments ??\n
                                                command.fieldErrors.attachments)\n
                                                    ? `${attachmentId}-error`\n
                                                    : undefined\n
                                            }\n
                                            aria-invalid={Boolean(\n
                                                form.errors.attachments ??\n
                                                command.fieldErrors.attachments,\n
                                            )}\n
                                            disabled={!command.canEdit}\n
                                            onFiles={(files) => {\n
                                                if (!command.canEdit) return;\n
                                                const error =\n
                                                    itAttachmentSelectionError(\n
                                                        files,\n
                                                        form.data.attachments\n
                                                            .length,\n
                                                    );\n
                                                if (error) {\n
                                                    form.setError(\n
                                                        'attachments',\n
                                                        error,\n
                                                    );\n
                                                    return;\n
                                                }\n
                                                form.clearErrors('attachments');\n
                                                form.setData('attachments', [\n
                                                    ...form.data.attachments,\n
                                                    ...files,\n
                                                ]);\n
                                            }}\n
                                            accept={IT_ATTACHMENT_ACCEPT}\n
                                            title="Drop a photo of the problem"\n
                                            hint="Images, PDF or documents — up to 5 files"\n
                                        />\n
                                        {form.data.attachments.map(\n
                                            (file, i) => (\n
                                                <StagedFileCard\n
                                                    key={`${file.name}-${i}`}\n
                                                    file={file}\n
                                                    onRemove={() => {\n
                                                        form.clearErrors(\n
                                                            'attachments',\n
                                                        );\n
                                                        form.setData(\n
                                                            'attachments',\n
                                                            form.data.attachments.filter(\n
                                                                (_, j) =>\n
                                                                    j !== i,\n
                                                            ),\n
                                                        );\n
                                                    }}\n
                                                />\n
                                            ),\n
                                        )}\n
                                    \n
                                )}\n
                            </Field>\n
                        \n
                    ) : (\n
                        // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax -- text-link disclosure, not a button chrome\n
                        <button\n
                            type="button"\n
                            onClick={() => setMoreDetails(true)}\n
                            className="justify-self-start text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-primary hover:underline"\n
                        >\n
                            + Add more details\n
                        </button>\n
                    )}\n
                </div>\n
            </WizardStepPane>\n
        </TicketCommandWizard>\n
    );\n
}\n
\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
/*  Resolve ticket (single step — the note IS the record)             */\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
\n
export { ResolveTicketDialog };\n
\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
/*  SLA targets (single step, admin-only — §N7)                       */\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
\n
const SLA_STEPS: readonly WizardStep[] = [\n
    {\n
        key: 'targets',\n
        label: 'SLA targets',\n
        blurb: 'Minutes per priority',\n
        icon: Timer,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'hours',\n
        label: 'Business hours',\n
        blurb: 'When the clock runs',\n
        icon: CalendarClock,\n
    },\n
];\n
\n
/** §G defaults — what "Reset to defaults" restores. */\n
const SLA_DEFAULTS: Record<\n
    string,\n
    { first_response_minutes: string; resolution_minutes: string }\n
> = {\n
    urgent: { first_response_minutes: '60', resolution_minutes: '240' },\n
    high: { first_response_minutes: '240', resolution_minutes: '1440' },\n
    normal: { first_response_minutes: '1440', resolution_minutes: '4320' },\n
    low: { first_response_minutes: '4320', resolution_minutes: '10080' },\n
};\n
\n
const SLA_PRIORITY_HINTS: Record<string, string> = {\n
    urgent: 'Stops someone supporting a person right now',\n
    high: 'Blocking their work',\n
    normal: 'Annoying, but they can work',\n
    low: 'Whenever there’s a moment',\n
};\n
\n
const SLA_WORKING_DAYS: readonly { key: string; short: string }[] = [\n
    { key: 'mon', short: 'Mon' },\n
    { key: 'tue', short: 'Tue' },\n
    { key: 'wed', short: 'Wed' },\n
    { key: 'thu', short: 'Thu' },\n
    { key: 'fri', short: 'Fri' },\n
    { key: 'sat', short: 'Sat' },\n
    { key: 'sun', short: 'Sun' },\n
];\n
\n
/** "90 min" / "4 h" / "3 d" — so minutes never need mental arithmetic. */\n
function minutesHuman(raw: string): string {\n
    const m = Number(raw);\n
    if (!Number.isFinite(m) || m <= 0) return '—';\n
    if (m < 60) return `${m} min`;\n
    if (m < 24 * 60) {\n
        const h = m / 60;\n
        return `${Number.isInteger(h) ? h : h.toFixed(1)} h`;\n
    }\n
    const d = m / (24 * 60);\n
    return `${Number.isInteger(d) ? d : d.toFixed(1)} d`;\n
}\n
\n
type SlaFormData = Record<\n
    string,\n
    { first_response_minutes: string; resolution_minutes: string }\n
>;\n
\n
export function SlaPolicyDialog({\n
    policies,\n
    calendar,\n
    onClose,\n
}: {\n
    policies: SlaPolicyGrid;\n
    calendar?: SlaCalendar | null;\n
    onClose: () => void;\n
}) {\n
    const wizard = useWizard(SLA_STEPS.length);\n
    const [done, setDone] = useState(false);\n
    const [cal, setCal] = useState({\n
        enabled: calendar?.enabled ?? false,\n
        open_time: calendar?.open_time ?? '08:00',\n
        close_time: calendar?.close_time ?? '17:00',\n
        working_days: calendar?.working_days ?? [\n
            'mon',\n
            'tue',\n
            'wed',\n
            'thu',\n
            'fri',\n
        ],\n
        holiday_dates: calendar?.holiday_dates ?? [],\n
    });\n
    const [holidayDraft, setHolidayDraft] = useState('');\n
\n
    const form = useForm<SlaFormData>(\n
        Object.fromEntries(\n
            Object.keys(SLA_DEFAULTS).map((priority) => [\n
                priority,\n
                {\n
                    first_response_minutes: String(\n
                        policies[priority]?.first_response_minutes ??\n
                            SLA_DEFAULTS[priority].first_response_minutes,\n
                    ),\n
                    resolution_minutes: String(\n
                        policies[priority]?.resolution_minutes ??\n
                            SLA_DEFAULTS[priority].resolution_minutes,\n
                    ),\n
                },\n
            ]),\n
        ),\n
    );\n
\n
    // Nested error keys ("urgent.resolution_minutes") aren't in the typed map.\n
    const errors = form.errors as Record<string, string | undefined>;\n
\n
    const set = (\n
        priority: string,\n
        field: 'first_response_minutes' | 'resolution_minutes',\n
        value: string,\n
    ) => form.setData(priority, { ...form.data[priority], [field]: value });\n
\n
    const toggleDay = (key: string) =>\n
        setCal((c) => ({\n
            ...c,\n
            working_days: c.working_days.includes(key)\n
                ? c.working_days.filter((d) => d !== key)\n
                : [...c.working_days, key],\n
        }));\n
    const addHoliday = () => {\n
        if (holidayDraft && !cal.holiday_dates.includes(holidayDraft)) {\n
            setCal((c) => ({\n
                ...c,\n
                holiday_dates: [...c.holiday_dates, holidayDraft].sort(),\n
            }));\n
        }\n
        setHolidayDraft('');\n
    };\n
    const removeHoliday = (d: string) =>\n
        setCal((c) => ({\n
            ...c,\n
            holiday_dates: c.holiday_dates.filter((x) => x !== d),\n
        }));\n
\n
    const submit = () => {\n
        form.transform((data) => ({\n
            ...data,\n
            business_hours_enabled: cal.enabled,\n
            open_time: cal.open_time,\n
            close_time: cal.close_time,\n
            working_days: cal.working_days,\n
            holiday_dates: cal.holiday_dates,\n
        }));\n
        form.put('/it/sla-policies', {\n
            preserveScroll: true,\n
            onSuccess: (page) => {\n
                const err = pageFlashError(page);\n
                if (err) {\n
                    toast.error(err);\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
                setDone(true);\n
                toast.success('SLA targets updated.');\n
            },\n
        });\n
    };\n
\n
    return (\n
        <WizardShell\n
            open\n
            onClose={onClose}\n
            title="SLA targets"\n
            description="The clock every ticket is stamped with, per priority."\n
            railIcon={Timer}\n
            railTitle="SLA targets"\n
            railSub="IT helpdesk"\n
            steps={SLA_STEPS}\n
            stepIndex={wizard.index}\n
            onStepClick={wizard.goTo}\n
            pct={wizard.progress}\n
            success={\n
                done ? (\n
                    <WizardSuccessPane\n
                        title="Targets saved"\n
                        blurb="New tickets and re-triaged priorities use these immediately. Existing tickets keep the targets they were promised."\n
                        actions={<Button onClick={onClose}>Done</Button>}\n
                    />\n
                ) : undefined\n
            }\n
            footerStart={\n
                wizard.isFirst ? (\n
                    <Button\n
                        variant="ghost"\n
                        onClick={() =>\n
                            form.setData(structuredClone(SLA_DEFAULTS))\n
                        }\n
                        disabled={form.processing}\n
                    >\n
                        <RotateCcw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> Reset to defaults\n
                    </Button>\n
                ) : (\n
                    <Button variant="outline" onClick={wizard.back}>\n
                        Back\n
                    </Button>\n
                )\n
            }\n
            footerEnd={\n
                <>\n
                    <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>\n
                        Cancel\n
                    </Button>\n
                    {wizard.isLast ? (\n
                        <Button onClick={submit} disabled={form.processing}>\n
                            {form.processing ? 'Saving…' : 'Save targets'}\n
                        </Button>\n
                    ) : (\n
                        <Button onClick={wizard.next}>Next</Button>\n
                    )}\n
                \n
            }\n
        >\n
            {wizard.index === 0 && (\n
                <WizardStepPane>\n
                    <StepHead\n
                        icon={Timer}\n
                        title="Response & resolution targets"\n
                        blurb="Targets count from creation. First response stops at the first public agent reply. Resolution pauses during recorded requester, vendor, approver, team, change or other waits."\n
                    />\n
                    <div className="grid gap-3">\n
                        {Object.keys(SLA_DEFAULTS).map((priority) => (\n
                            <div\n
                                key={priority}\n
                                className="rounded-xl border border-border bg-muted/30 p-3.5"\n
                            >\n
                                <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2">\n
                                    <div>\n
                                        <div className="text-[13px] font-bold capitalize">\n
                                            {priority}\n
                                        </div>\n
                                        <div className="text-[11.5px] text-muted-foreground">\n
                                            {SLA_PRIORITY_HINTS[priority]}\n
                                        </div>\n
                                    </div>\n
                                    {policies[priority]?.is_custom ? (\n
                                        <StatusBadge variant="info" size="sm">\n
                                            Custom\n
                                        </StatusBadge>\n
                                    ) : (\n
                                        <StatusBadge\n
                                            variant="neutral"\n
                                            size="sm"\n
                                        >\n
                                            Default\n
                                        </StatusBadge>\n
                                    )}\n
                                </div>\n
                                <div className="mt-2.5 grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">\n
                                    <Field\n
                                        label="First response (minutes)"\n
                                        error={\n
                                            errors[\n
                                                `${priority}.first_response_minutes`\n
                                            ]\n
                                        }\n
                                    >\n
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n
                                            <Input\n
                                                type="number"\n
                                                min={5}\n
                                                value={\n
                                                    form.data[priority]\n
                                                        .first_response_minutes\n
                                                }\n
                                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                                    set(\n
                                                        priority,\n
                                                        'first_response_minutes',\n
                                                        e.target.value,\n
                                                    )\n
                                                }\n
                                                aria-label={`${priority} first response minutes`}\n
                                            />\n
                                            <span className="text-[11.5px] whitespace-nowrap text-muted-foreground">\n
                                                ={' '}\n
                                                {minutesHuman(\n
                                                    form.data[priority]\n
                                                        .first_response_minutes,\n
                                                )}\n
                                            </span>\n
                                        </div>\n
                                    </Field>\n
                                    <Field\n
                                        label="Resolution (minutes)"\n
                                        error={\n
                                            errors[\n
                                                `${priority}.resolution_minutes`\n
                                            ]\n
                                        }\n
                                    >\n
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n
                                            <Input\n
                                                type="number"\n
                                                min={5}\n
                                                value={\n
                                                    form.data[priority]\n
                                                        .resolution_minutes\n
                                                }\n
                                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                                    set(\n
                                                        priority,\n
                                                        'resolution_minutes',\n
                                                        e.target.value,\n
                                                    )\n
                                                }\n
                                                aria-label={`${priority} resolution minutes`}\n
                                            />\n
                                            <span className="text-[11.5px] whitespace-nowrap text-muted-foreground">\n
                                                ={' '}\n
                                                {minutesHuman(\n
                                                    form.data[priority]\n
                                                        .resolution_minutes,\n
                                                )}\n
                                            </span>\n
                                        </div>\n
                                    </Field>\n
                                </div>\n
                            </div>\n
                        ))}\n
                        <InfoCard icon={Timer}>\n
                            {cal.enabled\n
                                ? 'Clocks run on the business-hours calendar set in the next step.'\n
                                : 'Clocks run 24/7 — set a business-hours calendar in the next step to pause them overnight and at weekends.'}{' '}\n
                            Changing targets never rewrites tickets already on\n
                            the queue.\n
                        </InfoCard>\n
                    </div>\n
                </WizardStepPane>\n
            )}\n
\n
            {wizard.index === 1 && (\n
                <WizardStepPane>\n
                    <StepHead\n
                        icon={CalendarClock}\n
                        title="Business hours"\n
                        blurb="When enabled, SLA clocks only tick during these hours — a Friday-evening ticket is due the next working morning, not over the weekend."\n
                    />\n
                    <div className="grid gap-3.5">\n
                        <label className="flex items-center gap-2 text-[13px] font-medium">\n
                            <Checkbox\n
                                checked={cal.enabled}\n
                                onCheckedChange={(v) =>\n
                                    setCal((c) => ({\n
                                        ...c,\n
                                        enabled: v === true,\n
                                    }))\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            Run SLA clocks on a business-hours calendar\n
                        </label>\n
\n
                        {cal.enabled ? (\n
                            <>\n
                                <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2">\n
                                    <Field\n
                                        label="Opens"\n
                                        error={errors.open_time}\n
                                    >\n
                                        <Input\n
                                            type="time"\n
                                            value={cal.open_time}\n
                                            onChange={(e) =>\n
                                                setCal((c) => ({\n
                                                    ...c,\n
                                                    open_time: e.target.value,\n
                                                }))\n
                                            }\n
                                            aria-label="Business hours open time"\n
                                        />\n
                                    </Field>\n
                                    <Field\n
                                        label="Closes"\n
                                        error={errors.close_time}\n
                                    >\n
                                        <Input\n
                                            type="time"\n
                                            value={cal.close_time}\n
                                            onChange={(e) =>\n
                                                setCal((c) => ({\n
                                                    ...c,\n
                                                    close_time: e.target.value,\n
                                                }))\n
                                            }\n
                                            aria-label="Business hours close time"\n
                                        />\n
                                    </Field>\n
                                </div>\n
\n
                                <Field\n
                                    label="Working days"\n
                                    error={errors.working_days}\n
                                >\n
                                    <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">\n
                                        {SLA_WORKING_DAYS.map((d) => {\n
                                            const on =\n
                                                cal.working_days.includes(\n
                                                    d.key,\n
                                                );\n
                                            return (\n
                                                // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax -- selector chip: custom selected fill + aria-pressed, not a <Button>\n
                                                <button\n
                                                    key={d.key}\n
                                                    type="button"\n
                                                    aria-pressed={on}\n
                                                    onClick={() =>\n
                                                        toggleDay(d.key)\n
                                                    }\n
                                                    className={`rounded-lg border px-2.5 py-1 text-[12px] font-semibold transition focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring focus-visible:outline-none ${\n
                                                        on\n
                                                            ? 'border-primary bg-primary text-primary-foreground'\n
                                                            : 'border-border bg-transparent text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted'\n
                                                    }`}\n
                                                >\n
                                                    {d.short}\n
                                                </button>\n
                                            );\n
                                        })}\n
                                    </div>\n
                                </Field>\n
\n
                                <Field label="Public holidays (optional)">\n
                                    <div className="grid gap-2">\n
                                        {cal.holiday_dates.length > 0 && (\n
