                                            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">\n
                                                {cal.holiday_dates.map((d) => (\n
                                                    <span\n
                                                        key={d}\n
                                                        className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-border bg-muted/40 px-2 py-1 text-[12px]"\n
                                                    >\n
                                                        {d}\n
                                                        {/* eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax -- tiny inline remove-chip control */}\n
                                                        <button\n
                                                            type="button"\n
                                                            onClick={() =>\n
                                                                removeHoliday(d)\n
                                                            }\n
                                                            aria-label={`Remove holiday ${d}`}\n
                                                            className="text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"\n
                                                        >\n
                                                            <X className="h-3 w-3" />\n
                                                        </button>\n
                                                    </span>\n
                                                ))}\n
                                            </div>\n
                                        )}\n
                                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">\n
                                            <Input\n
                                                type="date"\n
                                                value={holidayDraft}\n
                                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                                    setHolidayDraft(\n
                                                        e.target.value,\n
                                                    )\n
                                                }\n
                                                aria-label="Add a public holiday"\n
                                            />\n
                                            <Button\n
                                                variant="outline"\n
                                                onClick={addHoliday}\n
                                                disabled={!holidayDraft}\n
                                            >\n
                                                Add\n
                                            </Button>\n
                                        </div>\n
                                    </div>\n
                                </Field>\n
\n
                                <InfoCard icon={CalendarClock}>\n
                                    These hours apply to every priority.\n
                                    Weekends and listed holidays don’t count\n
                                    against any SLA clock.\n
                                </InfoCard>\n
                            \n
                        ) : (\n
                            <InfoCard icon={Timer}>\n
                                Clocks run 24/7 — every minute counts, including\n
                                nights and weekends. Enable a calendar above to\n
                                pause them outside working hours.\n
                            </InfoCard>\n
                        )}\n
                    </div>\n
                </WizardStepPane>\n
            )}\n
        </WizardShell>\n
    );\n
}\n
\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
/*  Merge ticket (agent — fold a duplicate into a survivor, §P-S2)     */\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
\n
export { MergeTicketDialog, type MergeTarget } from './merge-ticket-dialog';\n
\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
/*  KB article (agent, 4 steps: Basics → Audience → Content → Review) */\n
/* ================================================================== */\n
\n
const KB_STEPS: readonly WizardStep[] = [\n
    {\n
        key: 'basics',\n
        label: 'Basics',\n
        blurb: 'Title & category',\n
        icon: FileText,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'audience',\n
        label: 'Ownership',\n
        blurb: 'Audience & review',\n
        icon: Users,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'content',\n
        label: 'Content',\n
        blurb: 'Write & preview',\n
        icon: BookOpen,\n
    },\n
    {\n
        key: 'review',\n
        label: 'Review',\n
        blurb: 'Confirm & save',\n
        icon: ClipboardCheck,\n
    },\n
];\n
\n
/** A safe, block-level markdown preview: `#`/`##`/`###` headings, `-`/`*`\n
 *  bullets, blank-line paragraphs. No inline HTML / no dangerouslySetInnerHTML. */\n
export function KbPreview({ body }: { body: string }) {\n
    return (\n
        <div className="space-y-1.5 text-[13px] leading-relaxed">\n
            {body.split('\n').map((raw, i) => {\n
                const line = raw.trim();\n
                if (line === '')\n
                    return <div key={i} className="h-1.5" aria-hidden />;\n
                if (line.startsWith('### '))\n
                    return (\n
                        <div key={i} className="text-[13px] font-bold">\n
                            {line.slice(4)}\n
                        </div>\n
                    );\n
                if (line.startsWith('## '))\n
                    return (\n
                        <div key={i} className="text-[14px] font-bold">\n
                            {line.slice(3)}\n
                        </div>\n
                    );\n
                if (line.startsWith('# '))\n
                    return (\n
                        <div key={i} className="text-[15px] font-bold">\n
                            {line.slice(2)}\n
                        </div>\n
                    );\n
                if (/^[-*]\s/.test(line)) {\n
                    return (\n
                        <div key={i} className="flex gap-2">\n
                            <span aria-hidden className="text-muted-foreground">\n
                                •\n
                            </span>\n
                            <span>{line.replace(/^[-*]\s/, '')}</span>\n
                        </div>\n
                    );\n
                }\n
                return <p key={i}>{line}</p>;\n
            })}\n
        </div>\n
    );\n
}\n
\n
function KbArticleDialog({\n
    article,\n
    draft,\n
    options,\n
    onClose,\n
}: {\n
    article?: KbRow;\n
    draft?: KbDraft;\n
    options: KbOptions;\n
    onClose: () => void;\n
}) {\n
    const editing = Boolean(article);\n
    const wizard = useWizard(KB_STEPS.length);\n
    const [done, setDone] = useState(false);\n
\n
    // A `draft` (e.g. from a resolution note) pre-fills a NEW article — no\n
    // article means we still create, we just start with content in place.\n
    const form = useForm({\n
        title: article?.title ?? draft?.title ?? '',\n
        category: article?.category ?? draft?.category ?? 'hardware',\n
        audience: article?.audience ?? 'all_staff',\n
        site_scope: article?.site_scope ?? ([] as number[]),\n
        owner_user_id: String(article?.owner_user_id ?? ''),\n
        related_service_id: String(article?.related_service_id ?? ''),\n
        review_due_at: article?.review_due_at ?? '',\n
        body: article?.body ?? draft?.body ?? '',\n
    });\n
\n
    const basicsValid = form.data.title.trim().length > 0;\n
    const contentValid = form.data.body.trim().length > 0;\n
    const savedState = editing\n
        ? {\n
              title: 'Article updated',\n
              blurb: ' with its lifecycle state unchanged',\n
              action: 'Save changes',\n
          }\n
        : {\n
              title: 'Draft saved',\n
              blurb: ' as a draft, ready to send for review',\n
              action: 'Save draft',\n
          };\n
\n
    const afterSave = (addAnother: boolean) => {\n
        toast.success(editing ? 'Article updated.' : `${savedState.title}.`);\n
        if (addAnother && !editing) {\n
            form.reset();\n
            wizard.goTo(0);\n
        } else {\n
            setDone(true);\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    const submit = (addAnother = false) => {\n
        if (editing) {\n
            form.patch(`/it/kb/${article!.id}`, {\n
                preserveScroll: true,\n
                onSuccess: (page) => {\n
                    const err = pageFlashError(page);\n
                    if (err) {\n
                        toast.error(err);\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
                    afterSave(addAnother);\n
                },\n
            });\n
        } else {\n
            form.post('/it/kb', {\n
                preserveScroll: true,\n
                onSuccess: (page) => {\n
                    const err = pageFlashError(page);\n
                    if (err) {\n
                        toast.error(err);\n
                        return;\n
                    }\n
                    afterSave(addAnother);\n
                },\n
            });\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    return (\n
        <WizardShell\n
            open\n
            onClose={onClose}\n
            title={editing ? 'Edit article' : 'New KB article'}\n
            description="Write it once, deflect the ticket every time after."\n
            railIcon={BookOpen}\n
            railTitle="Knowledge"\n
            railSub="IT helpdesk"\n
            steps={KB_STEPS}\n
            stepIndex={wizard.index}\n
            onStepClick={wizard.goTo}\n
            pct={wizard.progress}\n
            success={\n
                done ? (\n
                    <WizardSuccessPane\n
                        title={editing ? 'Article saved' : savedState.title}\n
                        blurb={\n
                            <>\n
                                “{form.data.title}” is in the knowledge base\n
                                {savedState.blurb}.\n
                            \n
                        }\n
                        actions={<Button onClick={onClose}>Done</Button>}\n
                    />\n
                ) : undefined\n
            }\n
            footerStart={\n
                wizard.isFirst ? null : (\n
                    <Button variant="outline" onClick={wizard.back}>\n
                        Back\n
                    </Button>\n
                )\n
            }\n
            footerEnd={\n
                <>\n
                    <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>\n
                        Cancel\n
                    </Button>\n
                    {wizard.isLast ? (\n
                        <>\n
                            {!editing ? (\n
                                <Button\n
                                    variant="outline"\n
                                    onClick={() => submit(true)}\n
                                    disabled={\n
                                        form.processing ||\n
                                        !basicsValid ||\n
                                        !contentValid\n
                                    }\n
                                >\n
                                    Save & add another\n
                                </Button>\n
                            ) : null}\n
                            <Button\n
                                onClick={() => submit(false)}\n
                                disabled={\n
                                    form.processing ||\n
                                    !basicsValid ||\n
                                    !contentValid\n
                                }\n
                            >\n
                                {form.processing\n
                                    ? 'Saving…'\n
                                    : savedState.action}\n
                            </Button>\n
                        \n
                    ) : (\n
                        <Button\n
                            onClick={wizard.next}\n
                            disabled={\n
                                (wizard.index === 0 && !basicsValid) ||\n
                                (wizard.index === 1 &&\n
                                    form.data.audience === 'specific_sites' &&\n
                                    form.data.site_scope.length === 0) ||\n
                                (wizard.index === 2 && !contentValid)\n
                            }\n
                        >\n
                            Continue\n
                        </Button>\n
                    )}\n
                \n
            }\n
        >\n
            {wizard.index === 0 && (\n
                <WizardStepPane>\n
                    <StepHead\n
                        icon={FileText}\n
                        title="Basics"\n
                        blurb="What’s it about? New articles start as drafts."\n
                    />\n
                    <div className="grid gap-3.5">\n
                        <Field label="Title" required error={form.errors.title}>\n
                            <Input\n
                                value={form.data.title}\n
                                onChange={(e) =>\n
                                    form.setData('title', e.target.value)\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="e.g. Reset your work password"\n
                                maxLength={255}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field label="Category" error={form.errors.category}>\n
                            <TilePicker\n
                                value={form.data.category}\n
                                onChange={(v) => form.setData('category', v)}\n
                                options={[...CATEGORY_OPTIONS]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                    </div>\n
                </WizardStepPane>\n
            )}\n
\n
            {wizard.index === 1 && (\n
                <WizardStepPane>\n
                    <StepHead\n
                        icon={Users}\n
                        title="Ownership & audience"\n
                        blurb="Keep the article accountable and show it only where it applies."\n
                    />\n
                    <div className="grid gap-4 lg:grid-cols-2">\n
                        <Field label="Audience" error={form.errors.audience}>\n
                            <SelectInput\n
                                value={form.data.audience}\n
                                onChange={(value) =>\n
                                    form.setData('audience', value)\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="Choose who can find it"\n
                                options={[\n
                                    { value: 'all_staff', label: 'All staff' },\n
                                    {\n
                                        value: 'specific_sites',\n
                                        label: 'Only selected sites',\n
                                    },\n
                                    {\n
                                        value: 'it_agents',\n
                                        label: 'IT agents only',\n
                                    },\n
                                ]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Article owner"\n
                            error={form.errors.owner_user_id}\n
                        >\n
                            <SelectInput\n
                                value={form.data.owner_user_id || UNASSIGNED}\n
                                onChange={(value) =>\n
                                    form.setData(\n
                                        'owner_user_id',\n
                                        value === UNASSIGNED ? '' : value,\n
                                    )\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="Choose an owner"\n
                                options={[\n
                                    {\n
                                        value: UNASSIGNED,\n
                                        label: 'Use me as owner',\n
                                    },\n
                                    ...options.owners.map((owner) => ({\n
                                        value: String(owner.id),\n
                                        label: owner.name,\n
                                    })),\n
                                ]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Related service"\n
                            error={form.errors.related_service_id}\n
                        >\n
                            <SelectInput\n
                                value={\n
                                    form.data.related_service_id || UNASSIGNED\n
                                }\n
                                onChange={(value) =>\n
                                    form.setData(\n
                                        'related_service_id',\n
                                        value === UNASSIGNED ? '' : value,\n
                                    )\n
                                }\n
                                placeholder="Choose a service"\n
                                options={[\n
                                    {\n
                                        value: UNASSIGNED,\n
                                        label: 'No related service',\n
                                    },\n
                                    ...options.services.map((service) => ({\n
                                        value: String(service.id),\n
                                        label: service.name,\n
                                    })),\n
                                ]}\n
                            />\n
                        </Field>\n
                        <Field\n
                            label="Review due"\n

                                            </dt>\n
                                            <dd className="text-xs text-foreground">\n
                                                {formatContextValue(value)}\n
                                            </dd>\n
                                        </div>\n
                                    ),\n
                                )}\n
                            </dl>\n
                        </div>\n
                    ) : null}\n
                </div>\n
            </WizardStepPane>\n
        </WizardShell>\n

}\n
\n
function formatContextValue(value: unknown): string {\n
    if (value == null || value === '') return '—';\n
    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(formatContextValue).join(', ');\n
    if (typeof value !== 'object') return String(value);\n
\n
    const record = value as Record<string, unknown>;\n
    if (typeof record.name === 'string') return record.name;\n
    if ('from' in record || 'to' in record) {\n
        return `${formatContextValue(record.from)} → ${formatContextValue(record.to)}`;\n
    }\n
\n
    return Object.entries(record)\n
        .map(\n
            ([key, nested]) =>\n
                `${key.replaceAll('_', ' ')}: ${formatContextValue(nested)}`,\n
        )\n
        .join(' · ');\n
}\n

const FAIL_STEPS: readonly WizardStep[] = [\n

const ASSIGN_STEPS: readonly WizardStep[] = [\n
    {\n
        key: 'assign',\n
        label: 'Pick an owner',\n
        blurb: 'Who works this?',\n
        icon: User,\n
    },\n
];\n
\n
function initials(name: string): string {\n
    return name\n
        .split(/\s+/)\n
        .filter(Boolean)\n
        .slice(0, 2)\n
        .map((p) => p[0]?.toUpperCase() ?? '')\n
        .join('');\n
}\n
\n
function AssignDialog({\n
    heading,\n
    subject,\n
    currentId,\n
    endpoint,\n
    assignees,\n
    onClose,\n
}: {\n
    heading: string;\n
    subject: string;\n
    currentId: number | null;\n
    endpoint: {\n
        method: 'post' | 'patch';\n
        url: string;\n
        field: string;\n
        ticketId?: number;\n
        expectedVersion?: number;\n
    };\n
    assignees: AssigneeOption[];\n
    onClose: () => void;\n
}) {\n
    const [done, setDone] = useState(false);\n
    const [search, setSearch] = useState('');\n
    const [expectedVersion, setExpectedVersion] = useState(\n
        endpoint.expectedVersion,\n
    );\n
\n
    const form = useForm({\n
        [endpoint.field]: currentId != null ? String(currentId) : '',\n
        routing_reason: '',\n
    } as Record<string, string>);\n
\n
    const pickedId = form.data[endpoint.field];\n
    const picked = assignees.find((a) => String(a.id) === pickedId) ?? null;\n
\n
    const filtered = useMemo(() => {\n
        const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();\n
        if (!q) return assignees;\n
        return assignees.filter((a) => a.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));\n
    }, [search, assignees]);\n
\n
    const submit = () => {\n
        form.transform((data) => ({\n
            [endpoint.field]: Number(data[endpoint.field]),\n
            ...(endpoint.ticketId !== undefined\n
                ? {\n
                      expected_version: expectedVersion,\n
                      routing_reason: data.routing_reason,\n
                  }\n
                : {}),\n
        }));\n
        const opts = {\n
            preserveScroll: true,\n
            onSuccess: (page: { props: Record<string, unknown> }) => {\n
                const err = pageFlashError(page);\n
                if (err) {\n
                    toast.error(err);\n
                    return;\n
                }\n
                setDone(true);\n
            },\n
        };\n
        if (endpoint.method === 'patch') {\n
            form.patch(endpoint.url, opts);\n
        } else {\n
            form.post(endpoint.url, opts);\n
        }\n
    };\n
\n
    return (\n
        <WizardShell\n
            open\n
            onClose={onClose}\n
            title={heading}\n
            description={`Pick who owns “${subject}”.`}\n
            railIcon={UserCheck}\n
            railTitle={heading}\n
            railSub="IT & Support"\n
            steps={ASSIGN_STEPS}\n
            stepIndex={0}\n
            onStepClick={() => undefined}\n
            maxHeight="min(78vh, 600px)"\n
            success={\n
                done ? (\n
                    <WizardSuccessPane\n
                        title="Owner assigned"\n
                        blurb={\n
                            <>\n
                                “{subject}” is now with{' '}\n
                                {picked?.name ?? 'the new owner'}.\n
                            \n
                        }\n
                        actions={<Button onClick={onClose}>Done</Button>}\n
                    />\n
                ) : undefined\n
            }\n
            footerEnd={\n
                <>\n
                    <Button variant="ghost" onClick={onClose}>\n
                        Cancel\n
                    </Button>\n
                    <Button\n
                        onClick={submit}\n
                        disabled={\n
                            form.processing ||\n
                            !picked ||\n
                            (endpoint.ticketId !== undefined &&\n
                                !form.data.routing_reason.trim()) ||\n
                            !!form.errors.expected_version\n
                        }\n
                    >\n
                        {form.processing ? 'Assigning…' : 'Assign'}\n
                    </Button>\n
                \n
            }\n
        >\n
            <WizardStepPane>\n
                <StepHead\n
                    icon={User}\n
                    title="Who works this?"\n
                    blurb={`Pick the person taking ownership of “${subject}”.`}\n
                />\n
                <TicketVersionConflict\n
                    error={form.errors.expected_version}\n
                    ticketId={endpoint.ticketId ?? null}\n
                    onReviewed={(version) => {\n
                        setExpectedVersion(version);\n
                        form.clearErrors('expected_version');\n
                    }}\n
                />\n
                {endpoint.ticketId !== undefined && (\n
                    <Field\n
                        label="Reason for assignment"\n
                        required\n
                        error={form.errors.routing_reason}\n
                    >\n
                        <Textarea\n
                            value={form.data.routing_reason}\n
                            onChange={(event) =>\n
                                form.setData(\n
                                    'routing_reason',\n
                                    event.target.value,\n
                                )\n
                            }\n
                            rows={3}\n
                            maxLength={1000}\n
                            placeholder="Explain why this technician should take the work."\n
                        />\n
                    </Field>\n
                )}\n
                <FieldErr>{form.errors[endpoint.field]}</FieldErr>\n
                <div className="relative mb-3">\n
                    <Search className="pointer-events-none absolute top-1/2 left-2.5 h-4 w-4 -translate-y-1/2 text-muted-foreground" />\n
                    <Input\n
                        value={search}\n
                        onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}\n
                        placeholder="Search staff by name…"\n
                        className="pl-8"\n
                    />\n
                </div>\n
                <div className="flex max-h-72 flex-col gap-1.5 overflow-y-auto">\n
                    {filtered.map((a) => {\n
                        const active = String(a.id) === pickedId;\n
                        return (\n
                            // eslint-disable-next-line no-restricted-syntax -- selector card (avatar + name + check), not a Button case\n
                            <button\n
                                key={a.id}\n
                                type="button"\n
                                onClick={() =>\n
                                    form.setData(endpoint.field, String(a.id))\n
                                }\n
                                className={`flex items-center gap-3 rounded-xl border bg-card px-3 py-2.5 text-left transition-colors ${active ? 'border-primary bg-primary/5' : 'border-border hover:border-primary/50'}`}\n
                            >\n
                                <span className="grid h-9 w-9 flex-none place-items-center rounded-full bg-primary/10 text-[12.5px] font-bold text-primary">\n
                                    {initials(a.name)}\n
                                </span>\n
                                <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 truncate text-[13.5px] font-bold">\n
                                    {a.name}\n
                                </span>\n
                                {active ? (\n
                                    <CheckCircle2 className="h-5 w-5 shrink-0 text-primary" />\n
                                ) : null}\n
                            </button>\n
                        );\n
                    })}\n
                    {filtered.length === 0 ? (\n
                        <div className="py-6 text-center text-[13px] text-muted-foreground">\n
                            No staff match “{search}”.\n
                        </div>\n
                    ) : null}\n
                </div>\n
            </WizardStepPane>\n
        </WizardShell>\n
    );\n
}\n

import { summarizeItWorkTasks } from '@/hooks/it-work-task-lifecycle';\n
import {\n
    purgeItWorkTaskMemory,\n
    useItWorkTaskMemoryNotices,\n
} from '@/hooks/use-it-ticket-draft-memory';\n
import { pendingItWorkTaskCommands } from '@/hooks/use-it-work-task-command';\n
import { formatDateTime } from '@/lib/datetime';\n
import {\n
    Ban,\n
    CalendarClock,\n
    CheckCircle2,\n
    CircleAlert,\n
    ClipboardCheck,\n
    Clock3,\n
    ListChecks,\n
    ListOrdered,\n
    Pencil,\n
    PlayCircle,\n
    Plus,\n
    RotateCcw,\n
    UserRound,\n
    UsersRound,\n
} from 'lucide-react';\n
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';\n
import { TicketWorkTaskActionDialog } from './ticket-work-task-action-dialog';\n
import {\n
    TicketWorkTaskHistory,\n
    TicketWorkTaskReadiness,\n
} from './ticket-work-task-history';\n
import { TicketWorkTaskWizard } from './ticket-work-task-wizard';\n
\n
export type TicketWorkTask = ItWorkTaskRecord;\n
interface Option {\n
    id: number;\n
    name: string;\n
}\n
interface Props {\n
    actorId: number;\n
    ticketId: number;\n
    version: number;\n
    tasks: TicketWorkTask[];\n
    /** A redacted task array is not an empty work register. */\n
    canViewWork: boolean;\n
    canManage: boolean;\n
    taskWork?: {\n
        storage_ready: boolean;\n
        can_create: boolean;\n
        can_reorder: boolean;\n
    };\n
    assignees: Option[];\n
    teams: Option[];\n
    approvals?: { id: number; status: string }[];\n
    onCommitted: (result: ItWorkTaskCommitted) => void;\n
    onAccessLost?: () => void;\n
    onSessionExpired?: () => void;\n
}\n
type TaskDialog = {\n
    type: ItWorkTaskOperation;\n
    task: TicketWorkTask | null;\n
    version: number;\n
    intent?: 'cancel' | 'restore';\n
} | null;\n
const presentation = (\n
    status: TicketWorkTask['status'],\n
): { label: string; variant: StatusVariant; icon: typeof Clock3 } => {\n
    switch (status) {\n
        case 'in_progress':\n
            return { label: 'In progress', variant: 'info', icon: PlayCircle };\n
        case 'blocked':\n
            return { label: 'Blocked', variant: 'critical', icon: CircleAlert };\n
        case 'completed':\n
            return {\n
                label: 'Completed',\n
                variant: 'success',\n
                icon: CheckCircle2,\n
            };\n
        case 'cancelled':\n
            return { label: 'Cancelled', variant: 'neutral', icon: Ban };\n
        default:\n
            return { label: 'Pending', variant: 'warning', icon: Clock3 };\n
    }\n
};\n
\n
/** Existing work register; mutations now use actor/version-bound canonical commands. */\n
export function TicketWorkTasks({\n
    actorId,\n
    ticketId,\n
    version,\n
    tasks,\n
    canViewWork,\n
    canManage,\n
    taskWork,\n
    assignees,\n
    teams,\n
    approvals = [],\n
    onCommitted,\n
    onAccessLost,\n
    onSessionExpired,\n
}: Props) {\n
    const [dialog, setDialog] = useState[39m[31m<TaskDialog>[39m[31m(null);\n
    const [accessLost, setAccessLost] = useState(false);\n
    const [revision, setRevision] = useState(0);\n
    const [recoveryMessage, setRecoveryMessage] = useState[39m[31m<string | null>[39m[31m(null);\n
    const previousScope = useRef({ actorId, ticketId });\n
    const notices = useItWorkTaskMemoryNotices(actorId, ticketId);\n
    let pending: ReturnType[39m[31m<typeof pendingItWorkTaskCommands>[39m[31m = [];\n
    let pendingUnavailable = false;\n
    if (canViewWork && !accessLost && Number.isSafeInteger(actorId)) {\n
        try {\n
            pending = pendingItWorkTaskCommands(actorId, ticketId);\n
        } catch {\n
            pendingUnavailable = true;\n
        }\n
    }\n
    useEffect(() => {\n
        if (!canViewWork || accessLost) {\n
            purgeItWorkTaskMemory(actorId, ticketId);\n
            setDialog(null);\n
        }\n
    }, [actorId, ticketId, canViewWork, accessLost]);\n
    useEffect(() => {\n
        const previous = previousScope.current;\n
        if (previous.actorId !== actorId || previous.ticketId !== ticketId) {\n
            purgeItWorkTaskMemory(previous.actorId, previous.ticketId);\n
            setDialog(null);\n
            setAccessLost(false);\n
            previousScope.current = { actorId, ticketId };\n
        }\n
    }, [actorId, ticketId]);\n
    useEffect(() => {\n
        if (!canViewWork || accessLost || (!notices.length && !pending.length))\n
            return;\n
        const warn = (event: BeforeUnloadEvent) => {\n
            event.preventDefault();\n
            event.returnValue = '';\n
        };\n
        window.addEventListener('beforeunload', warn);\n
        return () => window.removeEventListener('beforeunload', warn);\n
    }, [canViewWork, accessLost, notices.length, pending.length, revision]);\n
    if (!canViewWork) return null;\n
    const deny = () => {\n
        purgeItWorkTaskMemory(actorId, ticketId);\n
        setDialog(null);\n
        setAccessLost(true);\n
        onAccessLost?.();\n
    };\n
    if (accessLost)\n
        return (\n
            [39m[31m<div\n
                role="status"\n
                className="rounded-lg border border-border p-4 text-sm"\n
            >[39m[31m\n
                Current access to private task work could not be confirmed. Task\n
                details have been removed. Refresh the ticket before continuing.\n
            [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
        );\n
    const {\n
        completed: complete,\n
        outstanding,\n
        needsReview,\n
        unverified,\n
    } = summarizeItWorkTasks(tasks);\n
    const commandsReady =\n
        canManage &&\n
        Number.isSafeInteger(actorId) &&\n
        actorId > 0 &&\n
        Number.isSafeInteger(version) &&\n
        version > 0;\n
    const open = (\n
        type: ItWorkTaskOperation,\n
        task: TicketWorkTask | null = null,\n
        intent?: 'cancel' | 'restore',\n
    ) => {\n
        if (\n
            commandsReady &&\n
            (type !== 'create' ||\n
                (taskWork?.storage_ready && taskWork.can_create)) &&\n
            (type !== 'reorder' ||\n
                (taskWork?.storage_ready && taskWork.can_reorder))\n
        )\n
            setDialog({ type, task, version, intent });\n
    };\n
    const recover = (operation: ItWorkTaskOperation, taskId: number | null) => {\n
        const task =\n
            taskId === null\n
                ? null\n
                : (tasks.find((item) => item.id === taskId) ?? null);\n
        if (taskId !== null && task === null) {\n
            setRecoveryMessage(\n
                'The task is not in the current authorized register. Refresh the ticket to check current access. The retained proposal and pending command reference have not been discarded.',\n
            );\n
            onAccessLost?.();\n
            return;\n
        }\n
        setRecoveryMessage(null);\n
        setDialog({ type: operation, task, version });\n
    };\n
    const handleCommitted = (result: ItWorkTaskCommitted) => {\n
        setRevision((value) => value + 1);\n
        onCommitted(result);\n
    };\n
    const close = () => {\n
        setDialog(null);\n
        setRevision((value) => value + 1);\n
    };\n
    return (\n
        [39m[31m<section aria-labelledby="ticket-work-tasks" className="space-y-4">[39m[31m\n
            [39m[31m<div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">[39m[31m\n
                [39m[31m<div className="flex items-center gap-2">[39m[31m\n
                    [39m[31m<ListChecks\n
                        aria-hidden="true"\n
                        className="size-4 text-muted-foreground"\n
                    />[39m[31m\n
                    [39m[31m<h2 id="ticket-work-tasks" className="text-section-title">[39m[31m\n
                        Work tasks\n
                    [39m[31m</h2>[39m[31m\n
                [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
                {canManage && (\n
                    [39m[31m<div className="flex gap-2">[39m[31m\n
                        {tasks.length > 1 && (\n
                            [39m[31m<Button\n
                                type="button"\n
                                size="sm"\n
                                variant="outline"\n
                                disabled={\n
                                    !commandsReady ||\n
                                    !taskWork?.storage_ready ||\n
                                    !taskWork.can_reorder\n
                                }\n
                                onClick={() =>[39m[31m open('reorder')}\n
                            >\n
                                [39m[31m<ListOrdered className="size-4" />[39m[31m\n
                                Reorder tasks\n
                            [39m[31m</Button>[39m[31m\n
                        )}\n
                        [39m[31m<Button\n
                            type="button"\n
                            size="sm"\n
                            variant="outline"\n
                            disabled={\n
                                !commandsReady ||\n
                                !taskWork?.storage_ready ||\n
                                !taskWork.can_create\n
                            }\n
                            onClick={() =>[39m[31m open('create')}\n
                        >\n
                            [39m[31m<Plus className="size-4" />[39m[31m\n
                            Add task\n
                        [39m[31m</Button>[39m[31m\n
                    [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
                )}\n
            [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
            {pendingUnavailable && (\n
                [39m[31m<p role="alert" className="text-sm text-status-warning">[39m[31m\n
                    Pending command references could not be read. Restore\n
                    browser session storage before changing tasks.\n
                [39m[31m</p>[39m[31m\n
            )}\n
            {canManage && !taskWork?.storage_ready && (\n
                [39m[31m<p role="status" className="text-sm text-muted-foreground">[39m[31m\n
                    Task creation and reordering are unavailable until current\n
                    work capabilities are ready.\n
                [39m[31m</p>[39m[31m\n
            )}\n
            {recoveryMessage && (\n
                [39m[31m<p role="status" className="text-sm text-status-warning">[39m[31m\n
                    {recoveryMessage}\n
                [39m[31m</p>[39m[31m\n
            )}\n
            {(notices.length > 0 || pending.length > 0) && (\n
                [39m[31m<div className="space-y-2 rounded-lg border border-border bg-muted/20 p-3">[39m[31m\n
                    [39m[31m<h3 className="text-sm font-semibold">[39m[31m\n
                        Task work to recover\n
                    [39m[31m</h3>[39m[31m\n
                    [39m[31m<p className="text-caption text-muted-foreground">[39m[31m\n
                        Entered details remain concealed until current access is\n
                        checked. A pending command must be checked or cancelled\n
                        before a new command can replace it.\n
                    [39m[31m</p>[39m[31m\n
                    {notices.map((notice, index) => (\n
                        [39m[31m<Button\n
                            key={notice.bufferId}\n
                            type="button"\n
                            variant="outline"\n
                            onClick={() =>[39m[31m\n
                                recover(\n
                                    notice.context.operation,\n
                                    notice.context.taskId,\n
                                )\n
                            }\n
                        >\n
                            Open retained {notice.context.operation} draft{' '}\n
                            {index + 1}\n
                        [39m[31m</Button>[39m[31m\n
                    ))}\n
                    {pending.map((entry, index) => (\n
                        [39m[31m<Button\n
                            key={`${entry.operation}:${entry.taskId}:${entry.requestUuid}`}\n
                            type="button"\n
                            variant="outline"\n
                            onClick={() =>[39m[31m\n
                                recover(entry.operation, entry.taskId)\n
                            }\n
                        >\n
                            Review pending task command {index + 1}\n
                        [39m[31m</Button>[39m[31m\n
                    ))}\n
                [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
            )}\n
            {tasks.length > 0 && (\n
                [39m[31m<div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">[39m[31m\n
                    [39m[31m<StatusBadge\n
                        variant={complete === tasks.length ? 'success' : 'info'}\n
                        size="sm"\n
                    >[39m[31m\n
                        [39m[31m<ClipboardCheck className="size-3" />[39m[31m\n
                        {complete} of {tasks.length} verified complete\n
                    [39m[31m</StatusBadge>[39m[31m\n
                    {needsReview > 0 && (\n
                        [39m[31m<StatusBadge variant="warning" size="sm">[39m[31m\n
                            {needsReview}{' '}\n
                            {needsReview === 1\n
                                ? 'completion needs'\n
                                : 'completions need'}{' '}\n
                            review\n
                        [39m[31m</StatusBadge>[39m[31m\n
                    )}\n
                    {unverified > 0 && (\n
                        [39m[31m<StatusBadge variant="warning" size="sm">[39m[31m\n
                            {unverified}{' '}\n
                            {unverified === 1 ? 'completion' : 'completions'}{' '}\n
                            unverified\n
                        [39m[31m</StatusBadge>[39m[31m\n
                    )}\n
                    {outstanding > 0 && (\n
                        [39m[31m<StatusBadge variant="warning" size="sm">[39m[31m\n
                            [39m[31m<CircleAlert className="size-3" />[39m[31m\n
                            {outstanding} required outstanding\n
                        [39m[31m</StatusBadge>[39m[31m\n
                    )}\n
                [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
            )}\n
            {tasks.length === 0 ? (\n
                [39m[31m<EmptyState\n
                    icon={ListChecks}\n
                    title="No work tasks yet"\n
                    description="Add concrete work and evidence requirements when they are needed for this ticket."\n
                    variant="compact"\n
                />[39m[31m\n
            ) : (\n
                [39m[31m<ul className="space-y-3">[39m[31m\n
                    {tasks.map((task) => {\n
                        const state = presentation(task.status);\n
                        const Icon = state.icon;\n
                        const prerequisitesBlocked =\n
                            task.readiness?.prerequisites !== 'ready';\n
                        const overdue =\n
                            task.due_at &&\n
                            !['completed', 'cancelled'].includes(task.status) &&\n
                            new Date(task.due_at).getTime() < Date.now();\n
                        return (\n
                            [39m[31m<li key={task.id} id={`task-${task.id}`}>[39m[31m\n
                                [39m[31m<Card className="gap-3 p-4">[39m[31m\n
                                    [39m[31m<div className="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-2">[39m[31m\n
                                        [39m[31m<h3 className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-sm font-semibold break-words">[39m[31m\n

                                        [39m[31m<div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">[39m[31m\n

                                                [39m[31m<Icon className="size-3" />[39m[31m\n
                                                {state.label}\n
                                            [39m[31m</StatusBadge>[39m[31m\n
                                            {task.is_required && (\n
                                                [39m[31m<StatusBadge\n

                                                [39m[31m<StatusBadge\n

                                                >[39m[31m\n
                                                    Evidence required\n
                                                [39m[31m</StatusBadge>[39m[31m\n
                                            )}\n
                                        [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
                                    [39m[31m</div>[39m[31m\n
                                    {task.description && (\n
                                        [39m[31m<p className="text-sm break-words whitespace-pre-wrap">[39m[31m\n
                                            {task.description}\n
                                        [39m[31m</p>[39m[31m\n
                                    )}\n
                                    [39m[31m<div className="text-caption flex flex-wrap gap-x-5 gap-y-2 text-muted-foreground">[39m[31m\n
                                        {task.due_at && (\n
                                            [39m[31m<span\n
                                                className={`flex items-center gap-1.5 ${overdue ? 'text-status-critical' : ''}`}\n

                                                [39m[31m<CalendarClock className="size-3.5" />[39m[31m\n

                                            [39m[31m</span>[39m[31m\n

                                            [39m[31m</span>[39m[31m\n
