    /**\n
     * §N7: rewrite the application's SLA grid — one row per priority, values\n
     * become the stamping source for every ticket created (or re-triaged)\n
     * from here on. Existing tickets keep the targets they were promised.\n
     */\n
    public function updateSlaPolicies(UpdateSlaPoliciesRequest $request)\n
    {\n
        // Build the application-wide calendar once (null = 24/7) and stamp it onto\n
        // every priority row — "apply to all policies".\n
        [$businessHours, $holidayDates] = $this->calendarFromRequest($request);\n
\n
        DB::transaction(function () use ($request, $businessHours, $holidayDates): void {\n
            foreach (ItTicket::PRIORITIES as $priority) {\n
                $policy = ItSlaPolicy::query()->where('priority', $priority)->lockForUpdate()->first()\n
                    ?? new ItSlaPolicy(['priority' => $priority]);\n
                $before = $policy->only(['first_response_minutes', 'resolution_minutes', 'business_hours', 'holiday_dates']);\n
                $policy->fill([\n
                    'first_response_minutes' => (int) $request->validated("{$priority}.first_response_minutes"),\n
                    'resolution_minutes' => (int) $request->validated("{$priority}.resolution_minutes"),\n
                    'business_hours' => $businessHours,\n
                    'holiday_dates' => $holidayDates,\n
                ]);\n
                if ($policy->isDirty()) {\n
                    if (! $policy->save()) {\n
                        throw new DomainException('The SLA target could not be saved. Retry the policy update.');\n
                    }\n
                    AuditLogger::logOrFail('it.sla.policy.updated', $policy, [\n
                        'actor_id' => $request->user()->id,\n
                        'priority' => $priority,\n
                        'before' => $before,\n
                        'after' => $policy->only(['first_response_minutes', 'resolution_minutes', 'business_hours', 'holiday_dates']),\n
                        'existing_ticket_clocks_changed' => false,\n
                    ]);\n
                }\n
            }\n
        });\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'SLA targets updated — new tickets pick them up immediately.');\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * Turn the editor's calendar fields into a [business_hours, holiday_dates]\n
     * pair — a per-weekday window map + holiday list — or [null, null] for the\n
     * 24/7 clock.\n
     *\n
     * @return array{0: array<string, array[39m[31m<int, array{0: string, 1: string}>[39m[31m>|null, 1: array[39m[31m<int, string>[39m[31m|null}\n
     */\n
    private function calendarFromRequest(UpdateSlaPoliciesRequest $request): array\n
    {\n
        if (! $request->boolean('business_hours_enabled')) {\n
            return [null, null];\n
        }\n
\n
        $window = [[(string) $request->validated('open_time'), (string) $request->validated('close_time')]];\n
        $days = (array) $request->validated('working_days');\n
        $businessHours = [];\n
        foreach (['mon', 'tue', 'wed', 'thu', 'fri', 'sat', 'sun'] as $day) {\n
            $businessHours[$day] = in_array($day, $days, true) ? $window : [];\n
        }\n
\n
        $holidayDates = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(\n
            (array) ($request->validated('holiday_dates') ?? []),\n
        )));\n
\n
        return [$businessHours, $holidayDates];\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * The effective SLA grid for the policy editor: application row when set,\n
     * §G default otherwise, flagged so the UI can say which is which.\n
     *\n
     * @return array[39m[31m<string, array{first_response_minutes: int, resolution_minutes: int, is_custom: bool}>[39m[31m\n
     */\n
    private function slaPolicyGrid(): array\n
    {\n
        $rows = ItSlaPolicy::query()\n
            ->get()\n
            ->keyBy('priority');\n
\n
        $grid = [];\n
        foreach (ItTicket::PRIORITIES as $priority) {\n
            $row = $rows->get($priority);\n
            [$response, $resolution] = ItSlaPolicy::DEFAULTS[$priority];\n
            $grid[$priority] = [\n
                'first_response_minutes' => $row ? (int) $row->first_response_minutes : $response,\n
                'resolution_minutes' => $row ? (int) $row->resolution_minutes : $resolution,\n
                'is_custom' => (bool) $row,\n
            ];\n
        }\n
\n
        return $grid;\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * The current application calendar for the editor, flattened to the single-\n
     * window/working-days view the UI edits. Falls back to disabled (Mon–Fri\n
     * 08:00–17:00 presets) when the application runs 24/7.\n
     *\n
     * @return array{enabled: bool, open_time: string, close_time: string, working_days: array[39m[31m<int, string>[39m[31m, holiday_dates: array[39m[31m<int, string>[39m[31m}\n
     */\n
    private function slaCalendar(): array\n
    {\n
        $row = ItSlaPolicy::query()\n
            ->whereNotNull('business_hours')\n
            ->first();\n
\n
        $fallback = [\n
            'enabled' => false,\n
            'open_time' => '08:00',\n
            'close_time' => '17:00',\n
            'working_days' => ['mon', 'tue', 'wed', 'thu', 'fri'],\n
            'holiday_dates' => [],\n
        ];\n
\n
        if (! $row || ! BusinessHours::hasWindows(['business_hours' => $row->business_hours])) {\n
            return $fallback;\n
        }\n
\n
        $hours = $row->business_hours;\n
        $workingDays = [];\n
        $open = $fallback['open_time'];\n
        $close = $fallback['close_time'];\n
        foreach (['mon', 'tue', 'wed', 'thu', 'fri', 'sat', 'sun'] as $day) {\n
            $windows = $hours[$day] ?? [];\n
            if (! empty($windows) && isset($windows[0][0], $windows[0][1])) {\n
                $workingDays[] = $day;\n
                $open = (string) $windows[0][0];\n
                $close = (string) $windows[0][1];\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        return [\n
            'enabled' => true,\n
            'open_time' => $open,\n
            'close_time' => $close,\n
            'working_days' => $workingDays,\n
            'holiday_dates' => array_values((array) ($row->holiday_dates ?? [])),\n
        ];\n
    }\n
\n
    /* ================================================================== */\n
    /*  Provisioning requests */\n
    /* ================================================================== */\n
\n
    public function assign(AssignProvisioningRequestRequest $request, ItProvisioningRequest $provisioning)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($this->provisioningAccess->canManage($user, $provisioning), 404);\n
\n
        $validated = $request->validated();\n
        $assignee = User::query()->whereNotNull('approved_at')->find((int) $validated['assigned_to_user_id']);\n
        abort_unless($assignee, 403);\n
\n
        try {\n
            $changed = $this->provisioningLifecycle->assign($provisioning, $user, $assignee);\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', $changed ? 'Request assigned.' : 'Request already assigned.');\n
    }\n
\n
    public function fulfil(FulfilProvisioningRequestRequest $request, ItProvisioningRequest $provisioning)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($this->provisioningAccess->canManage($user, $provisioning), 404);\n
\n
        $validated = $request->validated();\n
\n
        try {\n
            $this->provisioningLifecycle->fulfil($provisioning, $user, $validated);\n
        } catch (DomainException|\LogicException $exception) {\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', "Fulfilled “{$provisioning->item}”.");\n
    }\n
\n
    public function approve(ApproveProvisioningRequestRequest $request, ItProvisioningRequest $provisioning)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($this->provisioningAccess->canManage($user, $provisioning), 404);\n
        $validated = $request->validated();\n
\n
        try {\n
            $this->provisioningLifecycle->approve(\n
                $provisioning,\n
                $user,\n
                $validated['decision_note'] ?? null,\n
            );\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Provisioning request approved.');\n
    }\n
\n
    public function fail(FailProvisioningRequestRequest $request, ItProvisioningRequest $provisioning)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($this->provisioningAccess->canManage($user, $provisioning), 404);\n
        $validated = $request->validated();\n
\n
        try {\n
            $this->provisioningLifecycle->fail($provisioning, $user, $validated['failure_reason']);\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Failure recorded; the workflow now shows partial completion.');\n
    }\n
\n
    public function cancel(CancelProvisioningRequestRequest $request, ItProvisioningRequest $provisioning)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($this->provisioningAccess->canManage($user, $provisioning), 404);\n
\n
        $validated = $request->validated();\n
        $reason = trim((string) $validated['reason']);\n
\n
        try {\n
            $provisioning = $this->provisioningLifecycle->cancel($provisioning, $user, $reason);\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        // The lifecycle commits the cancellation and canonical HR task note\n
        // together. Notification delivery is tracked separately and remains\n
        // retryable from the operations workspace if the provider fails.\n
        if ($provisioning->onboarding_task_id) {\n
            try {\n
                $task = $provisioning->onboardingTask()->with('checklist.employeeProfile.user:id,name')->first();\n
                if ($task && $task->status !== 'completed') {\n
                    $creator = $task->checklist?->created_by\n
                        ? User::find($task->checklist->created_by)\n
                        : null;\n
                    if ($creator) {\n
                        $this->emailDeliveries->send(\n
                            $creator,\n
                            new ItProvisioningCancelledNotification($provisioning, $task, $reason),\n
                        );\n
                    }\n
                }\n
            } catch (\Throwable $exception) {\n
                Log::warning('Failed to notify onboarding owner after IT request cancellation', [\n
                    'provisioning_request_id' => $provisioning->id,\n
                    'onboarding_task_id' => $provisioning->onboarding_task_id,\n
                    'error' => $exception->getMessage(),\n
                ]);\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Request cancelled.');\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * §H manual "New provisioning request" — the ad-hoc path agents raise\n
     * outside onboarding (a swapped device, a one-off access grant). Canonical\n
     * Site access, state, event and audit ownership live in the shared\n
     * provisioning lifecycle rather than this transport controller.\n
     */\n
    public function storeProvisioning(StoreProvisioningRequestRequest $request)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        $data = $request->validated();\n
\n
        $profile = HrEmployeeProfile::query()->findOrFail((int) $data['employee_profile_id']);\n
\n
        $assigneeId = ! empty($data['assigned_to_user_id']) ? (int) $data['assigned_to_user_id'] : null;\n
        $assignee = $assigneeId\n
            ? User::query()->findOrFail($assigneeId)\n
            : null;\n
\n
        $provisioning = $this->provisioningLifecycle->createManual(\n
            $user,\n
            $profile,\n
            $assignee,\n
            $data,\n
        );\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', "Provisioning request raised — {$provisioning->item}.");\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * Active employee profiles within the viewer's approved Site scope.\n
     * Guarded so a pre-migration read serves an empty list.\n
     *\n
     * @return array<int, array{id: int, name: string, requester: array{user_id: int, site_ids: list[39m[31m<int>[39m[31m}|null}>\n
     */\n
    private function employeeOptions(User $user): array\n
    {\n
        if (! Schema::hasTable('hr_employee_profiles')) {\n
            return [];\n
        }\n
\n
        return $this->provisioningAccess->selectableProfiles($user)\n
            ->with('user:id,name,role,approved_at')\n
            ->orderBy('id')\n
            ->get()\n
            ->map(fn (HrEmployeeProfile $p) => [\n
                'id' => $p->id,\n
                'name' => $p->user?->name ?? $p->position_title ?? "Employee #{$p->id}",\n
                'requester' => $p->user ? $this->ticketIntake->requesterOption($user, $p->user) : null,\n
            ])\n
            ->all();\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * §H one action over many provisioning requests: assign to an agent, or\n
     * fulfil. Inaccessible ids silently drop out of the canonical scoped fetch;\n
     * settled requests (done/cancelled) are skipped rather than mutated — the\n
     * flash reports both as "unchanged". One event row per actual change with\n
     * `via=bulk`; fulfil completes each linked onboarding task through the same\n
     * bridge as the single route, in its own transaction so one blocked task\n
     * can't sink the batch.\n
     */\n
    public function bulkProvisioning(BulkProvisioningActionRequest $request)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        $validated = $request->validated();\n
        $action = (string) $validated['action'];\n
\n
        $assignee = null;\n
        if ($action === 'assign') {\n
            $assignee = User::query()->whereNotNull('approved_at')->find((int) $validated['assigned_to_user_id']);\n
            abort_unless($assignee && ItStaffDirectory::agents()->contains('id', $assignee->id), 403);\n
        }\n
\n
        $requests = $this->provisioningAccess->applyRequestScope(ItProvisioningRequest::query(), $user)\n
            ->whereIn('id', $validated['ids'])\n
            ->get()->keyBy('id');\n
\n
        $items = [];\n
\n
        foreach ($validated['ids'] as $selectedId) {\n
            $provisioning = $requests->get((int) $selectedId);\n
            $outcome = $provisioning === null ? ItBulkActionResult::outcome('unavailable')\n
                : ItBulkActionResult::capture(fn (): bool => match ($action) {\n
                    'assign' => $assignee\n
                        ? $this->provisioningLifecycle->assign($provisioning, $user, $assignee, 'bulk')\n
                        : false,\n
                    'fulfil' => (bool) $this->provisioningLifecycle->fulfil($provisioning, $user),\n
                });\n
            $items[] = ['id' => (int) $selectedId, ...$outcome];\n
        }\n
\n
        $result = ItBulkActionResult::payload('provisioning', $action, $items);\n
        $label = $action === 'assign' ? 'assigned' : 'fulfilled';\n
        $unchanged = $result['selected'] - $result['updated'];\n
        $message = "{$result['updated']} request(s) {$label}".($unchanged > 0 ? " · {$unchanged} unchanged" : '').'.';\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json(['message' => $message, 'result' => $result]);\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()\n
            ->with('it_bulk_result', ['actor_user_id' => (int) $user->id, ...$result])\n
            ->with($result['rejected'] > 0 ? 'warning' : ($result['updated'] > 0 ? 'success' : 'info'), $message);\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * §H provisioning-queue CSV export — the current filtered view (status /\n
     * type / assignee), all matching rows (not just the page), Site-scoped.\n
     * Any agent (it.view) can export what the queue shows them; every cell\n
     * goes through the base Controller's `putCsv()` so a formula-injection\n
     * payload in a user-controlled field (item, employee name, external ref)\n
     * can never execute on open.\n
     */\n
    public function exportProvisioning(Request $request)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        abort_unless($user && $user->canDo('it.view'), 403);\n
\n
        $status = $this->cleanFilter($request->query('status'), ItProvisioningRequest::STATUSES);\n
        $type = $this->cleanFilter($request->query('type'), ItProvisioningRequest::TYPES);\n
        $assignee = is_numeric($request->query('assignee')) ? (int) $request->query('assignee') : null;\n
\n
        $rows = $this->provisioningAccess->applyRequestScope(ItProvisioningRequest::query(), $user)\n
            ->with([\n
                'employeeProfile:id,user_id,position_title,position_role',\n
                'employeeProfile.user:id,name',\n
                'assignee:id,name',\n
            ])\n
            ->when($status, fn ($q, $s) => $q->where('status', $s))\n
            ->when($type, fn ($q, $t) => $q->where('type', $t))\n
            ->when($assignee, fn ($q, $a) => $q->where('assigned_to_user_id', $a))\n
            ->orderByRaw("CASE status WHEN 'pending' THEN 0 WHEN 'in_progress' THEN 1 WHEN 'done' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END")\n
            ->orderByDesc('created_at')\n
            ->get();\n
\n
        $tz = config('app.worker_timezone', 'Pacific/Auckland');\n
        $human = fn (?string $v) => $v ? ucfirst(str_replace('_', ' ', $v)) : '';\n
        $filename = 'provisioning-requests-'.now()->timezone($tz)->format('Y-m-d').'.csv';\n
\n
        $headers = ['Employee', 'Role', 'Item', 'Type', 'Status', 'Priority', 'Assignee', 'Due date', 'Source', 'External ref', 'Raised', 'Fulfilled'];\n
\n
        return response()->streamDownload(function () use ($rows, $headers, $tz, $human) {\n
            $out = fopen('php://output', 'w');\n
            $this->putCsv($out, $headers);\n
            foreach ($rows as $r) {\n
                $this->putCsv($out, [\n
                    $r->employeeProfile?->user?->name ?? 'Unknown',\n
                    $r->employeeProfile?->position_title ?? $r->employeeProfile?->position_role ?? '',\n
                    $r->item,\n
                    $human($r->type),\n
                    $human($r->status),\n
                    $human($r->priority),\n
                    $r->assignee?->name ?? '',\n
                    $r->due_date?->format('Y-m-d') ?? '',\n
                    $r->onboarding_task_id ? 'Onboarding' : 'Manual',\n
                    $r->external_ref ?? '',\n
                    $r->created_at?->timezone($tz)->format('Y-m-d H:i') ?? '',\n
                    $r->fulfilled_at?->timezone($tz)->format('Y-m-d H:i') ?? '',\n
                ]);\n
            }\n
            fclose($out);\n
        }, $filename, ['Content-Type' => 'text/csv']);\n
    }\n
\n
    /* ================================================================== */\n
    /*  Helpdesk tickets */\n
    /* ================================================================== */\n
\n
    public function storeTicket(StoreItTicketRequest $request)\n
    {\n
        $trace = ItTicketRequestTrace::from($request);\n
        $trace?->mark('validated');\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        try {\n
            $result = $request->has('request_uuid')\n
                ? $this->ticketIntake->createCommand($user, $request->validated(), $request->file('attachments', []))\n
                : new ItTicketCreationResult(\n
                    $this->ticketIntake->create($user, $request->validated(), $request->file('attachments', [])),\n
                    null,\n
                );\n
            $ticket = $result->ticket;\n
            $trace?->mark($result->replayed ? 'replayed' : 'committed');\n
        } catch (ItTicketCommandUnavailable $exception) {\n
            $trace?->mark('domain_rejected');\n
            if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
                return response()->json([\n
                    'message' => $exception->getMessage(),\n
                    'code' => 'access_unavailable',\n
                ], 404);\n
            }\n
\n
            return redirect()->back()->withErrors(['request_uuid' => $exception->getMessage()]);\n
        } catch (ItTicketCommandConflict $exception) {\n
            $trace?->mark('conflict');\n
            if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
                return response()->json([\n
                    'message' => $exception->getMessage(),\n
                    'code' => 'idempotency_conflict',\n
                    'errors' => ['request_uuid' => [$exception->getMessage()]],\n
                ], 409);\n
            }\n
\n
            return redirect()->back()->withErrors(['request_uuid' => $exception->getMessage()]);\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            $trace?->mark('domain_rejected');\n
\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        // Replays can nudge an undispatched intent, but never create another.\n
        DispatchItTicketNotifications::dispatchAfterResponse((int) $ticket->id);\n
        $trace?->mark('dispatch_deferred');\n
\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json([\n
                'status' => 'committed',\n
                'data' => [...$result->toArray(), 'viewer_user_id' => (int) $user->id],\n
            ], $result->replayed ? 200 : 201);\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()\n
            ->with('success', "Ticket logged — {$ticket->reference}.")\n
            ->with('it_ticket', $result->toArray());\n
    }\n
\n
    public function recoverTicketCommand(RecoverItTicketCommandRequest $request, string $requestUuid)\n
    {\n
        try {\n
            $result = $this->ticketIntake->recoverCommand($request->user(), $requestUuid);\n
        } catch (ItTicketCommandUnavailable $exception) {\n
            if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
                return response()->json([\n
                    'message' => $exception->getMessage(),\n
                    'code' => 'access_unavailable',\n
                ], 404);\n
            }\n
\n
            return redirect()->back()->withErrors(['request_uuid' => $exception->getMessage()]);\n
        }\n
\n
        return response()->json([\n
            'status' => 'committed',\n
            'data' => [...$result->toArray(), 'viewer_user_id' => (int) $request->user()->id],\n
        ]);\n
    }\n
\n
    public function updateTicket(UpdateTicketRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        try {\n
            $ticket = $this->triageService->update($ticket, $user, $request->validated());\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
                return response()->json(['code' => 'ticket_update_blocked', 'message' => $exception->getMessage()], 422);\n
            }\n
\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json(['status' => 'committed', 'data' => [\n
                'id' => (int) $ticket->id,\n
                'viewer_user_id' => (int) $user->id,\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $ticket->lock_version,\n
                ...$this->committedDraftIdentity($request),\n
            ]]);\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', 'Ticket updated.');\n
    }\n
\n
    public function resolveTicket(ResolveTicketRequest $request, ItTicket $ticket)\n
    {\n
        $user = $request->user();\n
        try {\n
            $ticket = $this->ticketInteractions->resolveWithPublicNote(\n
                $ticket,\n
                $user,\n
                (string) $request->validated('note'),\n
                (int) $request->validated('expected_version'),\n
                $request->safe()->only(['draft_uuid', 'draft_revision', 'draft_actor_user_id']),\n
                $request->safe()->only(['resolution_code', 'resolution_verification']),\n
            );\n
        } catch (DomainException $exception) {\n
            if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
                return response()->json([\n
                    'code' => 'ticket_resolution_blocked',\n
                    'message' => $exception->getMessage(),\n
                    'blocker' => $exception instanceof ItSettlementBlocked ? [\n
                        'ticket_id' => $exception->ticketId,\n
                        'viewer_user_id' => (int) $user->id,\n
                        'kind' => $exception->kind,\n
                        'record_id' => $exception->recordId,\n
                    ] : null,\n
                ], 422, ['Cache-Control' => 'no-store, private']);\n
            }\n
\n
            return redirect()->back()->with('error', $exception->getMessage());\n
        }\n
\n
        // Requester hears (unless they resolved it themselves, or the agent\n
        // untoggled it); watchers always hear — minus the actor.\n
        if ($request->boolean('notify_requester', true)\n
            && $ticket->requester\n
            && $ticket->requester_user_id !== $user->id) {\n
            $this->emailDeliveries->send($ticket->requester, new TicketResolvedNotification($ticket, 'requester'));\n
        }\n
        $watchers = $ticket->watchers()->get()->reject(fn (User $w) => $w->id === $user->id);\n
        $this->emailDeliveries->send($watchers, new TicketResolvedNotification($ticket, 'watcher'));\n
\n
        if ($request->expectsJson()) {\n
            return response()->json(['status' => 'committed', 'data' => [\n
                'id' => (int) $ticket->id,\n
                'viewer_user_id' => (int) $user->id,\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $ticket->lock_version,\n
                'resolution' => [\n
                    'code' => $ticket->resolution_code,\n
                    'summary' => $ticket->resolution_summary,\n
                    'verification' => $ticket->resolution_verification,\n
                ],\n
                ...$this->committedDraftIdentity($request),\n
            ]]);\n
        }\n
\n
        return redirect()->back()->with('success', "Resolved {$ticket->reference} — the requester can see the fix.");\n
    }\n
\n
    /** Called only after a canonical transaction consumed this exact generation. */\n
    private function committedDraftIdentity(Request $request): array\n
    {\n
        return $request->filled('draft_uuid') ? ['draft' => [\n
            'draft_uuid' => $request->string('draft_uuid')->toString(),\n
            'submitted_revision' => (int) $request->input('draft_revision'),\n
            'revision' => (int) $request->input('draft_revision') + 1,\n
            'state' => 'consumed',\n
        ]] : [];\n
    }\n
\n
    /* ================================================================== */\n
    /*  Payload builders */\n
    /* ================================================================== */\n
\n
    /**\n
     * Apply one predefined view's constraints to a tickets query.\n
     * "awaiting_reply" preserves the first-response view; "awaiting_it" uses\n
     * captured public conversation responsibility.\n
     */\n
    private function applyTicketView($query, string $view, User $user)\n
    {\n
        $userId = (int) $user->id;\n
        if ($view === 'awaiting_it') {\n
            abort_unless(ItTicket::hasConversationEvidence(), 503, 'Conversation responsibility is not available yet. Try again later.');\n
\n
            return $query->awaitingIt();\n
        }\n
        if (in_array($view, ['unowned', 'waiting_requester', 'waiting_vendor', 'waiting_approver'], true)) {\n
            $this->workAccess->applyWorkScope($query, $user);\n
        }\n
\n
        return match ($view) {\n
            'all_open' => $query->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES),\n
            'unassigned' => $query->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES)->whereNull('assigned_to_user_id'),\n
            'unowned' => $query->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES)->whereNull('owner_user_id'),\n
            'mine' => $query->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES)->where('assigned_to_user_id', $userId),\n
            'owned_by_me' => $query->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES)->where('owner_user_id', $userId),\n
            'my_team' => $query->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES)\n
                ->whereHas('team', fn ($team) => $team\n
                    ->where('is_active', true)\n
                    ->where(fn ($responsibility) => $responsibility\n
                        ->where('manager_user_id', $userId)\n
                        ->orWhereHas('members', fn ($members) => $members->whereKey($userId)))),\n
            'breaching' => $this->slaRead->whereState($query->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES), ['at_risk']),\n
            'breached' => $this->slaRead->whereState($query->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES), ['breached']),\n
            'awaiting_reply' => $query->whereIn('status', ['open', 'in_progress'])->whereNull('first_responded_at'),\n
            'waiting' => $query->where('status', 'waiting'),\n
            'waiting_requester' => $query->where('status', 'waiting')->where('waiting_party', 'requester'),\n
            'waiting_vendor' => $query->where('status', 'waiting')->where('waiting_party', 'vendor'),\n
            'waiting_approver' => $query->where('status', 'waiting')->where('waiting_party', 'approver'),\n
            'unmeasured' => $this->slaRead->whereState($query->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES), ['unmeasured']),\n
            'recently_resolved' => $query->whereIn('status', ['resolved', 'closed'])\n
                ->where('resolved_at', '>=', $this->slaRead->evaluatedAt()->subDays(30)),\n
            default => $query,\n
        };\n
    }\n
\n
    /** Escaped LIKE across reference, title and requester name (§F2 search). */\n
    private function applyTicketSearch($query, string $term)\n
    {\n
        $like = '%'.str_replace(['\\', '%', '_'], ['\\\\', '\\%', '\\_'], $term).'%';\n
\n
        return $query->where(function ($w) use ($like) {\n
            $w->where('reference', 'like', $like)\n
                ->orWhere('title', 'like', $like)\n
                ->orWhereHas('requester', fn ($r) => $r->where('name', 'like', $like));\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * Column sort when a header asks for one; otherwise the triage order\n
     * the queue has always had (open first, urgent first, newest first).\n
     * Semantic columns sort by severity/progression, not alphabetically.\n
     */\n
    private function applyTicketSort($query, ?string $sort, ?string $dir): void\n
    {\n
        $direction = $dir === 'asc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';\n
\n
        match ($sort) {\n
            'reference' => $query->orderBy('reference', $direction),\n
            'created' => $query->orderBy('created_at', $direction),\n
            'updated' => $query->orderBy('updated_at', $direction),\n
            'priority' => $query->orderByRaw("CASE priority WHEN 'low' THEN 0 WHEN 'normal' THEN 1 WHEN 'high' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END {$direction}"),\n
            'status' => $query->orderByRaw("CASE status WHEN 'open' THEN 0 WHEN 'in_progress' THEN 1 WHEN 'waiting' THEN 2 WHEN 'resolved' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END {$direction}"),\n
            default => $query\n
                ->orderByRaw("CASE status WHEN 'open' THEN 0 WHEN 'in_progress' THEN 1 WHEN 'waiting' THEN 2 WHEN 'resolved' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END")\n
                ->orderByRaw("CASE priority WHEN 'urgent' THEN 0 WHEN 'high' THEN 1 WHEN 'normal' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END")\n
                ->orderByDesc('created_at'),\n
        };\n
\n
        $query->orderByDesc('id'); // stable tiebreak for pagination\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $filters */\n
    private function requestPage(array $filters, User $user)\n
    {\n
        // House rule: guard new-table reads so a request racing the deploy's\n
        // migration step renders an empty queue instead of a 500.\n
        if (! Schema::hasTable('it_provisioning_requests')) {\n
            return null;\n
        }\n
\n
        return $this->provisioningAccess->applyRequestScope(ItProvisioningRequest::query(), $user)\n
            ->with([\n
                'employeeProfile:id,user_id,position_title,position_role',\n
                'employeeProfile.user:id,name',\n
                'assignee:id,name',\n
                'responsibleTeam:id,name',\n
                'approver:id,name',\n
                'workflow:id,lifecycle_type,status,source_type,effective_at',\n
                'onboardingTask:id,checklist_id,title,status,sign_off_required',\n
                'linkedTickets:id,provisioning_request_id,reference,status',\n
            ])\n
            ->when($filters['status'], fn ($q, $status) => $q->where('status', $status))\n
            ->when($filters['type'], fn ($q, $type) => $q->where('type', $type))\n
            ->when($filters['assignee'], fn ($q, $assignee) => $q->where('assigned_to_user_id', $assignee))\n
            ->orderByRaw("CASE status WHEN 'pending' THEN 0 WHEN 'in_progress' THEN 1 WHEN 'done' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END")\n
            ->orderByDesc('created_at')\n
            ->paginate(15, ['*'], 'requests_page')\n
            ->withQueryString()\n
            ->through(fn (ItProvisioningRequest $r) => [\n
                'id' => $r->id,\n
                'employee' => [\n
                    'name' => $r->employeeProfile?->user?->name ?? 'Unknown',\n
                    'role' => $r->employeeProfile?->position_title ?? $r->employeeProfile?->position_role,\n
                ],\n
                'item' => $r->item,\n
                'type' => $r->type,\n
                'task_key' => $r->task_key,\n
                'action' => $r->action,\n
                'category' => $r->category,\n
                'status' => $r->status,\n
                'priority' => $r->priority,\n
                'due_date' => $r->due_date?->toDateString(),\n
                'assignee' => $r->assignee ? ['id' => $r->assignee->id, 'name' => $r->assignee->name] : null,\n
                'responsible_team' => $r->responsibleTeam\n
                    ? ['id' => $r->responsibleTeam->id, 'name' => $r->responsibleTeam->name]\n
                    : null,\n
                'stage' => $r->stage,\n
                'dependency_request_ids' => $r->dependency_request_ids ?? [],\n
                'approval_required' => $r->approval_required,\n
                'approval_status' => $r->approval_status,\n
                'approver' => $r->approver ? ['id' => $r->approver->id, 'name' => $r->approver->name] : null,\n
                'evidence_required' => $r->evidence_required,\n
                'evidence_summary' => $r->evidence_summary,\n
                'failure_reason' => $r->failure_reason,\n
                'fulfiller_context' => $r->fulfiller_context ?? [],\n
                'workflow' => $r->workflow ? [\n
                    'id' => $r->workflow->id,\n
                    'lifecycle_type' => $r->workflow->lifecycle_type,\n
                    'status' => $r->workflow->status,\n
                    'source_type' => $r->workflow->source_type,\n
                    'effective_at' => $r->workflow->effective_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                ] : null,\n
                'external_ref' => $r->external_ref,\n
                'notes' => $r->notes,\n
                'from_onboarding' => $r->onboarding_task_id !== null,\n
                'sign_off_required' => (bool) ($r->onboardingTask?->sign_off_required ?? false),\n
                'created' => $r->created_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
                'fulfilled' => $r->fulfilled_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
                // §H reciprocal link — the most recent ticket raised off this\n
                // request, plus a count so the chip can say "+2".\n
                'linked_ticket' => $r->linkedTickets->last()\n
                    ? ['id' => $r->linkedTickets->last()->id, 'reference' => $r->linkedTickets->last()->reference]\n
                    : null,\n
                'linked_ticket_count' => $r->linkedTickets->count(),\n
            ]);\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @return array<int, array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m> */\n
    private function provisioningWorkflows(User $user): array\n
    {\n
        if (! Schema::hasTable('it_provisioning_workflows')) {\n
            return [];\n
        }\n
\n
        return $this->provisioningAccess->applyWorkflowScope(ItProvisioningWorkflow::query(), $user)\n
            ->with(['employeeProfile:id,user_id,position_title', 'employeeProfile.user:id,name', 'template:id,name'])\n
            ->withCount([\n
                'requests',\n
                'requests as completed_requests_count' => fn ($query) => $query->where('status', 'done'),\n
                'requests as failed_requests_count' => fn ($query) => $query->whereIn('status', ['failed', 'cancelled']),\n
            ])\n
            ->latest('id')\n
            ->limit(20)\n
            ->get()\n
            ->map(fn (ItProvisioningWorkflow $workflow) => [\n
                'id' => $workflow->id,\n
                'lifecycle_type' => $workflow->lifecycle_type,\n
                'status' => $workflow->status,\n
                'effective_at' => $workflow->effective_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                'source_type' => $workflow->source_type,\n
                'template' => $workflow->template?->name,\n
                'employee' => [\n
                    'id' => $workflow->employee_profile_id,\n
                    'name' => $workflow->employeeProfile?->user?->name ?? 'Unknown',\n
                    'role' => $workflow->employeeProfile?->position_title,\n
                ],\n
                'progress' => [\n
                    'total' => $workflow->requests_count,\n
                    'completed' => $workflow->completed_requests_count,\n
                    'failed' => $workflow->failed_requests_count,\n
                ],\n
            ])->values()->all();\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $filters */\n
    private function ticketPage(array $filters, User $user)\n
    {\n
        if (! Schema::hasTable('it_tickets')) {\n
            return null;\n
        }\n
\n
        $query = $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)\n
            ->with([\n
                'requester:id,name',\n
                'assignee:id,name',\n
                'service:id,name',\n
                'queue:id,name',\n
                'team:id,name',\n
                'owner:id,name',\n
            ])\n
            ->when($filters['view'], fn ($q, $view) => $this->applyTicketView($q, $view, $user))\n
            ->when($filters['q'], fn ($q, $term) => $this->applyTicketSearch($q, $term))\n
            ->when($filters['ticket_status'], fn ($q, $status) => $q->where('status', $status))\n
            ->when($filters['ticket_priority'], fn ($q, $priority) => $q->where('priority', $priority))\n
            ->when($filters['ticket_category'], fn ($q, $category) => $q->where('category', $category))\n
            ->when($filters['source'], fn ($q, $source) => $q->where('source', $source))\n
            ->when($filters['work_type'], fn ($q, $workType) => $q->where('work_type', $workType))\n
            ->when($filters['service'], fn ($q, $service) => $q->where('it_service_id', $service))\n
            ->when($filters['site_id'], fn ($q, $siteId) => $q->where('site_id', $siteId))\n
            ->when($filters['age'], fn ($q, $age) => $this->applyTicketAge($q, $age))\n
            ->when($filters['missing'], fn ($q, $missing) => $q->whereNull(match ($missing) {\n
                'service' => 'it_service_id',\n
                'queue' => 'queue_id',\n
                'team' => 'team_id',\n
                default => 'assigned_to_user_id',\n
            }))\n
            ->when($filters['reopened'], fn ($q) => $q->where('reopened_count', '>', 0))\n
            ->when($filters['first_contact'], fn ($q) => $q->firstContactResolved())\n
            ->when($filters['open_only'], fn ($q) => $q->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES))\n
            ->when($filters['device_linked'], fn ($q) => $q->whereHas('links', fn ($links) => $links\n
                ->where('linkable_type', 'security_device')\n
                ->where('relationship', 'affected_device')))\n
            ->when($filters['resolved_from'], fn ($q, $from) => $q->whereDate('resolved_at', '>=', $from))\n
            ->when($filters['resolved_to'], fn ($q, $to) => $q->whereDate('resolved_at', '<=', $to))\n
            ->when($filters['sla'], fn ($q, $sla) => $this->slaRead->whereState($q, [$sla]))\n
            ->when($filters['assignee'], fn ($q, $assignee) => $q->where('assigned_to_user_id', $assignee))\n
            ->when($filters['from'], fn ($q, $from) => $q->whereDate('created_at', '>=', $from))\n
            ->when($filters['to'], fn ($q, $to) => $q->whereDate('created_at', '<=', $to));\n
\n
        $this->applyTicketSort($query, $filters['sort'] ?? null, $filters['dir'] ?? null);\n
\n
        return $query\n
            ->paginate(15, ['*'], 'tickets_page')\n
            ->withQueryString()\n
            ->through(function (ItTicket $t) use ($user): array {\n
                $canWork = $this->workAccess->canWork($user, $t);\n
\n
                return [\n
                    'id' => $t->id,\n
                    'can' => ['manage' => $canWork],\n
                    'reference' => $t->reference,\n
                    'lock_version' => (int) $t->lock_version,\n
                    'title' => $t->title,\n
                    'description' => $t->description,\n
                    'work_type' => $t->work_type,\n
                    'service' => $t->service ? ['id' => $t->service->id, 'name' => $t->service->name] : null,\n
                    'category' => $t->category,\n
                    'priority' => $t->priority,\n
                    'status' => $t->status,\n
                    'waiting_party' => $canWork ? $t->waiting_party\n
                        : ($t->status === 'waiting' ? ($t->waiting_party === 'requester' ? 'requester' : 'other') : null),\n
                    ...($canWork ? [\n
                        'waiting_reason' => $t->waiting_reason,\n
                        'next_action' => $t->next_action,\n
                        'waiting_since' => $t->waiting_since?->toIso8601String(),\n
                    ] : []),\n
                    ...$this->slaRead->present($t),\n
                    'first_response_due_at' => $t->first_response_due_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                    'resolution_due_at' => $t->resolution_due_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                    'first_responded_at' => $t->first_responded_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                    'conversation' => app(ItTicketConversationPresenter::class)->present($t),\n
                    'requester' => $t->requester?->name ?? 'Unknown',\n
                    'assignee' => $t->assignee ? ['id' => $t->assignee->id, 'name' => $t->assignee->name] : null,\n
                    ...($canWork ? ['routing' => $this->routingPresenter->present($t)] : []),\n
                    'age' => $t->created_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
                    'updated' => $t->updated_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
                    'resolved' => $t->resolved_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
                ];\n
            });\n
    }\n
\n
    private function applyTicketAge($query, string $age): void\n
    {\n
        $now = now();\n
        $twoDays = $now->copy()->subDays(2);\n
        $sevenDays = $now->copy()->subDays(7);\n
        $thirtyDays = $now->copy()->subDays(30);\n
\n
        match ($age) {\n
            'under_2' => $query->where('created_at', '>=', $twoDays),\n
            '2_7' => $query->where('created_at', '>=', $sevenDays)->where('created_at', '<', $twoDays),\n
            '8_30' => $query->where('created_at', '>=', $thirtyDays)->where('created_at', '<', $sevenDays),\n
            'over_30' => $query->where('created_at', '<', $thirtyDays),\n
        };\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * The requester's own tickets — the ONLY ticket rows a self-service\n
     * payload may carry. No requester column (it is always "you") and no\n
     * assignee identity beyond a name, mirroring what a helpdesk shows the\n
     * person who raised the ticket.\n
     */\n
    private function myTicketRows(User $user): array\n
    {\n
        if (! Schema::hasTable('it_tickets')) {\n
            return [];\n
        }\n
\n
        return $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)\n
            ->where(fn ($participant) => $participant\n
                ->where('requester_user_id', $user->id)\n
                ->orWhere('requested_for_user_id', $user->id))\n
            ->with('assignee:id,name')\n
            ->orderByRaw("CASE status WHEN 'open' THEN 0 WHEN 'in_progress' THEN 1 WHEN 'resolved' THEN 2 ELSE 3 END")\n
            ->orderByDesc('created_at')\n
            ->get()\n
            ->map(fn (ItTicket $t) => [\n
                'id' => $t->id,\n
                'reference' => $t->reference,\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $t->lock_version,\n
                'title' => $t->title,\n
                'description' => $t->description,\n
                'category' => $t->category,\n
                'priority' => $t->priority,\n
                'status' => $t->status,\n
                'waiting_party' => $t->status === 'waiting'\n
                    ? ($t->waiting_party === 'requester' ? 'requester' : 'other')\n
                    : null,\n
                'assignee' => $t->assignee?->name,\n
                'age' => $t->created_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
                'resolved' => $t->resolved_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
                'conversation' => app(ItTicketConversationPresenter::class)->present($t),\n
                // Requested-for participation grants a public conversation,\n
                // but only the requester may rate under the canonical policy.\n
                'can_rate' => $user->can('csat', $t),\n
                'can_reply' => ! $t->isMerged() && in_array($t->status, ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES, true),\n
                'can_reopen' => ! $t->isMerged() && in_array($t->status, ['resolved', 'closed'], true)\n
                    && $user->can('reopen', $t),\n
                'csat_score' => $t->csat_submitted_at ? (int) $t->csat_score : null,\n
            ])\n
            ->values()\n
            ->all();\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * The server summary that feeds the hero, tab badges and predefined-view\n
     * chips. Computed over ALL rows (never the current page); requesters get\n
     * only their own `my` section — an agent-shaped summary must never reach\n
     * a self-service payload.\n
     *\n
     * @return array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m\n
     */\n
    private function summary(User $user, bool $isAgent): array\n
    {\n
        $ticketsReady = Schema::hasTable('it_tickets');\n
        $requestsReady = Schema::hasTable('it_provisioning_requests');\n
        $conversationReady = $ticketsReady && ItTicket::hasConversationEvidence();\n
\n
        $my = $ticketsReady\n
            ? $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)\n
                ->where(fn ($participant) => $participant\n
                    ->where('requester_user_id', $user->id)\n
                    ->orWhere('requested_for_user_id', $user->id))\n
                ->selectRaw(\n
                    "COUNT(*) AS total,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('open', 'in_progress', 'waiting')) AS open_count,\n
                     SUM(status = 'waiting') AS waiting,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('resolved', 'closed') AND resolved_at >= ?) AS resolved_30d",\n
                    [now()->subDays(30)],\n
                )\n
                ->first()\n
            : null;\n
\n
        $summary = [\n
            'my' => [\n
                'total' => (int) ($my->total ?? 0),\n
                'open' => (int) ($my->open_count ?? 0),\n
                'waiting' => (int) ($my->waiting ?? 0),\n
                'resolved_30d' => (int) ($my->resolved_30d ?? 0),\n
            ],\n
        ];\n
\n
        if (! $isAgent) {\n
            return $summary;\n
        }\n
\n
        $tickets = $ticketsReady\n
            ? $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)\n
                ->selectRaw(\n
                    "SUM(status IN ('open', 'in_progress', 'waiting')) AS open_count,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('open', 'in_progress', 'waiting') AND assigned_to_user_id IS NULL) AS unassigned,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('open', 'in_progress', 'waiting') AND assigned_to_user_id IS NULL AND priority = 'urgent') AS urgent_unassigned,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('open', 'in_progress', 'waiting') AND priority = 'urgent') AS urgent_open,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('open', 'in_progress') AND first_responded_at IS NULL) AS awaiting_reply,\n
                     SUM(status = 'waiting') AS waiting,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('open', 'in_progress', 'waiting') AND assigned_to_user_id = ?) AS mine,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('resolved', 'closed') AND resolved_at >= ?) AS resolved_30d,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('resolved', 'closed') AND resolved_at >= ?) AS recently_resolved,\n
                     SUM(status = 'open') AS status_open,\n
                     SUM(status = 'in_progress') AS status_in_progress,\n
                     SUM(status = 'resolved') AS status_resolved,\n
                     SUM(status = 'closed') AS status_closed",\n
                    [(int) $user->id, $this->slaRead->evaluatedAt()->subDays(30), $this->slaRead->evaluatedAt()->subDays(30)],\n
                )\n
                ->first()\n
            : null;\n
\n
        $ownedByMe = $ticketsReady\n
            ? $this->applyTicketView(\n
                $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user),\n
                'owned_by_me',\n
                $user,\n
            )->count()\n
            : 0;\n
        $myTeam = $ticketsReady\n
            ? $this->applyTicketView(\n
                $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user),\n
                'my_team',\n
                $user,\n
            )->count()\n
            : 0;\n
\n
        $awaitingIt = $conversationReady\n
            ? $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)->awaitingIt()->count()\n
            : null;\n
\n
        $operationalViews = [];\n
        foreach (['unowned', 'waiting_requester', 'waiting_vendor', 'waiting_approver'] as $view) {\n
            $operationalViews[$view] = $ticketsReady\n
                ? $this->applyTicketView(ItTicket::query(), $view, $user)->count()\n
                : 0;\n
        }\n
\n
        $requests = $requestsReady\n
            ? $this->provisioningAccess->applyRequestScope(ItProvisioningRequest::query(), $user)\n
                ->selectRaw(\n
                    "SUM(status = 'pending') AS pending,\n
                     SUM(status = 'in_progress') AS in_progress,\n
                     SUM(status = 'failed') AS failed,\n
                     SUM(status = 'done' AND fulfilled_at >= ?) AS done_30d,\n
                     SUM(status IN ('pending', 'in_progress', 'failed') AND due_date IS NOT NULL AND due_date < ?) AS overdue,\n
                     SUM(status = 'pending' AND created_at < ?) AS pending_over_7d",\n
                    [now()->subDays(30), now()->toDateString(), now()->subDays(7)],\n
                )\n
                ->first()\n
            : null;\n
\n
        $slaOpen = $ticketsReady ? $this->slaRead->summarize(\n
            $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES),\n
        ) : null;\n
        $slaResolved = $ticketsReady ? $this->slaRead->summarize(\n
            $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)\n
                ->whereIn('status', ['resolved', 'closed'])->where('resolved_at', '>=', $this->slaRead->evaluatedAt()->subDays(30)),\n
        ) : null;\n
\n
        $summary['tickets'] = [\n
            'open' => (int) ($tickets->open_count ?? 0),\n
            'unassigned' => (int) ($tickets->unassigned ?? 0),\n
            'urgent_unassigned' => (int) ($tickets->urgent_unassigned ?? 0),\n
            'urgent_open' => (int) ($tickets->urgent_open ?? 0),\n
            'at_risk' => $slaOpen['by_state']['at_risk'] ?? 0,\n
            'breached' => $slaOpen['by_state']['breached'] ?? 0,\n
            'awaiting_reply' => (int) ($tickets->awaiting_reply ?? 0),\n
            'conversation_ready' => $conversationReady,\n
            'awaiting_it' => $awaitingIt,\n
            'waiting' => (int) ($tickets->waiting ?? 0),\n
            'resolved_30d' => (int) ($tickets->resolved_30d ?? 0),\n
            'measured_30d' => $slaResolved['by_coverage']['full'] ?? 0,\n
            'met_30d' => $slaResolved['by_state']['met'] ?? 0,\n
            'sla_open' => $slaOpen,\n
            'sla_resolved_30d' => $slaResolved,\n
            'sla_watchdog' => app(ItAutomationScheduleCatalog::class)->freshnessFor('it.check-sla', $this->slaRead->evaluatedAt()),\n
            'by_status' => [\n
                'open' => (int) ($tickets->status_open ?? 0),\n
                'in_progress' => (int) ($tickets->status_in_progress ?? 0),\n
                'waiting' => (int) ($tickets->waiting ?? 0),\n
                'resolved' => (int) ($tickets->status_resolved ?? 0),\n
                'closed' => (int) ($tickets->status_closed ?? 0),\n
            ],\n
            'views' => [\n
                ...$operationalViews,\n
                'all_open' => (int) ($tickets->open_count ?? 0),\n
                'unassigned' => (int) ($tickets->unassigned ?? 0),\n
                'mine' => (int) ($tickets->mine ?? 0),\n
                'owned_by_me' => $ownedByMe,\n
                'my_team' => $myTeam,\n
                'breaching' => $slaOpen['by_state']['at_risk'] ?? 0,\n
                'breached' => $slaOpen['by_state']['breached'] ?? 0,\n
                'awaiting_reply' => (int) ($tickets->awaiting_reply ?? 0),\n
                'awaiting_it' => $awaitingIt,\n
                'waiting' => (int) ($tickets->waiting ?? 0),\n
                'unmeasured' => $slaOpen['by_state']['unmeasured'] ?? 0,\n
                'recently_resolved' => (int) ($tickets->recently_resolved ?? 0),\n
            ],\n
        ];\n
\n
        $summary['provisioning'] = [\n
            'pending' => (int) ($requests->pending ?? 0),\n
            'in_progress' => (int) ($requests->in_progress ?? 0),\n
            'failed' => (int) ($requests->failed ?? 0),\n
            'done_30d' => (int) ($requests->done_30d ?? 0),\n
            'overdue' => (int) ($requests->overdue ?? 0),\n
            'pending_over_7d' => (int) ($requests->pending_over_7d ?? 0),\n
        ];\n
\n
        return $summary;\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * §F1 Overview payload (agents): the avg-first-response KPI plus the four\n
     * "needs attention" lanes — short, capped, Site-scoped lists that each\n
     * deep-link into the filtered queue. The KPI *counts* come from the\n
     * summary; this adds the one metric the summary can't cheaply carry and\n
     * the lane contents. Guarded so a pre-migration read renders an empty board.\n
     *\n
     * @return array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m\n
     */\n
    private function overview(User $user): array\n
    {\n
        $empty = [\n
            'avg_first_response_mins' => null,\n
            'conversation_ready' => false,\n
            'sla_lane' => [],\n
            'awaiting_lane' => [],\n
            'awaiting_it_lane' => [],\n
            'aging_lane' => [],\n
            'unassigned_by_priority' => ['urgent' => 0, 'high' => 0, 'normal' => 0, 'low' => 0],\n
            'recent_activity' => [],\n
        ];\n
\n
        if (! Schema::hasTable('it_tickets')) {\n
            return $empty;\n
        }\n
\n
        $base = fn () => $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)\n
            ->with(['requester:id,name', 'assignee:id,name']);\n
        $conversationReady = ItTicket::hasConversationEvidence();\n
\n
        // Avg minutes from raise to first agent reply, over replies in the last 30d.\n
        $avg = $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)\n
            ->whereNotNull('first_responded_at')\n
            ->where('first_responded_at', '>=', now()->subDays(30))\n
            ->selectRaw('AVG(TIMESTAMPDIFF(MINUTE, created_at, first_responded_at)) AS mins')\n
            ->value('mins');\n
\n
        // SLA lane: open tickets at risk or breached, most urgent clock first.\n
        $attentionIds = $this->slaRead->attentionIds($base()->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES));\n
        $slaLane = $base()->whereKey($attentionIds)\n
            ->get()\n
            ->sortBy(fn (ItTicket $ticket) => array_search($ticket->id, $attentionIds, true))->values()\n
            ->map(fn (ItTicket $t) => [\n
                'id' => $t->id,\n
                'reference' => $t->reference,\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $t->lock_version,\n
                'title' => $t->title,\n
                'priority' => $t->priority,\n
                ...$this->slaRead->present($t),\n
                'resolution_due_at' => $t->resolution_due_at?->toIso8601String(),\n
                'assignee' => $t->assignee?->name,\n
            ]);\n
\n
        // Compatibility lane: first response only, never conversation responsibility.\n
        $awaitingLane = $base()\n
            ->whereIn('status', ['open', 'in_progress'])\n
            ->whereNull('first_responded_at')\n
            ->orderBy('created_at')\n
            ->limit(6)\n
            ->get()\n
            ->map(fn (ItTicket $t) => [\n
                'id' => $t->id,\n
                'reference' => $t->reference,\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $t->lock_version,\n
                'title' => $t->title,\n
                'priority' => $t->priority,\n
                'requester' => $t->requester?->name ?? 'Unknown',\n
                'age' => $t->created_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
            ]);\n
\n
        $awaitingItLane = $conversationReady ? $base()->awaitingIt()\n
            ->orderBy('created_at')->orderBy('id')->limit(6)->get()\n
            ->map(fn (ItTicket $ticket) => [\n
                'id' => $ticket->id, 'reference' => $ticket->reference, 'lock_version' => (int) $ticket->lock_version,\n
                'title' => $ticket->title, 'priority' => $ticket->priority,\n
                'requester' => $ticket->requester?->name ?? 'Unknown',\n
                // This is ticket age; no historical response-wait duration is inferred.\n
                'created_age' => $ticket->created_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
                'conversation' => app(ItTicketConversationPresenter::class)->present($ticket),\n
            ]) : collect();\n
\n
        // Aging: open longer than 7 days, oldest first.\n
        $agingLane = $base()\n
            ->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES)\n
            ->where('created_at', '<', now()->subDays(7))\n
            ->orderBy('created_at')\n
            ->limit(6)\n
            ->get()\n
            ->map(fn (ItTicket $t) => [\n
                'id' => $t->id,\n
                'reference' => $t->reference,\n
                'lock_version' => (int) $t->lock_version,\n
                'title' => $t->title,\n
                'priority' => $t->priority,\n
                'assignee' => $t->assignee?->name,\n
                'age' => $t->created_at?->diffForHumans(short: true),\n
            ]);\n
\n
        // Unassigned open tickets, split by priority (feeds the chip row).\n
        $byPriority = $this->workAccess->applyViewScope(ItTicket::query(), $user)\n
            ->whereIn('status', ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES)\n
            ->whereNull('assigned_to_user_id')\n
            ->selectRaw("SUM(priority = 'urgent') AS urgent, SUM(priority = 'high') AS high, SUM(priority = 'normal') AS normal, SUM(priority = 'low') AS low")\n
            ->first();\n
\n
        return [\n
            'avg_first_response_mins' => $avg !== null ? (int) round((float) $avg) : null,\n
            'conversation_ready' => $conversationReady,\n
            'sla_lane' => $slaLane,\n
            'awaiting_lane' => $awaitingLane,\n
            'awaiting_it_lane' => $awaitingItLane,\n
            'aging_lane' => $agingLane,\n
            'unassigned_by_priority' => [\n
                'urgent' => (int) ($byPriority->urgent ?? 0),\n
                'high' => (int) ($byPriority->high ?? 0),\n
                'normal' => (int) ($byPriority->normal ?? 0),\n
                'low' => (int) ($byPriority->low ?? 0),\n
            ],\n
            'recent_activity' => $this->recentActivity($user),\n
        ];\n
    }\n
\n
    /**\n
     * §F1 recent-activity feed — the latest visible ticket events,\n
     * humanised on the client. Ticket subjects only (each row deep-links to a\n
     * ticket); provisioning events stay on their own request rows. `subject`\n
     * is loaded via the morph so a deleted ticket drops out rather than 500s.\n
     *\n
     * @return array<int, array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m>\n
     */\n
    private function recentActivity(User $user): array\n
    {\n
        if (! Schema::hasTable('it_ticket_events')) {\n
            return [];\n
        }\n

        return ItTicketEvent::query()\n
            ->where('subject_type', 'it_ticket')\n
            ->whereHasMorph('subject', [ItTicket::class], fn ($ticket) => $this->workAccess->applyViewScope($ticket, $user))\n
            ->with(['actor:id,name', 'subject'])\n
            ->latest('created_at')\n
            ->limit(8)\n
            ->get()\n
            ->map(function (ItTicketEvent $event) use ($user): ?array {\n
                $projection = $this->activityPresenter->presentEvent($event, $user);\n
                if ($projection === null) {\n
                    return null;\n
                }\n

                return [\n

                    'type' => $projection['type'],\n
                    'payload' => $projection['payload'],\n
                    'actor' => $projection['actor'],\n

                    'reference' => $event->subject?->reference,\n

    }\n
\n
    /**\n

    /** @param array[39m[31m<int, string>[39m[31m $allowed */\n
    private function cleanFilter(mixed $value, array $allowed): ?string\n
    {\n
        return in_array($value, $allowed, true) ? $value : null;\n
    }\n
\n
    /** A strict, real Y-m-d date or null — the queue's date-range filters. */\n
    private function cleanDate(mixed $value): ?string\n
    {\n
        if (! is_string($value) || ! preg_match('/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/', $value, $matches)) {\n
            return null;\n
        }\n
\n
        return checkdate((int) $matches[2], (int) $matches[3], (int) $matches[1]) ? $value : null;\n
    }\n

    /**\n
     * Every adapter routes a persisted canonical ticket. Classification is\n
     * authoritative except for an explicit, still-eligible manual choice.\n
     */\n
    public function route(ItTicket $ticket, ?int $actorUserId = null): ItTicket\n
    {\n
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($ticket, $actorUserId): ItTicket {\n
            $ticket = ItTicket::query()->lockForUpdate()->findOrFail($ticket->id);\n
            $ticket->load('service');\n
            $before = $ticket->only([...self::OWNERSHIP, 'routing_decision']);\n
            if ($ticket->priority_decision === null) {\n
                $ticket->priority_decision = $this->priority->legacyDecision($ticket);\n
            }\n
\n

                if ($chosen && $this->queueCanReceive($chosen, $ticket)) {\n
                    $queue = $chosen;\n
                    $strategy = 'override';\n
                } else {\n
                    $suspended[] = 'queue_id';\n
                }\n
            }\n
\n

                    $ticket->owner_user_id = $fields['owner_user_id'];\n
                    $owner['source'] = 'override';\n

                } else {\n
                    $suspended[] = 'owner_user_id';\n
                }\n

            if ($candidate !== null && (int) $candidate === (int) $oldAutomaticAssignee) {\n
                $candidate = null;\n
            }\n
            $candidate = $candidate !== null && $this->eligibility->agent((int) $candidate, $ticket)\n
                ? (int) $candidate : null;\n
            $automaticAssignee = null;\n

    /**\n
     * Validate a manual intent for the locked triage command. This only builds\n
     * provenance; the canonical triage transaction persists it and routes.\n
     *\n
     * @param  array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m  $data\n
     * @return array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m\n
     */\n
    public function manualOverride(ItTicket $ticket, User $actor, array $data): array\n
    {\n
        if (! $actor->canDo('it.manage') || ! $this->workAccess->canWork($actor, $ticket)) {\n
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['routing_reason' => 'You cannot change this ticket ownership.']);\n
        }\n
        $reason = $this->reason($data['routing_reason'] ?? null);\n
        $fields = (array) ($ticket->routing_override['fields'] ?? []);\n
        foreach (Arr::only($data, ['queue_id', 'owner_user_id', 'assigned_to_user_id']) as $field => $value) {\n
            $value = $value !== null ? (int) $value : null;\n
            if ($field === 'queue_id') {\n
                $queue = $value !== null ? ItQueue::query()->with('team.members')->find($value) : null;\n
                if (! $queue || ! $this->queueCanReceive($queue, $ticket)) {\n
                    throw ValidationException::withMessages([$field => 'Choose an active queue within this ticket scope.']);\n
                }\n
            } elseif (($value === null && $field === 'owner_user_id')\n
                || ($value !== null && ! $this->eligibility->agent($value, $ticket))) {\n
                throw ValidationException::withMessages([$field => 'Choose an available current IT technician approved for this ticket.']);\n
            }\n
            $fields[$field] = $value;\n
        }\n
        if ($fields === []) {\n
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['routing_reason' => 'Choose the ownership you want to preserve.']);\n
        }\n
        if (($ticket->routing_override['fields'] ?? null) === $fields\n
            && ($ticket->routing_override['reason'] ?? null) === $reason\n
            && ($ticket->routing_override['actor_user_id'] ?? null) === $actor->id) {\n
            return $ticket->routing_override;\n
        }\n
\n
        return ['fields' => $fields, 'reason' => $reason, 'actor_user_id' => $actor->id,\n
            'set_at' => now()->toIso8601String()];\n
    }\n
\n
    public function reason(mixed $reason): string\n
    {\n
        if (! is_string($reason) || trim($reason) === '' || mb_strlen($reason) > 1000) {\n
            throw ValidationException::withMessages(['routing_reason' => 'Explain why this ownership is needed.']);\n
        }\n
\n
        return trim($reason);\n
    }\n

    /** @return list[39m[31m<array{id: int, name: string}>[39m[31m */\n
    public function queueOptions(ItTicket $ticket): array\n
    {\n
        return ItQueue::query()->with('team')->where('is_active', true)->orderBy('name')->get()\n
            ->filter(fn (ItQueue $queue) => $this->queueCanReceive($queue, $ticket))\n
            ->map(fn (ItQueue $queue): array => ['id' => $queue->id, 'name' => $queue->name])->values()->all();\n
    }\n

    /** Configuration readiness is separate from an individual ticket decision. */\n
    public function queueReadiness(ItQueue $queue): array\n
    {\n
        $queue->load(['team.manager', 'team.members']);\n
        $manager = $queue->team?->manager;\n
        $coverId = $queue->filter_rules['cover_user_id'] ?? null;\n
        $cover = is_numeric($coverId) ? User::query()->find((int) $coverId) : null;\n
        $gaps = [];\n
        if (! $queue->team?->is_active) {\n
            $gaps[] = 'An active accountable team is required.';\n
        }\n
        if (! $manager?->approved_at || ! $manager->canDo('it.manage') || ! $this->eligibility->currentlyEmployed($manager->id)) {\n
            $gaps[] = 'The accountable manager needs current IT and staff access.';\n
        }\n
        if (! $cover || ! $this->teamIncludes($queue, $cover->id) || $cover->id === $manager?->id\n
            || ! $cover->approved_at || ! $cover->canDo('it.manage') || ! $this->eligibility->currentlyEmployed($cover->id)) {\n
            $gaps[] = 'A distinct current cover person in the team is required.';\n
        }\n
        $configuredSites = (array) ($queue->filter_rules['site_ids'] ?? []);\n
        $sites = Site::query()->where('is_active', true)->where('archived', false)->whereNull('archived_at')\n
            ->when($configuredSites !== [], fn ($query) => $query->whereKey($configuredSites))->pluck('id');\n
        if ($sites->isEmpty() || ($configuredSites !== [] && $sites->count() !== count(array_unique($configuredSites)))) {\n
            $gaps[] = 'The selected Sites must be active.';\n
        }\n
        if ($manager && $cover && $sites->contains(function ($siteId) use ($manager, $cover): bool {\n
            $ticket = new ItTicket(['site_id' => (int) $siteId, 'is_organisation_wide' => false]);\n
\n
            return ! $this->eligibility->agent($manager->id, $ticket, available: false)\n
                || ! $this->eligibility->agent($cover->id, $ticket, available: false);\n
        })) {\n
            $gaps[] = 'The manager and cover need approved access to every Site this queue covers.';\n
        }\n
\n
        return ['ready' => $queue->is_active && $gaps === [], 'gaps' => $gaps,\n
            'accountable_owner' => $manager ? ['id' => $manager->id, 'name' => $manager->name] : null,\n
            'cover' => $cover ? ['id' => $cover->id, 'name' => $cover->name] : null];\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @return array{0: ?ItQueue, 1: string} */\n
    private function matchingQueue(ItTicket $ticket): array\n
    {\n
        $queues = ItQueue::query()->where('is_active', true)->with('team.members')->orderBy('id')->get()\n
            ->filter(fn (ItQueue $queue) => $this->queueCanReceive($queue, $ticket))\n
            ->sortBy([fn (ItQueue $a, ItQueue $b): int => ((int) ($b->filter_rules['routing_priority'] ?? 0) <=> (int) ($a->filter_rules['routing_priority'] ?? 0))\n
                ?: ($a->id <=> $b->id)]);\n
        $specific = $queues->filter(fn (ItQueue $queue) => ! ($queue->filter_rules['is_default'] ?? false)\n
            && $this->matches($queue->filter_rules ?? [], $ticket));\n
        $fallback = $queues->filter(fn (ItQueue $queue) => (bool) ($queue->filter_rules['is_default'] ?? false));\n
        foreach ([$specific, $fallback] as $index => $choices) {\n
            $ready = $choices->first(fn (ItQueue $queue) => $this->ownerDecision($queue, $ticket)['owner_user_id'] !== null);\n
            if ($ready) {\n
                return [$ready, $index === 0 ? 'rule' : 'fallback'];\n
            }\n
        }\n
        $queue = $fallback->first() ?? $specific->first();\n
\n
        return [$queue, $queue ? (($queue->filter_rules['is_default'] ?? false) ? 'fallback' : 'rule') : 'unconfigured'];\n
    }\n
\n
    private function queueCanReceive(ItQueue $queue, ItTicket $ticket): bool\n
    {\n
        return $queue->is_active\n
            && ($queue->team === null || $queue->team->is_active)\n
            && $this->allows((array) ($queue->filter_rules['site_ids'] ?? []), $ticket->site_id);\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @return array{owner_user_id: ?int, accountable_user_id: ?int, source: string} */\n
    private function ownerDecision(?ItQueue $queue, ItTicket $ticket): array\n
    {\n
        $serviceOwner = $ticket->service?->is_active ? $ticket->service->owner_user_id : null;\n
        $manager = $queue?->team?->manager_user_id;\n
        $primary = $serviceOwner !== null && $this->eligibility->agent((int) $serviceOwner, $ticket, available: false)\n
            ? (int) $serviceOwner\n
            : ($manager !== null && $this->eligibility->agent((int) $manager, $ticket, available: false) ? (int) $manager : null);\n
        if ($primary !== null && $this->eligibility->agent($primary, $ticket)) {\n
            return ['owner_user_id' => $primary, 'accountable_user_id' => $primary,\n
                'source' => $primary === (int) $serviceOwner ? 'service' : 'team'];\n
        }\n
        $cover = $this->eligibleCover($queue, $ticket);\n
\n
        return ['owner_user_id' => $cover?->id, 'accountable_user_id' => $primary, 'source' => $cover ? 'cover' : 'unavailable'];\n
    }\n
\n
    private function eligibleCover(?ItQueue $queue, ItTicket $ticket): ?User\n
    {\n
        $id = $queue?->filter_rules['cover_user_id'] ?? null;\n
        if (! is_numeric($id) || (int) $id === (int) $queue?->team?->manager_user_id\n
            || (int) $id === (int) $ticket->service?->owner_user_id\n
            || ! $this->teamIncludes($queue, (int) $id)) {\n
            return null;\n
        }\n
\n
        return $this->eligibility->agent((int) $id, $ticket);\n
    }\n
\n
    private function teamIncludes(?ItQueue $queue, int $userId): bool\n
    {\n
        return $queue?->team !== null && ((int) $queue->team->manager_user_id === $userId\n
            || $queue->team->members->contains('id', $userId));\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $rules */\n
    private function matches(array $rules, ItTicket $ticket): bool\n
    {\n
        return $this->allows((array) ($rules['work_types'] ?? []), $ticket->work_type)\n
            && $this->allows((array) ($rules['categories'] ?? []), $ticket->category)\n
            && $this->allows((array) ($rules['priorities'] ?? []), $ticket->priority)\n
            && $this->allows((array) ($rules['service_ids'] ?? []), $ticket->it_service_id)\n
            && $this->allows((array) ($rules['site_ids'] ?? []), $ticket->site_id);\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<int, mixed>[39m[31m $allowed */\n
    private function allows(array $allowed, mixed $actual): bool\n
    {\n
        return $allowed === [] || in_array((string) $actual, array_map('strval', $allowed), true);\n
    }\n

    /** Stable command identity for adapters; the existing mutation remains the property owner. */\n
    public function updateCommand(ItTicket $ticket, User $actor, array $data, ItTicketCommandChannel $channel): ItTicket\n
    {\n
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($ticket, $actor, $data, $channel): ItTicket {\n
            $currentEvidence = $channel === ItTicketCommandChannel::ServiceApi;\n
            $actor = $this->versions->currentActor($actor, $currentEvidence);\n
            $ticket = $this->lockTicket($ticket);\n
            $this->guardActor($ticket, $actor, $currentEvidence);\n
            Validator::make($data, ['request_uuid' => ['required', 'uuid'],\n
                'expected_version' => ['required', 'integer', 'min:1']])->validate();\n
            $payload = Arr::except($data, ['request_uuid']);\n
            ksort($payload);\n
            $hash = hash('sha256', json_encode([(int) $ticket->id, $payload], JSON_THROW_ON_ERROR));\n
            $receipt = ItTicketCommandReceipt::query()->where('actor_user_id', $actor->id)\n
                ->where('channel', $channel->value)->where('operation', ItTicketCommandReceipt::UPDATE_OPERATION)\n
                ->where('request_uuid', $data['request_uuid'])->lockForUpdate()->first();\n
            if ($receipt) {\n
                if ((int) $receipt->it_ticket_id !== (int) $ticket->id\n
                    || ! hash_equals((string) $receipt->request_hash, $hash)) {\n
                    throw new ItTicketCommandConflict;\n
                }\n
                if ($receipt->committed_at === null || (int) $receipt->committed_ticket_version < 1) {\n
                    throw new DomainException('This update has no confirmed outcome. Review its command history before retrying.');\n
                }\n
\n
                return $ticket;\n
            }\n
            $saved = $this->update($ticket, $actor, Arr::except($data, ['request_uuid']), $channel->value);\n
            ItTicketCommandReceipt::query()->create([\n
                'actor_user_id' => $actor->id, 'channel' => $channel->value,\n
                'operation' => ItTicketCommandReceipt::UPDATE_OPERATION, 'request_uuid' => $data['request_uuid'],\n
                'request_hash' => $hash, 'it_ticket_id' => $saved->id, 'committed_at' => now(),\n
                'committed_ticket_version' => $saved->lock_version,\n
            ]);\n
\n
            return $saved;\n
        });\n
    }\n

    /**\n

    public function bulkUpdate(ItTicket $ticket, User $actor, array $data, string $source): bool\n
    {\n
        return $this->bulkOutcome($ticket, $actor, $data, $source)['status'] === 'updated';\n
