    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $data @return array{status: string, message: string} */\n
    public function bulkOutcome(ItTicket $ticket, User $actor, array $data, string $source): array\n
    {\n
        return ItBulkActionResult::capture(fn (): bool => $this->mutate($ticket, $actor, $data, $source)['changed']);\n
    }\n
\n
    public function close(\n
        ItTicket $ticket,\n
        User $actor,\n
        string $reason,\n
        string $source = 'legacy_close',\n
        bool $staleIsUnchanged = false,\n
        ?int $expectedVersion = null,\n
    ): bool {\n
        try {\n
            $this->closeWithReason($ticket, $actor, $reason, $source, $expectedVersion);\n
\n
            return true;\n
        } catch (Throwable $exception) {\n
            if ($staleIsUnchanged && ($exception instanceof AuthorizationException\n
                || $exception instanceof DomainException\n
                || $exception instanceof ModelNotFoundException\n
                || $exception instanceof ValidationException\n
                || $exception instanceof ItTicketVersionConflict)) {\n
                return false;\n
            }\n
\n
            throw $exception;\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    /** Return the committed snapshot while retaining the legacy boolean adapter. */\n
    public function closeWithReason(\n
        ItTicket $ticket,\n
        User $actor,\n
        string $reason,\n
        string $source = 'legacy_close',\n
        ?int $expectedVersion = null,\n
    ): ItTicket {\n
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($ticket, $actor, $reason, $source, $expectedVersion): ItTicket {\n
            $locked = $this->lockTicket($ticket);\n
            $actor = $this->versions->currentActor($actor);\n
            $this->guardActor($locked, $actor);\n
            $this->versions->assertCurrent($locked, $expectedVersion);\n
\n
            if ($locked->status === 'closed') {\n
                throw new DomainException('This ticket is already closed.');\n
            }\n
            if ($locked->isMerged()) {\n
                throw new DomainException('This ticket was merged and cannot be closed again.');\n
            }\n
\n
            $closed = $this->transitionService->transition(\n
                $locked,\n
                new ItTransitionInput(\n
                    actor: $actor,\n
                    to: ItWorkflowState::Closed,\n
                    reason: $reason,\n
                    source: $source,\n
                ),\n
            );\n
\n
            AuditLogger::logOrFail('it.ticket.closed', $closed, [\n
                'actor_id' => $actor->id,\n
                'source' => $source,\n
                'reason_recorded' => true,\n
                'application_scope' => 'single_application',\n
            ]);\n
\n
            return $closed;\n
        });\n
    }\n

    /**\n
     * @param  array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m  $data\n
     * @return array{ticket: ItTicket, changed: bool}\n
     */\n
    private function mutate(ItTicket $ticket, User $actor, array $data, string $source): array\n
    {\n
        return DB::transaction(function () use ($ticket, $actor, $data, $source): array {\n
            $currentEvidence = $source === ItTicketCommandChannel::ServiceApi->value;\n
            $actor = $this->versions->currentActor($actor, $currentEvidence);\n
            $locked = $this->lockTicket($ticket);\n
            $this->guardActor($locked, $actor, $currentEvidence);\n
            $this->versions->assertCurrent($locked, isset($data['expected_version']) ? (int) $data['expected_version'] : null);\n
\n
            if ($locked->isMerged()) {\n
                throw new DomainException('This ticket was merged and can no longer be changed.');\n
            }\n
            if ($locked->status === 'closed') {\n
                throw new DomainException('Reopen this ticket before changing its triage properties.');\n
            }\n
            if ($source === 'bulk' && ! in_array($locked->status, ItTicket::OPEN_STATUSES, true)) {\n
                throw new DomainException('Settled tickets keep their existing triage history.');\n
            }\n
\n
            app(ItTicketDraftService::class)->consumeFromInput($actor, $data, ItTicketDraftPurpose::TicketEdit, (int) $locked->id);\n
\n
            $before = $locked->only(self::PROPERTY_FIELDS);\n
            if ($currentEvidence && array_key_exists('priority', $data)) {\n
                // API priority changes use the current assessment, including for\n
                // legacy tickets; they cannot borrow intake's compatibility path.\n
                $data = ['impact' => $locked->impact ?? 'individual',\n
                    'urgency' => $locked->urgency ?? 'normal', ...$data];\n
            }\n
            $manualOwnership = Arr::only($data, ['queue_id', 'owner_user_id', 'assigned_to_user_id']);\n
            $releaseRouting = (bool) ($data['release_routing_override'] ?? false);\n
            if ($releaseRouting && $manualOwnership !== []) {\n
                throw ValidationException::withMessages(['routing_reason' => 'Release the override separately from choosing new ownership.']);\n
            }\n
            if ($manualOwnership !== [] || $releaseRouting) {\n
                $this->routing->reason($data['routing_reason'] ?? null);\n
            }\n
            $waitingBefore = $locked->only(['waiting_party', 'waiting_reason', 'next_action']);\n
            [$siteId, $isApplicationWide] = $this->prospectiveScope($locked, $data, $actor);\n
            $this->releaseIneligibleAssigneeAfterScopeChange(\n
                $locked,\n
                $data,\n
                $siteId,\n
                $isApplicationWide,\n
            );\n
            $this->guardAssignee($data, $siteId, $isApplicationWide);\n
            $this->guardAsset($locked, $data, $siteId, $isApplicationWide);\n
            $this->guardService($data);\n
\n
            $targetStatus = $data['status'] ?? null;\n
            $waitingContextSupplied = array_key_exists('waiting_party', $data)\n
                || array_key_exists('waiting_reason', $data)\n
                || array_key_exists('next_action', $data);\n
            if (is_string($targetStatus)\n
                && ($targetStatus !== $locked->status\n
                    || ($targetStatus === 'waiting' && $waitingContextSupplied))) {\n
                $locked = $this->transitionService->transition(\n
                    $locked,\n
                    new ItTransitionInput(\n
                        actor: $actor,\n
                        to: $this->workingState($targetStatus),\n
                        reason: $data['waiting_reason']\n
                            ?? ($targetStatus === 'waiting' ? 'Waiting on requester' : 'Ticket properties updated'),\n
                        waitingParty: $targetStatus === 'waiting'\n
                            ? ($data['waiting_party'] ?? 'requester')\n
                            : null,\n
                        nextAction: $data['next_action'] ?? null,\n
                        source: $source === 'bulk' ? 'bulk_status' : 'legacy_status',\n
                    ),\n
                );\n
            }\n
\n
            $properties = Arr::only($data, self::PROPERTY_FIELDS);\n
            // Decisions are owned by the services, never accepted as JSON input.\n
            unset($properties['priority_decision'], $properties['routing_override'], $properties['team_id']);\n
            if (array_intersect(array_keys($data), ['impact', 'urgency', 'priority', 'release_priority_override']) !== []) {\n
                $properties = [...$properties, ...$this->priority->decide($data, $actor, $locked)];\n
            }\n
            unset(\n
                $properties['status'],\n
                $properties['waiting_reason'],\n
                $properties['waiting_party'],\n
                $properties['next_action'],\n
                $properties['resolution_code'],\n
                $properties['resolution_summary'],\n
                $properties['expected_version'],\n
            );\n
\n
            $locked->fill($properties);\n
            if (array_key_exists('category', $properties)\n
                && ItTicket::categoryNeedsApproval((string) $properties['category'])) {\n
                // Category policy may tighten after intake. Never silently\n
                // clear a catalogue or prior approval requirement here.\n
                $locked->requires_approval = true;\n
            }\n
            if ($locked->priority !== $before['priority']) {\n
                $locked->stampSlaDueDates();\n
            }\n
            if ($locked->isDirty()) {\n
                $locked->save();\n
            }\n
\n
            if ($releaseRouting && $locked->routing_override !== null) {\n
                $locked->routing_override = null;\n
                $locked->save();\n
                ItTicketEvent::record($locked, 'routing_override_released', $actor->id, [\n
                    'reason' => trim($data['routing_reason']), 'via' => $source,\n
                ]);\n
            } elseif ($manualOwnership !== []) {\n
                $locked->routing_override = $this->routing->manualOverride($locked, $actor, [\n
                    ...$manualOwnership, 'routing_reason' => $data['routing_reason'] ?? null,\n
                ]);\n
                if ($locked->isDirty('routing_override')) {\n
                    $locked->save();\n
                    ItTicketEvent::record($locked, 'routing_override_applied', $actor->id, [\n
                        'fields' => array_keys($manualOwnership), 'reason' => trim($data['routing_reason']), 'via' => $source,\n
                    ]);\n
                }\n
            }\n
\n
            if ($releaseRouting || $manualOwnership !== []\n
                || collect(['work_type', 'it_service_id', 'category', 'site_id', 'is_organisation_wide', 'priority'])\n
                    ->contains(fn (string $field): bool => array_key_exists($field, $properties))) {\n
                $locked = $this->routing->route($locked, $actor->id);\n
            }\n
            $locked->refresh();\n
\n
            $changedFields = collect(self::PROPERTY_FIELDS)\n
                ->filter(fn (string $field): bool => $before[$field] !== $locked->getAttribute($field))\n
                ->values()\n
                ->all();\n
            $waitingContextChanged = $waitingBefore !== $locked->only([\n
                'waiting_party',\n
                'waiting_reason',\n
                'next_action',\n
            ]);\n
\n
            if ($changedFields === [] && ! $waitingContextChanged) {\n
                return ['ticket' => $locked, 'changed' => false];\n
            }\n
\n
            if ($changedFields !== []) {\n
                $this->recordPropertyEvents($locked, $actor, $before, $changedFields, $source);\n
                AuditLogger::logOrFail('it.ticket.triage.updated', $locked, [\n
                    'actor_id' => $actor->id,\n
                    'changed_fields' => $changedFields,\n
                    'source' => $source,\n
                    'application_scope' => 'single_application',\n
                ]);\n
            }\n
\n
            if (in_array('assigned_to_user_id', $changedFields, true)\n
                && $locked->assignee\n
                && (int) $locked->assigned_to_user_id !== (int) $actor->id) {\n
                $this->emailDeliveries->send($locked->assignee, new TicketAssignedNotification($locked));\n
            }\n
\n
            return ['ticket' => $locked, 'changed' => true];\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    private function lockTicket(ItTicket $ticket): ItTicket\n
    {\n
        return ItTicket::query()->lockForUpdate()->findOrFail($ticket->getKey());\n
    }\n
\n
    private function guardActor(ItTicket $ticket, User $actor, bool $lockForUpdate = false): void\n
    {\n
        if (! $actor->canDo('it.manage')) {\n
            throw new AuthorizationException('You are not allowed to manage ticket triage.');\n
        }\n
        if (! $this->workAccess->canWork($actor, $ticket, $lockForUpdate)) {\n
            throw (new ModelNotFoundException)->setModel(ItTicket::class, [$ticket->id]);\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $data @return array{0: int|null, 1: bool} */\n
    private function prospectiveScope(ItTicket $ticket, array &$data, User $actor): array\n
    {\n
        $siteWasSupplied = array_key_exists('site_id', $data);\n
        $wideWasSupplied = array_key_exists('is_organisation_wide', $data);\n
        $siteId = $siteWasSupplied\n
            ? ($data['site_id'] !== null ? (int) $data['site_id'] : null)\n
            : ($ticket->site_id !== null ? (int) $ticket->site_id : null);\n
        $isApplicationWide = $wideWasSupplied\n
            ? (bool) $data['is_organisation_wide']\n
            : (bool) $ticket->is_organisation_wide;\n
\n
        if (($data['is_organisation_wide'] ?? false) && (! $siteWasSupplied || $siteId !== null)) {\n
            throw ValidationException::withMessages([\n
                'site_id' => 'Application-wide tickets cannot also have a Site.',\n
            ]);\n
        }\n
        if ($siteWasSupplied && $siteId !== null && ! $wideWasSupplied) {\n
            $isApplicationWide = false;\n
            $data['is_organisation_wide'] = false;\n
        }\n
        if (($siteWasSupplied || $wideWasSupplied)\n
            && ! $this->workAccess->canAssignScope($actor, $siteId, $isApplicationWide)) {\n
            throw new AuthorizationException('You cannot assign this ticket to that operational scope.');\n
        }\n
\n
        return [$siteId, $isApplicationWide];\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $data */\n
    private function guardAssignee(array $data, ?int $siteId, bool $isApplicationWide): void\n
    {\n
        if (! array_key_exists('assigned_to_user_id', $data) || $data['assigned_to_user_id'] === null) {\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        $assignee = User::query()->whereNotNull('approved_at')->find((int) $data['assigned_to_user_id']);\n
        $eligible = $assignee\n
            && ItStaffDirectory::agents()->contains('id', $assignee->id)\n
            && ($isApplicationWide\n
                ? $siteId === null && $assignee->canDo('it.organisationWide')\n
                : $siteId !== null && in_array($siteId, $this->workAccess->approvedSiteIds($assignee), true));\n
\n
        if (! $eligible) {\n
            throw new AuthorizationException('Choose a current IT technician for this ticket scope.');\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $data */\n
    private function releaseIneligibleAssigneeAfterScopeChange(\n
        ItTicket $ticket,\n
        array &$data,\n
        ?int $siteId,\n
        bool $isApplicationWide,\n
    ): void {\n
        $scopeChanged = array_key_exists('site_id', $data)\n
            || array_key_exists('is_organisation_wide', $data);\n
        if (! $scopeChanged\n
            || array_key_exists('assigned_to_user_id', $data)\n
            || $ticket->assigned_to_user_id === null) {\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        $assignee = User::query()\n
            ->whereNotNull('approved_at')\n
            ->find((int) $ticket->assigned_to_user_id);\n
        $eligible = $assignee\n
            && ItStaffDirectory::agents()->contains('id', $assignee->id)\n
            && ($isApplicationWide\n
                ? $siteId === null && $assignee->canDo('it.organisationWide')\n
                : $siteId !== null && in_array($siteId, $this->workAccess->approvedSiteIds($assignee), true));\n
\n
        if (! $eligible) {\n
            // Site scope is authoritative. Routing may now choose a suitable\n
            // default assignee for the new scope; it must never retain an\n
            // assignee who can no longer work the ticket.\n
            $data['assigned_to_user_id'] = null;\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $data */\n
    private function guardAsset(\n
        ItTicket $ticket,\n
        array $data,\n
        ?int $siteId,\n
        bool $isApplicationWide,\n
    ): void {\n
        $assetWasSupplied = array_key_exists('asset_id', $data);\n
        if ($assetWasSupplied && $data['asset_id'] === null) {\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        $scopeChanged = array_key_exists('site_id', $data)\n
            || array_key_exists('is_organisation_wide', $data);\n
        $assetId = $assetWasSupplied\n
            ? (int) $data['asset_id']\n
            : ($scopeChanged && $ticket->asset_id !== null ? (int) $ticket->asset_id : null);\n
        if ($assetId === null) {\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        $asset = Asset::query()->whereKey($assetId)->where('status', 'active')->first();\n
        $eligible = $asset && ($isApplicationWide\n
            ? $siteId === null\n
            : $siteId !== null && (int) $asset->site_id === $siteId);\n
        if (! $eligible) {\n
            if (! $assetWasSupplied) {\n
                throw new DomainException('Remove or change the linked Asset before changing the ticket Site.');\n
            }\n
\n
            throw new AuthorizationException('Choose an active Asset within the ticket scope.');\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $data */\n
    private function guardService(array $data): void\n
    {\n
        if (! array_key_exists('it_service_id', $data) || $data['it_service_id'] === null) {\n
            return;\n
        }\n
\n
        if (! ItService::query()\n
            ->whereKey((int) $data['it_service_id'])\n
            ->where('is_active', true)\n
            ->lockForUpdate()\n
            ->exists()) {\n
            throw new DomainException('The selected service is not available.');\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    private function workingState(string $status): ItWorkflowState\n
    {\n
        return match ($status) {\n
            'open' => ItWorkflowState::Submitted,\n
            'in_progress' => ItWorkflowState::InProgress,\n
            'waiting' => ItWorkflowState::Waiting,\n
            default => throw new DomainException('Use the governed resolve or close journey to settle a ticket.'),\n
        };\n
    }\n
\n
    /** @param array[39m[31m<string, mixed>[39m[31m $before @param list[39m[31m<string>[39m[31m $changedFields */\n
    private function recordPropertyEvents(\n
        ItTicket $ticket,\n
        User $actor,\n
        array $before,\n
        array $changedFields,\n
        string $source,\n
    ): void {\n
        if (in_array('priority_decision', $changedFields, true)) {\n
            ItTicketEvent::record($ticket, 'priority_assessed', $actor->id, [\n
                'impact' => $ticket->impact, 'urgency' => $ticket->urgency,\n
                'priority' => $ticket->priority, 'decision' => $ticket->priority_decision,\n
                'via' => $source,\n
            ]);\n
        }\n
        if (in_array('priority', $changedFields, true)) {\n
            ItTicketEvent::record($ticket, 'priority_changed', $actor->id, [\n
                'from' => $before['priority'],\n
                'to' => $ticket->priority,\n
                'via' => $source,\n
            ]);\n
        }\n
        if (in_array('assigned_to_user_id', $changedFields, true)) {\n
            ItTicketEvent::record($ticket, 'assigned', $actor->id, [\n
                'from' => $before['assigned_to_user_id'],\n
                'to' => $ticket->assigned_to_user_id,\n
                'via' => $source,\n
            ]);\n
        }\n
\n
        $propertyFields = array_values(array_diff(\n
            $changedFields,\n
            ['status', 'priority', 'assigned_to_user_id'],\n
        ));\n
        if ($propertyFields !== []) {\n
            ItTicketEvent::record($ticket, 'properties_updated', $actor->id, [\n
                'fields' => $propertyFields,\n
                'via' => $source,\n
            ]);\n
        }\n
    }\n
}\n

                                                                        .completed\n
                                                                }{' '}\n
                                                                of{' '}\n
                                                                {\n
                                                                    workflow\n
                                                                        .progress\n
                                                                        .total\n
                                                                }{' '}\n
                                                                complete\n
                                                            </span>\n
                                                            <span>\n
                                                                {formatDateTime(\n
                                                                    workflow.effective_at,\n
                                                                )}\n
                                                            </span>\n
                                                        </div>\n
                                                        {workflow.progress\n
                                                            .failed > 0 ? (\n
                                                            <p className="mt-2 text-[11px] font-semibold text-[color:var(--status-critical)]">\n
                                                                {\n
                                                                    workflow\n
                                                                        .progress\n
                                                                        .failed\n
                                                                }{' '}\n
                                                                step\n
                                                                {workflow\n
                                                                    .progress\n
                                                                    .failed ===\n
                                                                1\n
                                                                    ? ''\n
                                                                    : 's'}{' '}\n
                                                                need recovery\n
                                                            </p>\n
                                                        ) : null}\n
                                                    </article>\n
                                                );\n
                                            },\n
                                        )}\n
                                    </div>\n
                                ) : (\n
                                    <div className="mt-4 rounded-xl border border-dashed border-border px-4 py-5 text-center text-xs text-muted-foreground">\n
                                        No lifecycle workflows yet. Create\n
                                        templates in Setup; matching HR\n
                                        onboarding, role/site changes, and\n
                                        offboarding events will appear\n
                                        automatically.\n
                                    </div>\n
                                )}\n
                                <p className="mt-3 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">\n
                                    Asset custody remains in Assets, device\n
                                    assignments in Security & Devices, and\n
                                    employee identity in HR. This queue\n
                                    coordinates those canonical records without\n
                                    copying them.\n
                                </p>\n
                            </section>\n
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">\n
                                <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">\n
                                    <Button asChild size="sm" variant="outline">\n
                                        <a\n
                                            href={provisioningExportUrl()}\n
                                            aria-label="Export the provisioning queue as CSV"\n
                                        >\n
                                            <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />{' '}\n
                                            Export CSV\n
                                        </a>\n
                                    </Button>\n
                                    {can.manage ? (\n
                                        <Button\n
                                            size="sm"\n
                                            variant="outline"\n
                                            onClick={() =>\n
                                                setModal({\n
                                                    type: 'new-request',\n
                                                })\n
                                            }\n
                                        >\n
                                            <Plus className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> New\n
                                            request\n
                                        </Button>\n
                                    ) : null}\n
                                </div>\n
                            </div>\n
\n
                            {/* Bulk action bar — appears when requests are selected */}\n
                            {can.manage && reqSel.selected.size > 0 ? (\n
                                <div className="sticky top-2 z-20 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-primary/40 bg-primary/10 px-3 py-2 shadow-sm">\n
                                    <span className="text-[12.5px] font-semibold text-foreground">\n
                                        {reqSel.selected.size} selected\n
                                    </span>\n
                                    <span\n
                                        className="mx-1 h-5 w-px bg-border"\n
                                        aria-hidden\n
                                    />\n
                                    <Select\n
                                        disabled={bulkBusy || bulkBlocked}\n
                                        value=""\n
                                        onValueChange={(v) =>\n
                                            runProvisioningBulk({\n
                                                action: 'assign',\n
                                                assigned_to_user_id: Number(v),\n
                                            })\n
                                        }\n
                                    >\n
                                        <SelectTrigger\n
                                            className="h-8 w-[160px]"\n

                                        <SelectContent>\n

                                                >\n
                                                    {a.name}\n
                                                </SelectItem>\n
                                            ))}\n

                                        size="sm"\n

                                        disabled={bulkBusy}\n

                                        }\n

                                        <CheckCircle2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />{' '}\n
                                        Fulfil\n
                                    </Button>\n
                                    <Button\n
                                        size="sm"\n
                                        variant="ghost"\n
                                        className="ml-auto"\n
                                        onClick={() => reqSel.clear()}\n
                                    >\n
                                        Clear\n
                                    </Button>\n
                                </div>\n

\n

                                                          ? 'indeterminate'\n
                                                          : false\n
                                                }\n

                                                    reqSel.toggleAll(\n
                                                        checked === true,\n
                                                    )\n
                                                }\n
                                            />\n
                                            Select this page\n
                                        </label>\n
                                    ) : undefined\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            <ItProvisioningList\n
                                rows={requests?.data ?? []}\n

                                busy={bulkBusy}\n
                                today={todayISO()}\n

                                    <EmptyState\n
                                        icon={Inbox}\n
                                        title="No provisioning steps match"\n
                                        blurb="Change the filters to see other permitted work. Matching HR events and manual requests create steps here."\n
                                    />\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            {requests ? (\n
                                <LaravelPagination\n
                                    links={requests.links}\n
                                    lastPage={requests.last_page}\n
                                />\n
                            ) : null}\n
                        \n
                    )}\n
\n
                    {/* ── Ticket queue (agents) ── */}\n
                    {can.view && tab === 'tickets' && (\n
                        <>\n
                            {/* Bulk action bar — appears when rows are selected */}\n
                            {can.manage && ticketSel.selected.size > 0 ? (\n
                                <div className="sticky top-2 z-20 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 rounded-xl border border-primary/40 bg-primary/10 px-3 py-2 shadow-sm">\n

                                    </span>\n
                                    <span\n

                                    />\n
                                    <Select\n
                                        disabled={bulkBusy || bulkBlocked}\n
                                        value=""\n

                                            runBulk({\n
                                                action: 'assign',\n

                                                    key={a.id}\n

                                                </SelectItem>\n
                                            ))}\n
                                        </SelectContent>\n
                                    </Select>\n
                                    <Select\n
                                        disabled={bulkBusy || bulkBlocked}\n

                                            ))}\n
                                        </SelectContent>\n
                                    </Select>\n
                                    <Select\n
                                        disabled={bulkBusy || bulkBlocked}\n
                                        value=""\n

                                            }\n
                                            runBulk({\n
                                                action: 'status',\n
                                                status: v,\n
                                            });\n
                                        }}\n
                                    >\n
                                        <SelectTrigger\n
                                            className="h-8 w-[150px]"\n
                                            aria-label="Set status for selected"\n
                                        >\n
                                            <SelectValue placeholder="Set status…" />\n
                                        </SelectTrigger>\n

                                                </SelectItem>\n

                                    </Select>\n
                                    <Button\n
                                        size="sm"\n
                                        variant="outline"\n
                                        className="min-h-11"\n
                                        disabled={bulkBusy}\n
                                        onClick={() =>\n
                                            setCloseSelectedTickets(true)\n
                                        }\n
                                    >\n
                                        <XCircle className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />{' '}\n
                                        Close\n
                                    </Button>\n
                                    <Button\n
                                        size="sm"\n
                                        variant="ghost"\n
                                        className="ml-auto"\n
                                        onClick={() => ticketSel.clear()}\n
                                    >\n
                                        Clear\n
                                    </Button>\n

                            ) : null}\n
\n
                            <ListCaption\n
                                title="Tickets"\n
                                caption={`${tickets?.data.length ?? 0} of ${tickets?.total ?? 0} shown · filtered results`}\n
                                right={\n
                                    can.manage &&\n
                                    manageableTickets.length > 0 ? (\n
                                        <label className="flex min-h-11 items-center gap-2 text-sm">\n
                                            <Checkbox\n
                                                aria-label="Select all manageable tickets on this page"\n
                                                disabled={bulkBusy}\n
                                                checked={\n
                                                    ticketSel.allOnPage\n
                                                        ? true\n
                                                        : ticketSel.someOnPage\n
                                                          ? 'indeterminate'\n
                                                          : false\n

                                                    )\n

                                            />\n
                                            Select this page\n
                                        </label>\n
                                    ) : undefined\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            <ItTicketList\n
                                conversationReady={conversation_ready === true}\n
                                rows={tickets?.data ?? []}\n
                                view={listView}\n
                                actionsFor={ticketActions}\n
                                onPeek={(ticket) => openTicket(ticket.id)}\n
                                canSelect={canManageTicket}\n
                                selected={ticketSel.selected}\n
                                onSelect={(ticket, checked) =>\n
                                    ticketSel.toggle(ticket.id, checked)\n
                                }\n
                                busy={bulkBusy}\n
                                emptyState={\n
                                    ticketFiltersActive ? (\n
                                        <EmptyState\n
                                            icon={Ticket}\n

                                            blurb="Widen or clear the filters to see more of the queue."\n
                                            action={{\n
                                                label: 'Clear filters',\n
                                                onClick: clearTicketFilters,\n
                                            }}\n
                                        />\n
                                    ) : (\n
                                        <EmptyState\n
                                            icon={Ticket}\n
                                            title="No tickets"\n
                                            blurb={\n
                                                can.manage\n
                                                    ? 'Log a helpdesk ticket with the button above.'\n
                                                    : 'There are no tickets in your permitted queue.'\n
                                            }\n
                                        />\n
                                    )\n
                                }\n
                            />\n
                            {tickets ? (\n
                                <LaravelPagination\n
                                    links={tickets.links}\n
                                    lastPage={tickets.last_page}\n
                                />\n
                            ) : null}\n
                        \n
                    )}\n
\n
                    {/* ── Service catalogue (everyone with it.request) ── */}\n
                    {can.request && tab === 'catalog' ? (\n
                        <ItServiceCatalogue\n

                            category={\n
                                catalogCategory === ALL ? null : catalogCategory\n

                        />\n
                    ) : null}\n
\n
                    {/* ── My tickets (everyone with it.request) ── */}\n
                    {can.request && tab === 'my-tickets' && (\n
                        <>\n
                            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">\n
                                <p className="text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground">\n
                                    Requests you raised or that were raised for\n
                                    you.\n
                                </p>\n
                            </div>\n
\n
                            {/* CSAT prompt (§K) — a nudge to rate freshly resolved tickets;\n
                            it empties as each is rated (confetti on a perfect five). */}\n
                            {myTickets.some(\n
                                (t) => t.can_rate && t.csat_score == null,\n
                            ) ? (\n
                                <div className="rounded-2xl border border-primary/20 bg-primary/5 px-4.5 py-4">\n
                                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">\n
                                        <span className="grid h-8 w-8 flex-none place-items-center rounded-lg bg-primary/10 text-primary">\n
                                            <Star className="h-4 w-4" />\n
                                        </span>\n
                                        <div className="min-w-0">\n
                                            <h3 className="text-[14px] leading-tight font-bold">\n
                                                How did IT do?\n

                                                Rate your resolved tickets — it\n

                                                improve.\n
                                            </p>\n
                                        </div>\n
                                    </div>\n
                                    <div className="mt-3 flex flex-col gap-2.5">\n
                                        {myTickets\n
                                            .filter(\n
                                                (t) =>\n
                                                    t.can_rate &&\n
                                                    t.csat_score == null,\n

                                                <div\n
                                                    key={t.id}\n

                                                        <span className="text-[13px] font-semibold">\n
                                                            {t.title}\n
                                                        </span>\n

                                                            {t.reference ?? ''}\n
                                                            {t.resolved\n
                                                                ? ` · resolved ${t.resolved}`\n
                                                                : ''}\n
                                                        </span>\n
                                                    </div>\n
                                                    <div className="mt-2">\n
                                                        <CsatRater\n
                                                            ticketId={t.id}\n
                                                            expectedVersion={\n
                                                                t.lock_version\n
                                                            }\n
                                                        />\n
                                                    </div>\n
                                                </div>\n
                                            ))}\n
                                    </div>\n
                                </div>\n
                            ) : null}\n
\n
                            <ListCaption\n
                                title="My requests"\n
                                caption={`${filteredMyTickets.length} of ${myTickets.length} shown`}\n

                                            myQuery || myStatus !== ALL\n

                    )}\n
\n
                    {/* ── Knowledge base (agents) ── */}\n
                    {showKnowledgeCatalogue && tab === 'knowledge' && (\n
                        <>\n

                                </p>\n
                                {canAuthorKnowledge ? (\n
                                    <Button\n

                                        <BookOpen className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> New\n
                                        KB article\n

                            </div>\n
\n
                            <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">\n
                                <div className="grid min-w-[920px] grid-cols-[2.5fr_1.15fr_1.6fr_1.1fr_1.1fr_44px] gap-3 border-b border-border bg-muted px-4.5 py-2.5 text-[10.5px] font-bold tracking-wide text-muted-foreground uppercase">\n
                                    <span>Title</span>\n
                                    <span>Lifecycle</span>\n

                                    <span>Impact</span>\n
                                    <span>Review</span>\n

                                </div>\n
                                {agentKb.map((a) => (\n

                                                    'in_review',\n
                                                    'published',\n

                                                </button>\n

                                            canAuthorKnowledge\n

                                        }\n
                                        action={\n
                                            canAuthorKnowledge\n
                                                ? {\n
                                                      label: 'New KB article',\n
                                                      onClick: () =>\n
                                                          setModal({\n
                                                              type: 'kb',\n
                                                          }),\n
                                                  }\n
                                                : undefined\n
                                        }\n
                                    />\n
                                ) : null}\n

                        \n
                    )}\n
\n
                    {/* ── Knowledge browse (requesters) ── */}\n
                    {!showKnowledgeCatalogue &&\n
                        can.request &&\n
                        tab === 'knowledge' && (\n
                            <>\n
                                {filteredKb.length === 0 ? (\n
                                    <div className="overflow-hidden rounded-2xl border border-border bg-card">\n
                                        <EmptyState\n
                                            icon={BookOpen}\n
                                            title={\n
                                                kbPublished.length === 0\n
                                                    ? 'No articles yet'\n
                                                    : 'No matches'\n
                                            }\n
                                            blurb={\n
                                                kbPublished.length === 0\n
                                                    ? 'IT will publish fixes here — check back, or raise a ticket and they’ll sort it.'\n
                                                    : 'Nothing matches your search. Try a different word or category.'\n
                                            }\n
                                        />\n
                                    </div>\n
                                ) : (\n
                                    <div className="grid gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">\n
                                        {filteredKb.map((a) => (\n
                                            <button\n
                                                key={a.id}\n
                                                type="button"\n

    );\n

/** Per-page row selection for the bulk-action queues (tickets & provisioning).\n
 *  The selection is per-view: it clears whenever the visible page changes\n
 *  (filter, sort, page, or a bulk action that reshuffles rows). */\n
function useRowSelection(\n
    pageIds: number[],\n
    pageVersions: Record<number, number> = {},\n
) {\n
    const [selected, setSelected] = useState<Set<number>>(new Set());\n
    const [versions, setVersions] = useState<Record<number, number>>({});\n
    const key = pageIds.join(',');\n
    useEffect(() => {\n
        setSelected(new Set());\n
        setVersions({});\n
    }, [key]);\n
\n
    const toggle = (id: number, on: boolean) => {\n
        if (!pageIds.includes(id)) return;\n
        setVersions((previous) => {\n
            const next = { ...previous };\n
            if (on && pageVersions[id] !== undefined)\n
                next[id] = previous[id] ?? pageVersions[id];\n
            else if (!on) delete next[id];\n
            return next;\n
        });\n
        setSelected((prev) => {\n
            const next = new Set(prev);\n
            if (on && next.size < 50) next.add(id);\n
            else next.delete(id);\n
            return next;\n
        });\n
    };\n
\n
    const toggleAll = (on: boolean) => {\n
        setVersions((previous) =>\n
            on\n
                ? Object.fromEntries(\n
                      pageIds\n
                          .filter((id) => pageVersions[id] !== undefined)\n
                          .map((id) => [id, previous[id] ?? pageVersions[id]]),\n
                  )\n
                : {},\n
        );\n
        setSelected((prev) => {\n
            const next = new Set(prev);\n
            pageIds.forEach((id) => {\n
                if (on && next.size < 50) next.add(id);\n
                else if (!on) next.delete(id);\n
            });\n
            return next;\n
        });\n
    };\n
\n
    return {\n
        selected: new Set([...selected].filter((id) => pageIds.includes(id))),\n
        versions: Object.fromEntries(\n
            Object.entries(versions).filter(([id]) =>\n
                pageIds.includes(Number(id)),\n
            ),\n
        ),\n
        clear: () => {\n
            setSelected(new Set());\n
            setVersions({});\n
        },\n
        remove: (ids: number[]) => {\n
            setSelected(\n
                (previous) =>\n
                    new Set([...previous].filter((id) => !ids.includes(id))),\n
            );\n
            setVersions((previous) =>\n
                Object.fromEntries(\n
                    Object.entries(previous).filter(\n
                        ([id]) => !ids.includes(Number(id)),\n
                    ),\n
                ),\n
            );\n
        },\n
        toggle,\n
        toggleAll,\n
        allOnPage:\n
            pageIds.length > 0 && pageIds.every((id) => selected.has(id)),\n
        someOnPage: pageIds.some((id) => selected.has(id)),\n
    };\n
}\n
\n
/** Created-date range — two native pickers feeding the `from`/`to` params. */\n
function DateRange({\n
    from,\n
    to,\n
    onChange,\n
}: {\n
    from: string;\n
    to: string;\n
    onChange: (key: 'from' | 'to', value: string) => void;\n
}) {\n
    const base =\n
        'h-8 rounded-md border border-border bg-card px-2 text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground outline-none focus-visible:ring-2 focus-visible:ring-ring';\n
    return (\n
        <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">\n
            <input\n
                type="date"\n
                value={from}\n
                max={to || undefined}\n
                onChange={(e) => onChange('from', e.target.value)}\n
                aria-label="Raised from"\n
                className={base}\n
            />\n
            <span className="text-[12px] text-muted-foreground">→</span>\n
            <input\n
                type="date"\n
                value={to}\n
                min={from || undefined}\n
                onChange={(e) => onChange('to', e.target.value)}\n
                aria-label="Raised to"\n
                className={base}\n
            />\n
        </div>\n
    );\n
}\n
function EmptyState({\n
    icon: Icon,\n
    title,\n
    blurb,\n
    action,\n
}: {\n
    icon: typeof Inbox;\n
    title: string;\n
    blurb: string;\n
    action?: { label: string; onClick: () => void };\n
}) {\n
    return (\n
        <div className="flex flex-col items-center gap-2 px-6 py-14 text-center">\n
            <span className="grid h-12 w-12 place-items-center rounded-2xl bg-muted text-muted-foreground">\n
                <Icon className="h-6 w-6" />\n
            </span>\n
            <div className="text-[14px] font-bold">{title}</div>\n
            <p className="max-w-sm text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">\n
                {blurb}\n
            </p>\n
            {action ? (\n
                <Button\n
                    size="sm"\n
                    variant="outline"\n
                    className="mt-1"\n
                    onClick={action.onClick}\n
                >\n
                    {action.label}\n
                </Button>\n
            ) : null}\n
        </div>\n
    );\n
}\n

Select the credential and copy it before clearing this pane.', ); } }; const title = operation === 'update' ? 'API identity grants saved' : operation === 'revoke' ? 'API identity revoked' : operation === 'rotate' ? 'API credential rotated' : 'API identity issued'; return ( <WizardSuccessPane title={title} blurb={ credential ? ( <div className="space-y-3 text-left"> <p> This credential is shown only in this confirmed response. Store it in the approved secret manager before clearing it. </p> <Input aria-label="One-time API credential" value={credential.token} readOnly onFocus={(event) => event.currentTarget.select()} className="font-mono text-xs" /> {copyError ? ( <p role="alert" className="text-status-critical"> {copyError} </p> ) : null} </div> ) : unavailable ? ( 'The command was confirmed, but its one-time credential is unavailable here.
